+import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
+import {
+  IWorkflowRepository,
+  WorkflowRunDTO,
+  WorkflowStepDTO,
+  CreateWorkflowRunData,
+  UpdateWorkflowRunData,
+  CreateWorkflowStepData,
+  UpdateWorkflowStepData,
+} from '../../domain/repositories';
+import { PrismaService } from '../../prisma/prisma.service';
+
+/**
+ * Prisma implementation of IWorkflowRepository
+ * Infrastructure layer - depends on domain interfaces
+ */
+@Injectable()
+export class PrismaWorkflowRepository implements IWorkflowRepository {
+  constructor(private readonly prisma: PrismaService) {}
+
+  // WorkflowRun operations
+  async findRunById(runId: string): Promise<WorkflowRunDTO | null> {
+    const run = await this.prisma.workflowRun.findUnique({ where: { id: runId } });
+    if (!run) return null;
+    return this.mapRunToDTO(run);
+  }
+
+  async findRunsByProjectId(
+    projectId: string,
+    options?: { limit?: number }
+  ): Promise<WorkflowRunDTO[]> {
+    const runs = await this.prisma.workflowRun.findMany({
+      where: { projectId },
+      orderBy: { startedAt: 'desc' },
+      take: options?.limit,
+    });
+    return runs.map((r) => this.mapRunToDTO(r));
+  }
+
+  async createRun(data: CreateWorkflowRunData): Promise<WorkflowRunDTO> {
+    const run = await this.prisma.workflowRun.create({
+      data: {
+        projectId: data.projectId,
+        workflowDefId: data.workflowDefId,
+        status: data.status || 'running',
+        currentNodeId: data.currentNodeId,
+        context: data.context,
+      },
+    });
+    return this.mapRunToDTO(run);
+  }
+
+  async updateRun(runId: string, data: UpdateWorkflowRunData): Promise<WorkflowRunDTO> {
+    const run = await this.prisma.workflowRun.update({
+      where: { id: runId },
+      data,
+    });
+    return this.mapRunToDTO(run);
+  }
+
+  async deleteRun(runId: string): Promise<void> {
+    // Delete related steps first
+    await this.prisma.workflowStep.deleteMany({ where: { workflowRunId: runId } });
+    await this.prisma.approval.deleteMany({ where: { workflowRunId: runId } });
+    await this.prisma.workflowRun.delete({ where: { id: runId } });
+  }
+
+  // WorkflowStep operations
+  async findStepById(stepId: string): Promise<WorkflowStepDTO | null> {
+    const step = await this.prisma.workflowStep.findUnique({ where: { id: stepId } });
+    if (!step) return null;
+    return this.mapStepToDTO(step);
+  }
+
+  async findStepsByRunId(runId: string): Promise<WorkflowStepDTO[]> {
+    const steps = await this.prisma.workflowStep.findMany({
+      where: { workflowRunId: runId },
+      orderBy: { startedAt: 'asc' },
+    });
+    return steps.map((s) => this.mapStepToDTO(s));
+  }
+
+  async createStep(data: CreateWorkflowStepData): Promise<WorkflowStepDTO> {
+    const step = await this.prisma.workflowStep.create({
+      data: {
+        workflowRunId: data.workflowRunId,
+        taskId: data.taskId,
+        nodeId: data.nodeId,
+        agentRole: data.agentRole,
+        status: data.status || 'pending',
+        input: data.input,
+      },
+    });
+    return this.mapStepToDTO(step);
+  }
+
+  async updateStep(stepId: string, data: UpdateWorkflowStepData): Promise<WorkflowStepDTO> {
+    const step = await this.prisma.workflowStep.update({
+      where: { id: stepId },
+      data,
+    });
+    return this.mapStepToDTO(step);
+  }
+
+  async deleteStepsByRunId(runId: string): Promise<void> {
+    await this.prisma.workflowStep.deleteMany({ where: { workflowRunId: runId } });
+  }
+
+  private mapRunToDTO(run: any): WorkflowRunDTO {
+    return {
+      id: run.id,
+      projectId: run.projectId,
+      workflowDefId: run.workflowDefId,
+      status: run.status,
+      currentNodeId: run.currentNodeId,
+      context: run.context,
+      startedAt: run.startedAt,
+      completedAt: run.completedAt,
+    };
+  }
+
+  private mapStepToDTO(step: any): WorkflowStepDTO {
+    return {
+      id: step.id,
+      workflowRunId: step.workflowRunId,
+      taskId: step.taskId,
+      nodeId: step.nodeId,
+      agentRole: step.agentRole,
+      status: step.status,
+      input: step.input,
+      output: step.output,
+      error: step.error,
+      retryCount: step.retryCount,
+      startedAt: step.startedAt,
+      completedAt: step.completedAt,
+      durationMs: step.durationMs,
+    };
+  }
+}

+          agentContext.projectId || projectId,

+        ));

+      }

+      
+      // Reset to IDLE so agent can be reused
+      this.stateMachine.transitionTo(AgentState.IDLE, 'Agent reset after error');
+      this.emitStateChanged(agentId, AgentState.ERROR, AgentState.IDLE, projectId);
+      
+      // Re-throw the error so callers know it failed
+      throw error;
+    }

+   * Emit a state change event via AgentEventEmitter and WebSocket
+   * 
+   * @param agentId - Agent identifier
+   * @param previousState - State before transition
+   * @param currentState - State after transition

+    previousState: AgentState,

+    projectId: string,

+  ) {
+    const event: StateChangedEvent = {
+      agentId,
+      agentRole: this.role,
+      previousState,
+      currentState,
+      timestamp: new Date(),
+      reason
+    };
+
+    // Emit via AgentEventEmitter (for observers)

+
+    // Emit via WebSocket for real-time UI updates
+    this.websocketGateway.sendToProject(projectId, {
+      type: 'agent:state_changed',
+      payload: event
+    });
+
+    // Also broadcast to all clients for VirtualOffice
+    this.websocketGateway.broadcastEvent({
+      type: 'agent:state_changed',
+      payload: event
+    });
+
+    // Log state transition for debugging
+    this.logger.debug(`Agent ${this.role} state: ${previousState} ΓåÆ ${currentState}${reason ? ` (${reason})` : ''}`);
+  }

+import { Injectable, Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
+import { IActionStrategy } from '../../../domain/agent/interfaces/IActionStrategy.interface';
+import { AgentContext, AgentThought, AgentActionResult } from '../../../domain/common/types';
+import { MemoryService } from '../../memory/memory.service';
+import { LLMProviderFactory } from '../../llm/llm-provider.factory';
+import { AppWebSocketGateway } from '../../websocket/websocket.gateway';
+import { AgentRole, ModelRouteConfig } from '@ai-corp/shared-types';
+
+@Injectable()
+export class ArchitectActionStrategy implements IActionStrategy {
+  private readonly logger = new Logger(ArchitectActionStrategy.name);
+
+  constructor(
+    private readonly memoryService: MemoryService,
+    private readonly llmFactory: LLMProviderFactory,
+    private readonly websocketGateway: AppWebSocketGateway
+  ) {}
+
+  async act(context: AgentContext, thought: AgentThought): Promise<AgentActionResult> {
+    const memories = thought.memories || [];
+    const memoryContext = memories.length > 0
+      ? `\n\nRelevant architecture patterns:\n${memories.join('\n')}`
+      : '';
+
+    const goal = context.goal || context.projectContext?.goal || '';
+    const description = context.projectContext?.description || '';
+    const requirements = context.requirements || '';
+
+    const systemPrompt = `You are a Software Architect. Design a comprehensive system architecture.`;
+
+    const prompt = `You are a Software Architect. Design a comprehensive system architecture.
+
+PROJECT GOAL: ${goal}
+PROJECT DESCRIPTION: ${description}
+${requirements ? `REQUIREMENTS:\n${requirements}\n` : ''}
+${memoryContext}
+
+Create a detailed architecture design document:
+
+1. SYSTEM ARCHITECTURE
+   - Choose architecture style (Layered, Microservices, Event-Driven, etc.)
+   - Justify the choice based on requirements
+   - Describe major system components and their responsibilities
+
+2. DESIGN PATTERNS
+   - Identify applicable GoF patterns (Strategy, Factory, Observer, Repository, etc.)
+   - Explain where and why each pattern will be used
+   - Consider SOLID principles in the design
+
+3. TECHNOLOGY STACK
+   - Backend framework and language
+   - Database (SQL vs NoSQL, justify choice)
+   - Frontend framework
+   - Infrastructure (cloud, containerization)
+
+4. DATA FLOW & COMPONENT DIAGRAM (text-based)
+   - Describe how data flows through the system
+   - Show key components and their interactions
+
+5. SCALABILITY & PERFORMANCE CONSIDERATIONS
+   - Caching strategy
+   - Load balancing
+   - Database optimization
+
+Format with clear headings and bullet points.`;
+
+    // Fetch latest model config
+    const modelConfig: ModelRouteConfig = { provider: 'opencode', model: 'openrouter/nvidia/nemotron-3-super-120b-a12b:free', fallbackProvider: 'opencode' };
+    const actualModelConfig = await this.memoryService.getModelConfigForAgent(AgentRole.ARCHITECT, modelConfig);
+    const provider = this.llmFactory.getProvider(actualModelConfig);
+
+    const result = await provider.chat({
+      messages: [
+        { role: 'system', content: systemPrompt },
+        { role: 'user', content: prompt }
+      ],
+      model: actualModelConfig.model,
+      maxTokens: 2000
+    });
+
+    // Save memory
+    const agentId = await this.memoryService.getAgentIdByRole(AgentRole.ARCHITECT);
+    if (agentId) {
+      await this.memoryService.saveMemory(
+        agentId,
+        AgentRole.ARCHITECT,
+        'architecture_patterns',
+        `Designed architecture for: ${goal}`,
+        { importance: 9, projectId: context.projectId }
+      );
+    }
+
+    // Emit action event
+    this.websocketGateway.sendToProject(context.projectId || 'unknown', {
+      type: 'agent:action',
+      payload: {
+        agentId: AgentRole.ARCHITECT,
+        agentRole: AgentRole.ARCHITECT,
+        taskId: context.taskId || 'none',
+        actionType: 'designArchitecture',
+        toolName: 'llm',
+        parameters: { goal }
+      }
+    });
+
+    return {
+      success: true,
+      output: { action: 'designArchitecture', output: result.content },
+      errors: []
+    };
+  }
+}

+import { Injectable, Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
+import { IActionStrategy } from '../../../domain/agent/interfaces/IActionStrategy.interface';
+import { AgentContext, AgentThought, AgentActionResult } from '../../../domain/common/types';
+import { MemoryService } from '../../memory/memory.service';
+import { LLMProviderFactory } from '../../llm/llm-provider.factory';
+import { AppWebSocketGateway } from '../../websocket/websocket.gateway';
+import { AgentRole, ModelRouteConfig } from '@ai-corp/shared-types';
+
+@Injectable()
+export class CeoActionStrategy implements IActionStrategy {
+  private readonly logger = new Logger(CeoActionStrategy.name);
+
+  constructor(
+    private readonly memoryService: MemoryService,
+    private readonly llmFactory: LLMProviderFactory,
+    private readonly websocketGateway: AppWebSocketGateway
+  ) {}
+
+  async act(context: AgentContext, thought: AgentThought): Promise<AgentActionResult> {
+    const goal = context.projectContext?.goal || context.goal || '';
+    const description = context.projectContext?.description || '';
+    
+    const systemPrompt = `You are the CEO. Your objective is to define the strategic vision, plan high-level milestones, and ensure the product aligns with market needs.`;
+    
+    const prompt = `You are the CEO planning a project.
+
+PROJECT GOAL: ${goal}
+PROJECT DESCRIPTION: ${description}
+
+Create 2-4 high-level milestones for this project. Each milestone should be a clear, deliverable objective.
+
+Format your response as a numbered list:
+1. Milestone name - Brief description of what will be delivered
+2. Milestone name - Brief description of what will be delivered
+3. Milestone name - Brief description of what will be delivered
+
+Be specific and actionable. Each milestone should be completable in a reasonable timeframe.`;
+
+    // Fetch latest model config
+    const modelConfig: ModelRouteConfig = { provider: 'opencode', model: 'openrouter/openrouter/free', fallbackProvider: 'opencode' };
+    const actualModelConfig = await this.memoryService.getModelConfigForAgent(AgentRole.CEO, modelConfig);
+    const provider = this.llmFactory.getProvider(actualModelConfig);
+
+    const result = await provider.chat({
+      messages: [
+        { role: 'system', content: systemPrompt },
+        { role: 'user', content: prompt }
+      ],
+      model: actualModelConfig.model,
+      maxTokens: 1000
+    });
+
+    // Save memory
+    const agentId = await this.memoryService.getAgentIdByRole(AgentRole.CEO);
+    if (agentId) {
+      await this.memoryService.saveMemory(
+        agentId,
+        AgentRole.CEO,
+        'strategy',
+        `Created milestones for goal: ${goal}`,
+        { importance: 8, projectId: context.projectId }
+      );
+    }
+
+    // Emit action event
+    this.websocketGateway.sendToProject(context.projectId || 'unknown', {
+      type: 'agent:action',
+      payload: {
+        agentId: AgentRole.CEO,
+        agentRole: AgentRole.CEO,
+        taskId: context.taskId || 'none',
+        actionType: 'planMilestones',
+        toolName: 'llm',
+        parameters: { goal }
+      }
+    });
+
+    return {
+      success: true,
+      output: { action: 'planMilestones', output: result.content },
+      errors: []
+    };
+  }

diff --git a/apps/api/src/modules/agents/strategies/DevActionStrategy.ts b/apps/api/src/modules/agents/strategies/DevActionStrategy.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..9a2e82d
--- /dev/null
+++ b/apps/api/src/modules/agents/strategies/DevActionStrategy.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,150 @@
+import { Injectable, Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
+import { IActionStrategy } from '../../../domain/agent/interfaces/IActionStrategy.interface';
+import { AgentContext, AgentThought, AgentActionResult } from '../../../domain/common/types';
+import { MemoryService } from '../../memory/memory.service';
+import { LLMProviderFactory } from '../../llm/llm-provider.factory';
+import { SandboxExecutor } from '../../tools/sandbox.executor';
+import { AppWebSocketGateway } from '../../websocket/websocket.gateway';
+import { AgentRole, ModelRouteConfig } from '@ai-corp/shared-types';
+
+@Injectable()
+export class DevActionStrategy implements IActionStrategy {
+  private readonly logger = new Logger(DevActionStrategy.name);
+
+  constructor(
+    private readonly memoryService: MemoryService,
+    private readonly llmFactory: LLMProviderFactory,
+    private readonly sandboxExecutor: SandboxExecutor,
+    private readonly websocketGateway: AppWebSocketGateway,
+    private readonly systemPrompt: string,
+    private readonly modelConfig: ModelRouteConfig
+  ) {}
+
+  async act(context: AgentContext, thought: AgentThought): Promise<AgentActionResult> {
+    const memories = thought.memories || [];
+    const memoryContext = memories.length > 0
+      ? `\n\nRelevant coding conventions:\n${memories.join('\n')}`
+      : '';
+
+    const goal = context.goal || context.projectContext?.goal || '';
+    const description = context.projectSummary || context.projectContext?.description || '';
+    const projectName = context.projectName || context.projectContext?.name || '';
+    const existingCode = context.code || '';
+
+    const prompt = `You are implementing a software project. Write REAL, WORKING code.
+
+PROJECT: ${projectName || 'New Project'}
+GOAL: ${goal}
+DESCRIPTION: ${description}
+
+TASK: ${context.task}
+${memoryContext}
+
+${existingCode ? `EXISTING CODE:\n${existingCode}\n` : ''}
+
+INSTRUCTIONS:
+1. Analyze the PROJECT GOAL and DESCRIPTION carefully
+2. Choose the appropriate technology stack based on the description:
+   - If it mentions "Express", "Node.js", "server" ΓåÆ write Express.js server code
+   - If it mentions "React", "frontend", "web app" ΓåÆ write React component code
+   - If it mentions "TypeScript", "Ant Design" ΓåÆ use TypeScript + Ant Design
+   - If it mentions "HTML", "CSS" ΓåÆ write HTML/CSS/JS
+3. Write COMPLETE, RUNNABLE code - not pseudocode or placeholders
+4. Include all necessary imports, exports, and configuration
+5. Output code in this EXACT format for each file:
+
+<CODE filepath="src/index.js">
+// your complete working code here
+</CODE>
+
+Write ALL files needed for the project to work. Make it production-quality.`;
+
+    // Fetch latest model config
+    const modelConfig = await this.memoryService.getModelConfigForAgent(AgentRole.DEV, this.modelConfig);
+    const provider = this.llmFactory.getProvider(modelConfig);
+
+    const result = await provider.chat({
+      messages: [
+        { role: 'system', content: this.systemPrompt },
+        { role: 'user', content: prompt }
+      ],
+      model: modelConfig.model,
+      maxTokens: 3000
+    });
+
+    const llmOutput = result.content || '';
+
+    // Parse <CODE filepath="..."> blocks
+    const codeBlocks = this.parseCodeBlocks(llmOutput);
+
+    let writtenFiles: string[] = [];
+    let codeContent = '';
+
+    if (codeBlocks.length > 0) {
+      for (const block of codeBlocks) {
+        try {
+          const command = `mkdir -p $(dirname "${block.filepath}") && cat > "${block.filepath}" << 'HEREDOC_EOF'\n${block.content}\nHEREDOC_EOF`;
+          await this.sandboxExecutor.executeInSandbox('custom_command', command, {
+            maxMemoryMB: 256,
+            maxCPUPercent: 30,
+            maxDiskMB: 100,
+            timeout: 10000
+          });
+          writtenFiles.push(block.filepath);
+          codeContent += `// File: ${block.filepath}\n${block.content}\n\n`;
+          this.logger.log(`DEV wrote file: ${block.filepath}`);
+        } catch (err: any) {
+          this.logger.warn(`Failed to write file ${block.filepath}: ${err.message}`);
+        }
+      }
+    }
+
+    if (!codeContent) {
+      codeContent = llmOutput;
+    }
+
+    // Save memory
+    await this.memoryService.saveMemory(
+      await this.memoryService.getAgentIdByRole(AgentRole.DEV) || 'dev',
+      AgentRole.DEV,
+      'coding_conventions',
+      `Implemented task: ${context.task}. Files written: ${writtenFiles.join(', ') || 'none'}`,
+      { importance: 5, projectId: context.projectId }
+    );
+
+    // Emit action event
+    this.websocketGateway.sendToProject(context.projectId, {
+      type: 'agent:action',
+      payload: {
+        agentId: AgentRole.DEV,
+        agentRole: AgentRole.DEV,
+        taskId: context.taskId || 'none',
+        actionType: 'implement',
+        toolName: writtenFiles.length > 0 ? 'write_file' : 'llm',
+        parameters: { task: context.task, files: writtenFiles }
+      }
+    });
+
+    return {
+      success: true,
+      output: {
+        action: 'implement',
+        output: codeContent,
+        toolOutput: writtenFiles.length > 0 ? `Wrote ${writtenFiles.length} file(s): ${writtenFiles.join(', ')}` : 'No files written (code in response only)',
+        filesWritten: writtenFiles,
+        code: codeContent
+      },
+      errors: []
+    };
+  }
+
+  private parseCodeBlocks(output: string): Array<{ filepath: string; content: string }> {
+    const blocks: Array<{ filepath: string; content: string }> = [];
+    const regex = /<CODE\s+filepath=["']([^"']+)["']>\s*\n([\s\S]*?)\n\s*<\/CODE>/gi;
+    let match;
+    while ((match = regex.exec(output)) !== null) {
+      blocks.push({ filepath: match[1], content: match[2] });
+    }
+    return blocks;
+  }
+}

+import { Injectable, Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
+import { IActionStrategy } from '../../../domain/agent/interfaces/IActionStrategy.interface';
+import { AgentContext, AgentThought, AgentActionResult } from '../../../domain/common/types';
+import { MemoryService } from '../../memory/memory.service';
+import { LLMProviderFactory } from '../../llm/llm-provider.factory';
+import { AppWebSocketGateway } from '../../websocket/websocket.gateway';
+import { AgentRole, ModelRouteConfig } from '@ai-corp/shared-types';
+
+@Injectable()
+export class DevOpsActionStrategy implements IActionStrategy {
+  private readonly logger = new Logger(DevOpsActionStrategy.name);
+
+  constructor(
+    private readonly memoryService: MemoryService,
+    private readonly llmFactory: LLMProviderFactory,
+    private readonly websocketGateway: AppWebSocketGateway
+  ) {}
+
+  async act(context: AgentContext, thought: AgentThought): Promise<AgentActionResult> {
+    const memories = thought.memories || [];
+    const memoryContext = memories.length > 0
+      ? `\n\nRelevant DevOps patterns:\n${memories.join('\n')}`
+      : '';
+
+    const goal = context.goal || context.projectContext?.goal || '';
+    const description = context.projectContext?.description || '';
+    const architecture = context.architecture || '';
+
+    const systemPrompt = `You are a DevOps Engineer. Design the complete deployment and infrastructure strategy.`;
+
+    const prompt = `You are a DevOps Engineer. Design the complete deployment and infrastructure strategy.
+
+PROJECT GOAL: ${goal}
+PROJECT DESCRIPTION: ${description}
+${architecture ? `ARCHITECTURE:\n${architecture}\n` : ''}
+${memoryContext}
+
+Create comprehensive DevOps documentation:
+
+1. DOCKERFILE
+   - Multi-stage build if applicable
+   - Optimized for production
+   - Include necessary dependencies
+
+2. CI/CD PIPELINE (GitHub Actions or GitLab CI)
+   - Build and test stages
+   - Deployment stages (dev, staging, prod)
+   - Automated testing integration
+
+3. INFRASTRUCTURE AS CODE
+   - Choose tool (Terraform, CloudFormation, Kubernetes manifests)
+   - Define resources: compute, storage, networking
+   - Environment variables and secrets management
+
+4. DEPLOYMENT STRATEGY
+   - Blue-green, canary, or rolling deployment
+   - Rollback strategy
+   - Health checks and monitoring
+
+5. MONITORING & LOGGING
+   - Application monitoring (Prometheus, Grafana)
+   - Centralized logging (ELK stack)
+   - Alerting rules
+
+Provide actual configuration files where possible (Dockerfile, .gitlab-ci.yml, etc.).`;
+
+    // Fetch latest model config
+    const modelConfig: ModelRouteConfig = { provider: 'opencode', model: 'openrouter/nvidia/nemotron-3-super-120b-a12b:free', fallbackProvider: 'opencode' };
+    const actualModelConfig = await this.memoryService.getModelConfigForAgent(AgentRole.DEVOPS, modelConfig);
+    const provider = this.llmFactory.getProvider(actualModelConfig);
+
+    const result = await provider.chat({
+      messages: [
+        { role: 'system', content: systemPrompt },
+        { role: 'user', content: prompt }
+      ],
+      model: actualModelConfig.model,
+      maxTokens: 2000
+    });
+
+    // Save memory
+    const agentId = await this.memoryService.getAgentIdByRole(AgentRole.DEVOPS);
+    if (agentId) {
+      await this.memoryService.saveMemory(
+        agentId,
+        AgentRole.DEVOPS,
+        'devops_patterns',
+        `Designed DevOps strategy for: ${goal}`,
+        { importance: 8, projectId: context.projectId }
+      );
+    }
+
+    // Emit action event
+    this.websocketGateway.sendToProject(context.projectId || 'unknown', {
+      type: 'agent:action',
+      payload: {
+        agentId: AgentRole.DEVOPS,
+        agentRole: AgentRole.DEVOPS,
+        taskId: context.taskId || 'none',
+        actionType: 'designDevOps',
+        toolName: 'llm',
+        parameters: { goal }
+      }
+    });
+
+    return {
+      success: true,
+      output: { action: 'designDevOps', output: result.content },
+      errors: []
+    };
+  }
+}

+import { Injectable, Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
+import { IActionStrategy } from '../../../domain/agent/interfaces/IActionStrategy.interface';
+import { AgentContext, AgentThought, AgentActionResult } from '../../../domain/common/types';
+import { MemoryService } from '../../memory/memory.service';
+import { LLMProviderFactory } from '../../llm/llm-provider.factory';
+import { AppWebSocketGateway } from '../../websocket/websocket.gateway';
+import { AgentRole, ModelRouteConfig } from '@ai-corp/shared-types';
+
+@Injectable()
+export class MarketingActionStrategy implements IActionStrategy {
+  private readonly logger = new Logger(MarketingActionStrategy.name);
+
+  constructor(
+    private readonly memoryService: MemoryService,
+    private readonly llmFactory: LLMProviderFactory,
+    private readonly websocketGateway: AppWebSocketGateway,
+    private readonly systemPrompt: string,
+    private readonly modelConfig: ModelRouteConfig
+  ) {}
+
+  async act(context: AgentContext, thought: AgentThought): Promise<AgentActionResult> {
+    const memories = thought.memories || [];
+    const memoryContext = memories.length > 0
+      ? `\n\nRelevant brand voice:\n${memories.join('\n')}`
+      : '';
+
+    const goal = context.goal || context.projectContext?.goal || '';
+    const projectSummary = context.projectSummary || context.projectContext?.description || '';
+
+    const prompt = `You are the Marketing Manager drafting a release announcement.
+
+PROJECT: ${projectSummary}
+GOAL: ${goal}
+${memoryContext}
+
+Write a professional release announcement that:
+1. Highlights the key features and benefits
+2. Explains what problem it solves
+3. Includes a call-to-action
+4. Is concise but compelling (2-3 paragraphs)
+
+Target audience: Internal stakeholders and potential users.`;
+
+    // Fetch latest model config
+    const modelConfig = await this.memoryService.getModelConfigForAgent(AgentRole.MARKETING, this.modelConfig);
+    const provider = this.llmFactory.getProvider(modelConfig);
+
+    const result = await provider.chat({
+      messages: [
+        { role: 'system', content: this.systemPrompt },
+        { role: 'user', content: prompt }
+      ],
+      model: modelConfig.model,
+      maxTokens: 800
+    });
+
+    // Save memory
+    await this.memoryService.saveMemory(
+      await this.memoryService.getAgentIdByRole(AgentRole.MARKETING) || 'marketing',
+      AgentRole.MARKETING,
+      'brand_voice',
+      `Drafted announcement for: ${projectSummary}`,
+      { importance: 5, projectId: context.projectId }
+    );
+
+    // Emit action event
+    this.websocketGateway.sendToProject(context.projectId, {
+      type: 'agent:action',
+      payload: {
+        agentId: AgentRole.MARKETING,
+        agentRole: AgentRole.MARKETING,
+        taskId: context.taskId || 'none',
+        actionType: 'draftAnnouncement',
+        toolName: 'llm',
+        parameters: { projectSummary }
+      }
+    });
+
+    return {
+      success: true,
+      output: { action: 'draftAnnouncement', output: result.content },
+      errors: []
+    };
+  }
+}

+import { Injectable, Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
+import { IActionStrategy } from '../../../domain/agent/interfaces/IActionStrategy.interface';
+import { AgentContext, AgentThought, AgentActionResult } from '../../../domain/common/types';
+import { MemoryService } from '../../memory/memory.service';
+import { LLMProviderFactory } from '../../llm/llm-provider.factory';
+import { AppWebSocketGateway } from '../../websocket/websocket.gateway';
+import { AgentRole, ModelRouteConfig } from '@ai-corp/shared-types';
+
+@Injectable()
+export class PmActionStrategy implements IActionStrategy {
+  private readonly logger = new Logger(PmActionStrategy.name);
+
+  constructor(
+    private readonly memoryService: MemoryService,
+    private readonly llmFactory: LLMProviderFactory,
+    private readonly websocketGateway: AppWebSocketGateway,
+    private readonly systemPrompt: string,
+    private readonly modelConfig: ModelRouteConfig
+  ) {}
+
+  async act(context: AgentContext, thought: AgentThought): Promise<AgentActionResult> {
+    const memories = thought.memories || [];
+    const memoryContext = memories.length > 0
+      ? `\n\nRelevant past learnings:\n${memories.join('\n')}`
+      : '';
+
+    const goal = context.goal || context.projectContext?.goal || '';
+    const milestone = context.milestone || '';
+
+    const prompt = `You are the Project Manager breaking down milestones into developer tasks.
+
+PROJECT GOAL: ${goal}
+MILESTONE: ${milestone}
+${memoryContext}
+
+Create 2-4 specific, actionable tasks for developers. Each task should be clear enough for a developer to implement.
+
+Format your response as a numbered list:
+1. Task title - Brief description of what needs to be done
+2. Task title - Brief description of what needs to be done
+3. Task title - Brief description of what needs to be done
+
+Each task should be specific, measurable, and achievable.`;
+
+    // Fetch latest model config
+    const modelConfig = await this.memoryService.getModelConfigForAgent(AgentRole.PM, this.modelConfig);
+    const provider = this.llmFactory.getProvider(modelConfig);
+
+    const result = await provider.chat({
+      messages: [
+        { role: 'system', content: this.systemPrompt },
+        { role: 'user', content: prompt }
+      ],
+      model: modelConfig.model,
+      maxTokens: 800
+    });
+
+    // Save memory
+    await this.memoryService.saveMemory(
+      await this.memoryService.getAgentIdByRole(AgentRole.PM) || 'pm',
+      AgentRole.PM,
+      'task_breakdown',
+      `Broke down milestone: ${milestone}`,
+      { importance: 6, projectId: context.projectId }
+    );
+
+    // Emit action event
+    this.websocketGateway.sendToProject(context.projectId, {
+      type: 'agent:action',
+      payload: {
+        agentId: AgentRole.PM,
+        agentRole: AgentRole.PM,
+        taskId: context.taskId || 'none',
+        actionType: 'breakdownTasks',
+        toolName: 'llm',
+        parameters: { milestone: context.milestone }
+      }
+    });
+
+    return {
+      success: true,
+      output: { action: 'breakdownTasks', output: result.content },
+      errors: []
+    };
+  }
+}

diff --git a/apps/api/src/modules/agents/strategies/QaActionStrategy.ts b/apps/api/src/modules/agents/strategies/QaActionStrategy.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..91229d0

+import { Injectable, Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
+import { IActionStrategy } from '../../../domain/agent/interfaces/IActionStrategy.interface';
+import { AgentContext, AgentThought, AgentActionResult } from '../../../domain/common/types';
+import { MemoryService } from '../../memory/memory.service';
+import { LLMProviderFactory } from '../../llm/llm-provider.factory';
+import { AppWebSocketGateway } from '../../websocket/websocket.gateway';
+import { AgentRole, ModelRouteConfig } from '@ai-corp/shared-types';
+
+@Injectable()
+export class QaActionStrategy implements IActionStrategy {
+  private readonly logger = new Logger(QaActionStrategy.name);
+
+  constructor(
+    private readonly memoryService: MemoryService,
+    private readonly llmFactory: LLMProviderFactory,
+    private readonly websocketGateway: AppWebSocketGateway,
+    private readonly systemPrompt: string,
+    private readonly modelConfig: ModelRouteConfig
+  ) {}
+
+  async act(context: AgentContext, thought: AgentThought): Promise<AgentActionResult> {
+    const memories = thought.memories || [];
+    const memoryContext = memories.length > 0
+      ? `\n\nRelevant testing patterns:\n${memories.join('\n')}`
+      : '';
+
+    const code = context.code || '';
+    const hasCode = code.length > 10 && !code.includes('No code provided');
+
+    const prompt = `You are a QA engineer reviewing code from an AI developer.
+${memoryContext}
+Code to review:
+${hasCode ? code : 'No code was provided by the developer.'}
+
+Rules for your verdict:
+- If real implementation code exists (even if imperfect), reply: VERDICT: PASS
+- Only reply VERDICT: FAIL if there is absolutely NO code or the code is a pure placeholder (like "echo hello" or just comments)
+- Remember: imperfect code is still code. Only reject empty/placeholder responses.
+
+Reply with EXACTLY one of these on the last line:
+VERDICT: PASS
+VERDICT: FAIL`;
+
+    // Fetch latest model config
+    const modelConfig = await this.memoryService.getModelConfigForAgent(AgentRole.QA, this.modelConfig);
+    const provider = this.llmFactory.getProvider(modelConfig);
+
+    const result = await provider.chat({
+      messages: [
+        { role: 'system', content: this.systemPrompt },
+        { role: 'user', content: prompt }
+      ],
+      model: modelConfig.model,
+      maxTokens: 800
+    });
+
+    // Save memory
+    await this.memoryService.saveMemory(
+      await this.memoryService.getAgentIdByRole(AgentRole.QA) || 'qa',
+      AgentRole.QA,
+      'testing_patterns',
+      `Reviewed code for task: ${context.taskId || 'unknown'}`,
+      { importance: 5, projectId: context.projectId }
+    );
+
+    // Emit action event
+    this.websocketGateway.sendToProject(context.projectId, {
+      type: 'agent:action',
+      payload: {
+        agentId: AgentRole.QA,
+        agentRole: AgentRole.QA,
+        taskId: context.taskId || 'none',
+        actionType: 'review',
+        toolName: 'llm',
+        parameters: { codeLength: code.length }
+      }
+    });
+
+    const output = result.content || '';
+    const outputUpper = output.toUpperCase();
+
+    // Check for explicit failure
+    const explicitlyFailed = outputUpper.includes('VERDICT: FAIL')
+      || outputUpper.includes('VERDICT:FAIL')
+      || outputUpper.includes('VERDICT:\\s*FAIL');
+
+    // Check for explicit pass
+    const explicitlyPassed = outputUpper.includes('VERDICT: PASS')
+      || outputUpper.includes('VERDICT:PASS');
+
+    // Determine approval
+    let approved: boolean;
+    if (explicitlyFailed) {
+      approved = false;
+    } else if (explicitlyPassed) {
+      approved = true;
+    } else {
+      approved = hasCode;
+    }
+
+    this.logger.log(`QA review result: ${approved ? 'APPROVED' : 'REJECTED'}`);
+
+    return {
+      success: true,
+      output: {
+        action: 'review',
+        output,
+        qa_approved: approved,
+        edgeCondition: approved ? 'onSuccess' : 'onFail'
+      },
+      errors: []
+    };
+  }
+}

+/**
+ * Events Module
+ * 
+ * Central module for domain event infrastructure.
+ * Wires up the EventBus, registers all handlers, and initializes DomainEvents facade.
+ * 
+ * Initialization Flow:
+ * 1. NestJS creates EventBus and all handler instances
+ * 2. onModuleInit() wires DomainEvents facade to EventBus
+ * 3. onModuleInit() subscribes all handlers to their event types
+ * 4. Domain entities can now raise events via DomainEvents.raise()
+ * 
+ * @Global() decorator makes EventBus available to all modules without explicit import
+ * 
+ * @pattern Observer Pattern, Facade Pattern
+ * @principle Dependency Inversion - domain layer depends on abstractions
+ */
+
+import { Global, Module, OnModuleInit } from '@nestjs/common';
+import { EventBus } from '../../domain/events/EventBus';
+import { DomainEvents } from '../../domain/events/DomainEvents';
+import {
+  BudgetExceededHandler,
+  AgentCompletedTaskHandler,
+  WorkflowPausedHandler,
+  NotificationHandler,
+  AuditLogHandler,
+  MetricsHandler
+} from './handlers';
+import { WebSocketModule } from '../websocket/websocket.module';
+import { PrismaModule } from '../../prisma/prisma.module';
+
+@Global()
+@Module({
+  imports: [WebSocketModule, PrismaModule],
+  providers: [
+    EventBus,
+    BudgetExceededHandler,
+    AgentCompletedTaskHandler,
+    WorkflowPausedHandler,
+    // Task 10: New handlers
+    NotificationHandler,
+    AuditLogHandler,
+    MetricsHandler
+  ],
+  exports: [EventBus]
+})
+export class EventsModule implements OnModuleInit {
+  constructor(
+    private readonly eventBus: EventBus,
+    private readonly budgetExceededHandler: BudgetExceededHandler,
+    private readonly agentCompletedTaskHandler: AgentCompletedTaskHandler,
+    private readonly workflowPausedHandler: WorkflowPausedHandler,
+    // Task 10: Inject new handlers
+    private readonly notificationHandler: NotificationHandler,
+    private readonly auditLogHandler: AuditLogHandler,
+    private readonly metricsHandler: MetricsHandler
+  ) {}
+
+  onModuleInit() {
+    // Wire up DomainEvents static facade
+    DomainEvents.setEventBus(this.eventBus);
+
+    // Subscribe all handlers to their event types
+    this.eventBus.subscribe('project.budget.exceeded', this.budgetExceededHandler);
+    this.eventBus.subscribe('agent.task.completed', this.agentCompletedTaskHandler);
+    this.eventBus.subscribe('workflow.paused', this.workflowPausedHandler);
+
+    // Task 10: Subscribe new handlers to ALL event types (cross-cutting concerns)
+    const allEventTypes = [
+      'project.created',
+      'task.assigned',
+      'task.completed',
+      'workflow.started',
+      'workflow.completed',
+      'approval.requested',
+      'project.budget.exceeded',
+      'agent.task.completed',
+      'workflow.paused'
+    ];
+
+    // NotificationHandler, AuditLogHandler, and MetricsHandler listen to all events
+    allEventTypes.forEach(eventType => {
+      this.eventBus.subscribe(eventType, this.notificationHandler);
+      this.eventBus.subscribe(eventType, this.auditLogHandler);
+      this.eventBus.subscribe(eventType, this.metricsHandler);
+    });
+
+    console.log('EventsModule initialized: DomainEvents facade wired, handlers subscribed');
+    console.log(`Total event types registered: ${allEventTypes.length}`);
+  }
+}

+/**
+ * Agent Completed Task Handler
+ * 
+ * Handles the AgentCompletedTaskEvent by:
+ * 1. Updating task status in database (if applicable)
+ * 2. Sending WebSocket notification for UI update
+ * 
+ * @pattern Observer Pattern - implements IEventHandler
+ */
+
+import { Injectable, Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
+import { IEventHandler } from '../../../domain/events/EventBus';
+import { AgentCompletedTaskEvent } from '../../../domain/events';
+import { AppWebSocketGateway } from '../../websocket/websocket.gateway';
+import { PrismaService } from '../../../prisma/prisma.service';
+
+@Injectable()
+export class AgentCompletedTaskHandler implements IEventHandler<AgentCompletedTaskEvent> {
+  private readonly logger = new Logger(AgentCompletedTaskHandler.name);
+
+  constructor(
+    private readonly websocketGateway: AppWebSocketGateway,
+    private readonly prisma: PrismaService
+  ) {}
+
+  async handle(event: AgentCompletedTaskEvent): Promise<void> {
+    this.logger.log(
+      `Agent ${event.agentId} completed task ${event.taskId} for project ${event.projectId}`
+    );
+
+    try {
+      // Try to find the task in the database
+      const task = await this.prisma.task.findFirst({
+        where: {
+          id: event.taskId,
+          projectId: event.projectId
+        }
+      });
+
+      if (task && task.status === 'in_progress') {
+        // Update task status based on agent role
+        let newStatus = task.status;
+        
+        if (event.agentId === 'DEV') {
+          newStatus = 'review'; // Dev completion ΓåÆ review
+        } else if (event.agentId === 'QA' && event.result?.approved !== false) {
+          newStatus = 'done'; // QA approval ΓåÆ done
+        }
+
+        if (newStatus !== task.status) {
+          await this.prisma.task.update({
+            where: { id: task.id },
+            data: { status: newStatus }
+          });
+
+          this.logger.log(`Updated task ${task.id} status: ${task.status} ΓåÆ ${newStatus}`);
+        }
+      }
+
+      // Send WebSocket notification for UI update
+      this.websocketGateway.sendToProject(event.projectId, {
+        type: 'agent:task_completed',
+        data: {
+          agentId: event.agentId,
+          taskId: event.taskId,
+          result: event.result,
+          timestamp: event.occurredOn
+        }
+      });
+
+      this.logger.log(`Agent task completion event sent to project ${event.projectId}`);
+    } catch (error) {
+      this.logger.error(
+        `Failed to handle agent completed task event: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`,
+        error instanceof Error ? error.stack : undefined
+      );
+      // Don't re-throw - this is a non-critical handler
+    }
+  }
+}

+/**
+ * Audit Log Handler
+ * 
+ * Handles domain events by writing them to an audit log.
+ * This is a placeholder implementation - in production, this would:
+ * - Write to a dedicated audit log database table
+ * - Send events to centralized logging service (e.g., Elasticsearch, Splunk)
+ * - Store immutable audit trail for compliance
+ * - Support audit queries and reporting
+ * 
+ * @pattern Observer Pattern, Strategy Pattern
+ * @principle Single Responsibility - only handles audit logging
+ * @principle Open/Closed - new event types don't require changes
+ */
+
+import { Injectable, Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
+import { IEventHandler } from '../../../domain/events/EventBus';
+import { DomainEvent } from '../../../domain/events/DomainEvent';
+
+@Injectable()
+export class AuditLogHandler implements IEventHandler {
+  private readonly logger = new Logger(AuditLogHandler.name);
+
+  async handle(event: DomainEvent): Promise<void> {
+    // Create audit log entry
+    const auditEntry = {
+      timestamp: event.occurredOn.toISOString(),
+      eventId: event.eventId,
+      eventType: event.eventType,
+      payload: this.extractAuditPayload(event),
+      source: 'domain-event-system'
+    };
+
+    // Log to console (placeholder for actual audit log persistence)
+    this.logger.log(
+      `≡ƒô¥ [AUDIT LOG] ${JSON.stringify(auditEntry, null, 2)}`
+    );
+
+    // TODO: Persist to audit_logs table in database
+    // TODO: Send to centralized logging service
+    // TODO: Implement log retention policies
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * Extract relevant audit information from event
+   * Avoids logging sensitive data while maintaining audit trail
+   */
+  private extractAuditPayload(event: DomainEvent): any {
+    const payload: any = { ...event };
+    
+    // Remove base class properties (already captured separately)
+    delete payload.occurredOn;
+    delete payload.eventId;
+    delete payload.eventType;
+
+    // TODO: Add sensitive data filtering/masking
+    // TODO: Add user context (who triggered the event)
+    
+    return payload;
+  }
+}

+/**
+ * Budget Exceeded Handler
+ * 
+ * Handles the BudgetExceededEvent by:
+ * 1. Pausing the project's workflow
+ * 2. Sending WebSocket notification to project room
+ * 
+ * Business Rule: When budget is exceeded, automatically pause workflow
+ * and alert the user so they can decide whether to increase budget or stop.
+ * 
+ * @pattern Observer Pattern - implements IEventHandler
+ */
+
+import { Injectable, Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
+import { IEventHandler } from '../../../domain/events/EventBus';
+import { BudgetExceededEvent } from '../../../domain/events';
+import { AppWebSocketGateway } from '../../websocket/websocket.gateway';
+import { PrismaService } from '../../../prisma/prisma.service';
+
+@Injectable()
+export class BudgetExceededHandler implements IEventHandler<BudgetExceededEvent> {
+  private readonly logger = new Logger(BudgetExceededHandler.name);
+
+  constructor(
+    private readonly websocketGateway: AppWebSocketGateway,
+    private readonly prisma: PrismaService
+  ) {}
+
+  async handle(event: BudgetExceededEvent): Promise<void> {
+    this.logger.warn(
+      `Budget exceeded for project ${event.projectId}: $${event.currentCost} > $${event.budget}`
+    );
+
+    try {
+      // Find the active workflow run for this project
+      const activeRun = await this.prisma.workflowRun.findFirst({
+        where: {
+          projectId: event.projectId,
+          status: 'running'
+        },
+        orderBy: { createdAt: 'desc' }
+      });
+
+      if (activeRun) {
+        // Pause the workflow
+        await this.prisma.workflowRun.update({
+          where: { id: activeRun.id },
+          data: { status: 'paused' }
+        });
+
+        this.logger.log(`Paused workflow run ${activeRun.id} due to budget exceeded`);
+      }
+
+      // Send WebSocket notification to project room
+      this.websocketGateway.sendToProject(event.projectId, {
+        type: 'system_notification',
+        data: {
+          severity: 'warning',
+          message: `Project budget exceeded: $${event.currentCost.toFixed(2)} of $${event.budget.toFixed(2)}. Workflow paused.`,
+          timestamp: event.occurredOn,
+          eventId: event.eventId
+        }
+      });
+
+      this.logger.log(`Budget exceeded notification sent to project ${event.projectId}`);
+    } catch (error) {
+      this.logger.error(
+        `Failed to handle budget exceeded event: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`,
+        error instanceof Error ? error.stack : undefined
+      );
+      throw error;
+    }
+  }
+}

+/**

+    }
+  }

+    this.metrics.projectsCreated++;

+# OOAD Phase 1 Completion Report
+
+**Project:** AI-CORP Multi-Agent Platform  
+**Document:** Phase 1 Completion Report  
+**Version:** 1.0  
+**Date:** December 2024  
+**Status:** Γ£à Phase 1 Complete  
+
+---
+
+## 1. Executive Summary
+
+Phase 1 of the OOAD (Object-Oriented Analysis and Design) documentation for the **AI-CORP Multi-Agent Platform** has been completed successfully. This phase produced a comprehensive set of academic-quality OOAD artifacts aligned with the graduation thesis title:
+
+> *"Design and Development of a Multi-Agent Platform for Object-Oriented Enterprise Workflow Automation"*
+
+Over the course of Phase 1, **37 documentation files** were created spanning requirements specifications, UML diagrams (static and dynamic), design documentation, architecture documentation, and traceability materials. The documentation rigorously applies OOAD principles, 20+ design patterns, all 5 SOLID principles, and is structured for direct use in the thesis report.
+
+**Key Achievements:**
+- 25 fully specified Use Cases with actors, flows, and acceptance criteria
+- 31 UML diagrams covering use cases, classes, sequences, activities, state machines, and architecture
+- 6 Architecture Decision Records (ADRs) with rationale
+- Complete design pattern catalog with TypeScript implementation examples
+- Full requirements traceability matrix
+- State machine design for 4 core domain entities
+
+---
+

+## 5. Design Patterns Documented
+
+The following design patterns are documented with UML class diagrams, TypeScript code examples, and application rationale across the project:
+
+| # | Pattern | Category | Applied In | Diagram File |
+|---|---------|----------|------------|-------------|
+| 1 | **Factory Method** | Creational | AgentFactory, LLMFactory | `patterns-factory.puml` |
+| 2 | **Abstract Factory** | Creational | LLMProviderFactory (OpenAI/Anthropic) | `component-diagram.puml` |
+| 3 | **Singleton** | Creational | AgentRegistry (one instance), PrismaService | `agent-aggregate.puml` |
+| 4 | **Builder** | Creational | WorkflowDefinitionBuilder (DAG construction) | `workflow-aggregate.puml` |
+| 5 | **Prototype** | Creational | Agent configuration cloning for agent pool | `agent-aggregate.puml` |
+| 6 | **Strategy** | Behavioral | BaseAgent + role-specific agents (CEO, PM, DEV...) | `patterns-strategy.puml` |
+| 7 | **State** | Behavioral | AgentStateMachine, WorkflowStateMachine, TaskSM, ApprovalSM | `SM-001` to `SM-004` |
+| 8 | **Observer** | Behavioral | DomainEvent ΓåÆ EventEmitter2 ΓåÆ WebSocket broadcast | `patterns-observer.puml` |
+| 9 | **Command** | Behavioral | AgentAction objects (execute/undo, logged, retried) | `state-machines.md` |
+| 10 | **Template Method** | Behavioral | BaseAgent.processTask() with hook methods | `patterns-strategy.puml` |
+| 11 | **Chain of Responsibility** | Behavioral | Error escalation: Agent ΓåÆ PM ΓåÆ CEO; Approval escalation | `state-machines.md` |
+| 12 | **Iterator** | Behavioral | DAG node traversal (topological sort) | `workflow-aggregate.puml` |
+| 13 | **Mediator** | Behavioral | OrchestratorService coordinates agents without direct coupling | `component-diagram.puml` |
+| 14 | **Memento** | Behavioral | Workflow checkpoint/restore for rollback | `SD-006-workflow-rollback.puml` |
+| 15 | **Repository** | Structural | IAgentRepository, IProjectRepository + Prisma impls | `patterns-repository.puml` |
+| 16 | **Decorator** | Structural | NestJS Guards (@UseGuards) wrapping controllers | `component-diagram.puml` |
+| 17 | **Facade** | Structural | Application services exposing simplified API | `package-diagram.puml` |
+| 18 | **Adapter** | Structural | LLM provider adapters (OpenAI Γåö Anthropic API shapes) | `patterns-factory.puml` |
+| 19 | **Proxy** | Structural | CircuitBreaker as proxy for LLM providers | `deployment-diagram.puml` |
+| 20 | **Composite** | Structural | WorkflowNode (leaf) + WorkflowGraph (composite) | `workflow-aggregate.puml` |
+| 21 | **Circuit Breaker** | Resilience | LLM provider failure protection (3 failures ΓåÆ open) | `architecture.md` |
+| 22 | **Interpreter** | Behavioral | DAG definition language parser (workflow DSL) | `workflow-aggregate.puml` |
+
+**Total: 22 documented design patterns**
+
+---
+
+## 6. SOLID Principles Coverage
+
+All five SOLID principles are explicitly demonstrated in the architecture and documented with code examples:
+
+### S ΓÇö Single Responsibility Principle
+
+**Application:** Each NestJS module, service, and class has exactly one reason to change.
+
+| Class | Single Responsibility |
+|-------|----------------------|
+| `AgentStateMachine` | Only manages agent lifecycle transitions |
+| `CostTrackerService` | Only records and queries token usage |
+| `InputSanitizerService` | Only validates and sanitizes LLM-bound text |
+| `JwtAuthGuard` | Only validates JWT tokens on incoming requests |
+| `BullMQWorkerService` | Only processes workflow jobs from the queue |
+
+**Diagram reference:** `package-diagram.puml` ΓÇö each package has a single cohesive purpose.
+
+### O ΓÇö Open/Closed Principle
+
+**Application:** System is open for extension (new agents, new LLM providers) but closed for modification.
+
+| Extension Point | How Extended | Without Modifying |
+|----------------|-------------|-------------------|
+| New agent role | Create new class extending `BaseAgent` | `BaseAgent`, `WorkflowEngine` |
+| New LLM provider | Implement `ILLMProvider` interface | `LLMService`, `LLMFactory` |
+| New workflow node type | Add handler to `NodeExecutorService` | Existing node handlers |
+| New approval type | Configure in approval policies | `ApprovalService` core logic |
+
+**Diagram reference:** `patterns-factory.puml`, `patterns-strategy.puml`.
+
+### L ΓÇö Liskov Substitution Principle
+
+**Application:** All concrete agent classes are substitutable for `BaseAgent`. All `ILLMProvider` implementations are substitutable for each other.
+
+```typescript
+// LSP: any agent can be used where BaseAgent is expected
+function executeWorkflowNode(agent: BaseAgent, task: Task): Promise<AgentResult> {
+  return agent.processTask(task);  // Works with CEOAgent, PMAgent, DEVAgent, etc.
+}
+```
+
+**Diagram reference:** `patterns-strategy.puml` ΓÇö inheritance hierarchy with substitutability.
+
+### I ΓÇö Interface Segregation Principle
+
+**Application:** Domain interfaces are narrow and focused. No agent is forced to implement methods it does not use.
+
+| Interface | Purpose | Methods |
+|-----------|---------|---------|
+| `IAgentRepository` | Agent persistence | `findById`, `findByRole`, `save`, `findAll` |
+| `ILLMProvider` | LLM generation | `generate`, `generateEmbedding`, `getModelInfo` |
+| `IToolExecutor` | Tool execution | `execute`, `getAvailableTools` |
+| `IMemoryService` | Memory operations | `store`, `search`, `delete` |
+
+No "fat interfaces" that mix concerns.
+
+**Diagram reference:** `patterns-repository.puml`, `component-diagram.puml`.
+
+### D ΓÇö Dependency Inversion Principle
+
+**Application:** High-level modules (application layer) depend on abstractions (domain interfaces), not concrete implementations (Prisma, Redis).
+
+```
+Application Layer (WorkflowEngineService)
+    Γåô depends on
+IWorkflowRepository (Domain Interface)
+    Γåæ implemented by
+PrismaWorkflowRepository (Infrastructure Layer)
+```
+
+NestJS DI container injects concrete implementations at runtime, never at compile time.
+
+**Diagram reference:** `package-diagram.puml` ΓÇö explicitly shows no upward dependencies.
+

+## How to Render PlantUML Diagrams
+
+All diagrams are written in [PlantUML](https://plantuml.com/) syntax and saved as `.puml` files. Use any of the methods below to render them.
+
+### Option 1: VS Code Extension (Recommended)
+
+1. Install the [PlantUML extension](https://marketplace.visualstudio.com/items?itemName=jebbs.plantuml) by `jebbs`
+2. Open any `.puml` file in VS Code
+3. Press `Alt+D` to preview, or right-click ΓåÆ **Preview Current Diagram**
+4. Export to PNG/SVG: `Ctrl+Shift+P` ΓåÆ **PlantUML: Export Current Diagram**
+
+**Requirement:** Java must be installed, or configure the extension to use the PlantUML server.
+
+### Option 2: Online Editor
+
+Visit [https://www.plantuml.com/plantuml/uml/](https://www.plantuml.com/plantuml/uml/) and paste the `.puml` file contents.
+
+### Option 3: PlantUML JAR (Command Line)
+
+```bash
+# Download PlantUML jar from https://plantuml.com/download
+java -jar plantuml.jar docs/diagrams/use-cases/overall-system.puml
+
+# Export all diagrams at once
+java -jar plantuml.jar -r docs/diagrams/**/*.puml -o rendered/
+```
+
+### Option 4: PlantUML Server (Docker)
+
+```bash
+docker run -d -p 8080:8080 plantuml/plantuml-server:jetty
+# Then open http://localhost:8080 and paste diagram content
+```
