+@startuml deployment-diagram
+title AI-CORP System Deployment Diagram
+'Shows physical deployment topology, network boundaries and protocols
+
+skinparam defaultFontSize 11
+skinparam backgroundColor #FEFEFE
+skinparam node {
+  BackgroundColor #D5F5E3
+  BorderColor #27AE60
+}
+skinparam artifact {
+  BackgroundColor #D6EAF8
+  BorderColor #2980B9
+}
+skinparam database {
+  BackgroundColor #FCF3CF
+  BorderColor #D4AC0D
+}
+skinparam cloud {
+  BackgroundColor #EBDEF0
+  BorderColor #8E44AD
+}
+skinparam rectangle {
+  BackgroundColor #FDFEFE
+  BorderColor #7F8C8D
+}
+skinparam note {
+  BackgroundColor #FFFDE7
+  BorderColor #F9A825
+}
+
+' ============================================================
+' SECURITY ZONES
+' ============================================================
+
+rectangle "<<Security Zone: Public Internet>>" as PUBLIC_ZONE #EEF {
+  node "Client Browser\n<<User Device>>" as CLIENT {
+    artifact "React 18 SPA\n(Vite bundled)" as REACT_APP
+    artifact "Zustand State\n(Client State)" as ZUSTAND
+    artifact "Socket.IO Client\n(WebSocket)" as WS_CLIENT
+    artifact "React Query\n(HTTP Cache)" as RQ
+  }
+}
+
+rectangle "<<Security Zone: DMZ / Reverse Proxy>>" as DMZ_ZONE #FEF {
+  node "Reverse Proxy\n<<Nginx>>" as NGINX {
+    artifact "TLS Termination\n(HTTPS/WSS)" as TLS
+    artifact "Rate Limiter\n(req/min)" as RATE_LIMIT
+    artifact "Static Assets\n(CDN Cache)" as STATIC
+  }
+}
+
+rectangle "<<Security Zone: Application Tier>>" as APP_ZONE #EFE {
+
+  node "API Server\n<<Docker Container>>\nPort: 3000" as API_SERVER {
+    artifact "NestJS Application\n(Node.js 18 LTS)" as NESTJS
+    artifact "Auth Module\n(JWT / RBAC)" as AUTH_MOD
+    artifact "Agent Orchestrator\n(8 AI Agents)" as AGENT_ORCH
+    artifact "Workflow Engine\n(DAG + BullMQ)" as WF_ENGINE_ART
+    artifact "WebSocket Gateway\n(Socket.IO)" as WS_GW
+    artifact "LLM Client\n(Circuit Breaker)" as LLM_CLIENT_ART
+    artifact "Health Endpoint\n(/health)" as HEALTH
+  }
+
+  node "BullMQ Worker\n<<Docker Container>>\n(Separate Process)" as BULL_CONTAINER {
+    artifact "Workflow Worker\n(BullMQ Consumer)" as BULL_WORKER_ART
+    artifact "Retry Handler\n(Exponential Backoff)" as RETRY_ART
+    artifact "Dead Letter Queue\n(Failed Jobs)" as DLQ
+  }
+
+}
+
+rectangle "<<Security Zone: Data Tier>>" as DATA_ZONE #FFF8E1 {
+
+  database "PostgreSQL 16\n+ pgvector\n<<Docker Container>>\nPort: 5432" as POSTGRES {
+    artifact "ai_corp_dev\n(Database)" as PG_DB
+    artifact "pgvector extension\n(Vector Embeddings)" as PGVEC
+    artifact "Prisma Migrations\n(Schema Versioning)" as PG_SCHEMA
+  }
+
+  database "Redis 7\n<<Docker Container>>\nPort: 6379" as REDIS_NODE {
+    artifact "BullMQ Queues\n(Job Processing)" as BULL_Q_ART
+    artifact "Session Cache\n(JWT Blacklist)" as SESSION_CACHE
+    artifact "Pub/Sub Channels\n(Event Bus)" as PUBSUB
+  }
+
+}
+
+rectangle "<<Security Zone: AI/ML Tier>>" as AI_ZONE #F0F8FF {
+
+  node "9Router LLM Gateway\n<<External / Docker>>\nPort: 20128" as LLM_GW {
+    artifact "OpenAI-Compatible API\n(REST Proxy)" as OPENAI_API
+    artifact "Model Router\n(Load Balancer)" as MODEL_ROUTER
+    artifact "Usage Tracker\n(Token Metering)" as USAGE_TRACKER
+  }
+
+  cloud "LLM Providers\n<<External APIs>>" as LLM_CLOUD {
+    artifact "OpenCode Free Models\n(gpt-4o-mini, etc.)" as FREE_MODELS
+    artifact "Anthropic Claude\n(claude-3-haiku)" as CLAUDE
+    artifact "Google Gemini\n(gemini-pro)" as GEMINI
+  }
+
+}
+
+rectangle "<<Security Zone: Execution Sandbox>>" as SANDBOX_ZONE #FFF0F0 {
+
+  node "Docker Host\n<<Sandbox Manager>>" as DOCKER_HOST {
+    node "Sandbox Container 1\n<<node:18-alpine>>\n(Ephemeral)" as SANDBOX_1 {
+      artifact "DEV Agent Code\n(Isolated Exec)" as CODE_EXEC_1
+    }
+    node "Sandbox Container 2\n<<node:18-alpine>>\n(Ephemeral)" as SANDBOX_2 {
+      artifact "QA Agent Tests\n(Isolated Exec)" as CODE_EXEC_2
+    }
+    artifact "seccomp Profile\n(System Call Filter)" as SECCOMP
+    artifact "Non-root User\n(UID 1001)" as NONROOT
+  }
+
+}
+
+' ============================================================
+' CONNECTIONS & PROTOCOLS
+' ============================================================
+
+' Client ΓåÆ DMZ
+CLIENT --> NGINX : HTTPS (TLS 1.3)\nPort 443
+
+' DMZ ΓåÆ App
+NGINX --> API_SERVER : HTTP (internal)\nPort 3000
+NGINX --> API_SERVER : WS (internal)\nPort 3000
+
+' Client WebSocket
+WS_CLIENT --> WS_GW : WSS (WebSocket Secure)\nvia Nginx Proxy
+
+' App ΓåÆ Data
+NESTJS --> POSTGRES : TCP\nPort 5432\n(Prisma ORM)
+NESTJS --> REDIS_NODE : TCP\nPort 6379\n(ioredis)
+BULL_CONTAINER --> REDIS_NODE : TCP\nPort 6379\n(BullMQ)
+WF_ENGINE_ART --> REDIS_NODE : TCP\nPort 6379\n(Job Enqueue)
+
+' App ΓåÆ AI
+LLM_CLIENT_ART --> LLM_GW : HTTP (internal)\nPort 20128\n(OpenAI-compatible)
+LLM_GW --> LLM_CLOUD : HTTPS\nPort 443\n(External API)
+
+' App ΓåÆ Sandbox
+AGENT_ORCH --> DOCKER_HOST : Docker API\nUnix Socket\n(/var/run/docker.sock)
+DOCKER_HOST --> SANDBOX_1 : spawn/destroy
+DOCKER_HOST --> SANDBOX_2 : spawn/destroy
+
+' Worker ΓåÆ App Events
+BULL_WORKER_ART --> NESTJS : Internal Events\n(NestJS EventEmitter)
+NESTJS --> WS_GW : WebSocket Broadcast\n(State Changes)
+
+note bottom of POSTGRES
+  <b>Persistence Strategy:</b>
+  - Relational data (Projects, Tasks, Agents)
+  - Vector embeddings for memory search
+  - Prisma ORM with type-safe queries
+  - Automated migrations via Prisma Migrate
+  
+  <b>Backup:</b> Daily pg_dump snapshots
+  <b>Replication:</b> Not configured (dev/thesis)
+end note
+
+note bottom of REDIS_NODE
+  <b>Usage Patterns:</b>
+  - BullMQ job queues (persistent jobs)
+  - Session cache (JWT token blacklist)
+  - Pub/Sub (real-time state events)
+  
+  <b>Persistence:</b> AOF (Append Only File)
+  <b>Max Memory:</b> 256MB (dev configuration)
+end note
+
+note right of DOCKER_HOST
+  <b>Sandbox Security:</b>
+  - No network access from sandbox
+  - seccomp system call filtering
+  - Non-root execution (UID 1001)
+  - 30 second timeout limit
+  - 256MB memory limit per container
+  - Read-only filesystem (except /tmp)
+  - Containers destroyed after use
+end note
+
+note right of LLM_GW
+  <b>Circuit Breaker Config:</b>
+  - Opens after 3 consecutive failures
+  - Half-open after 30 seconds
+  - Closes after 2 successful probes
+  
+  <b>Rate Limiting:</b>
+  - 60 requests/minute per project
+  - 1000 tokens/request limit
+  
+  <b>Cost Tracking:</b>
+  - Per-request token metering
+  - Budget enforcement at API layer
+end note
+
+note top of CLIENT
+  <b>Security Headers:</b>
+  - Content-Security-Policy
+  - X-Frame-Options: DENY
+  - HSTS (Strict-Transport-Security)
+  
+  <b>Auth Flow:</b>
+  - JWT stored in httpOnly cookie
+  - Token refresh via /auth/refresh
+  - Auto-logout on 401 responses
+end note
+
+@enduml

index 0000000..f1a0c00

+@startuml package-diagram
+title AI-CORP Package / Module Dependency Diagram
+'Architecture: Layered Architecture with Dependency Inversion
+'Enforces: No upward dependencies (outer layers depend on inner layers)
+
+skinparam defaultFontSize 11
+skinparam backgroundColor #FEFEFE
+skinparam package {
+  BackgroundColor #EBF5FB
+  BorderColor #2980B9
+}
+skinparam rectangle {
+  BackgroundColor #FDFEFE
+  BorderColor #7F8C8D
+}
+skinparam note {
+  BackgroundColor #FFFDE7
+  BorderColor #F9A825
+}
+skinparam ArrowColor #2C3E50
+
+' ============================================================
+' LAYER 1: DOMAIN (Core ΓÇö No Dependencies)
+' ============================================================
+package "Domain Layer\n(Core Business Logic)" as DOMAIN_LAYER #FEF9E7 {
+
+  package "agent (domain)" as PKG_AGENT_DOMAIN {
+    class "AgentEntity" as AgentEntity
+    class "AgentStateMachine" as AgentSM
+    class "AgentRole (enum)" as AgentRole
+    class "AgentState (enum)" as AgentState
+    interface "IAgentRepository" as IAgentRepo
+    interface "IAgentFactory" as IAgentFactory
+  }
+
+  package "workflow (domain)" as PKG_WF_DOMAIN {
+    class "WorkflowRunEntity" as WFRun
+    class "WorkflowNodeVO" as WFNode
+    class "WorkflowStateMachine" as WFSM
+    interface "IWorkflowRepository" as IWFRepo
+    interface "IWorkflowEngine" as IWFEngine
+  }
+
+  package "project (domain)" as PKG_PROJ_DOMAIN {
+    class "ProjectAggregateRoot" as ProjAgg
+    class "TaskEntity" as TaskEnt
+    class "MilestoneEntity" as MileEnt
+    class "TaskStateMachine" as TaskSM
+    interface "IProjectRepository" as IProjRepo
+    interface "ITaskRepository" as ITaskRepo
+  }
+
+  package "approval (domain)" as PKG_APPR_DOMAIN {
+    class "ApprovalEntity" as ApprEnt
+    class "ApprovalStateMachine" as ApprSM
+    interface "IApprovalRepository" as IApprRepo
+  }
+
+  package "shared (domain)" as PKG_SHARED {
+    class "DomainEvent" as DomEvent
+    class "AggregateRoot" as AggRoot
+    class "ValueObject" as VO
+    class "Entity" as EntityBase
+    class "Result<T>" as Result
+  }
+}
+
+' ============================================================
+' LAYER 2: APPLICATION (Uses Domain via Interfaces)
+' ============================================================
+package "Application Layer\n(Use Cases / Services)" as APP_LAYER #E8F8F5 {
+
+  package "agents (application)" as PKG_AGENT_APP {
+    class "AgentService" as AgentSvc
+    class "AgentRegistryService" as RegSvc
+    class "CEOAgent" as CEOAgent

+    class "QAAgent" as QAAgent
+    class "MarketingAgent" as MktgAgent
+    class "BAAgent" as BAAgent
+    class "ArchitectAgent" as ArchAgent
+    class "DevOpsAgent" as DevOpsAgent
+  }
+
+  package "workflow (application)" as PKG_WF_APP {
+    class "WorkflowEngineService" as WFEngSvc
+    class "WorkflowNodeExecutorService" as NodeExSvc
+    class "DAGValidatorService" as DAGVal
+    class "BullMQWorkerService" as BullWorker
+  }
+
+  package "projects (application)" as PKG_PROJ_APP {
+    class "ProjectService" as ProjSvc
+    class "TaskService" as TaskSvc
+    class "MilestoneService" as MileSvc
+  }
+
+  package "approvals (application)" as PKG_APPR_APP {
+    class "ApprovalService" as ApprSvc
+    class "ApprovalNotifierService" as ApprNotify
+  }
+
+  package "orchestrator (application)" as PKG_ORCH_APP {
+    class "OrchestratorService" as OrchSvc
+  }
+
+  package "cost (application)" as PKG_COST_APP {
+    class "CostTrackerService" as CostSvc
+    class "BudgetAlertService" as BudgetSvc
+  }
+
+  package "auth (application)" as PKG_AUTH_APP {
+    class "AuthService" as AuthSvc
+    class "JwtStrategy" as JwtStrat
+    class "LocalStrategy" as LocalStrat
+  }
+}

+' ============================================================
+' LAYER 3: INFRASTRUCTURE (Implements Domain Interfaces)

+package "Infrastructure Layer\n(Persistence / External)" as INFRA_LAYER #F4ECF7 {

+' ============================================================
+package "Presentation Layer\n(REST Controllers + WebSocket)" as PRES_LAYER #FDEDEC {

+  package "rest (presentation)" as PKG_REST {
+    class "AuthController" as AuthCtrl

+
+' ============================================================
+' DEPENDENCY ARROWS (Outer ΓåÆ Inner only)
+' ============================================================
+
+' Presentation ΓåÆ Application
+PKG_REST ..> PKG_AGENT_APP : uses
+PKG_REST ..> PKG_PROJ_APP : uses
+PKG_REST ..> PKG_WF_APP : uses
+PKG_REST ..> PKG_APPR_APP : uses
+PKG_REST ..> PKG_COST_APP : uses
+PKG_REST ..> PKG_AUTH_APP : uses
+PKG_WS ..> PKG_WF_APP : subscribes
+PKG_WS ..> PKG_AGENT_APP : observes
+
+' Presentation ΓåÆ Domain (DTOs use domain types)
+PKG_DTO ..> PKG_PROJ_DOMAIN : references
+
+' Application ΓåÆ Domain (via interfaces)
+PKG_AGENT_APP ..> PKG_AGENT_DOMAIN : uses
+PKG_WF_APP ..> PKG_WF_DOMAIN : uses
+PKG_PROJ_APP ..> PKG_PROJ_DOMAIN : uses
+PKG_APPR_APP ..> PKG_APPR_DOMAIN : uses
+PKG_ORCH_APP ..> PKG_AGENT_DOMAIN : uses
+PKG_ORCH_APP ..> PKG_WF_DOMAIN : uses
+PKG_AUTH_APP ..> PKG_AGENT_DOMAIN : uses
+PKG_COST_APP ..> PKG_PROJ_DOMAIN : uses
+
+' Application ΓåÆ Domain Interfaces (Dependency Inversion)
+PKG_AGENT_APP ..> IAgentRepo : depends on interface
+PKG_WF_APP ..> IWFRepo : depends on interface
+PKG_PROJ_APP ..> IProjRepo : depends on interface
+PKG_PROJ_APP ..> ITaskRepo : depends on interface
+PKG_APPR_APP ..> IApprRepo : depends on interface
+
+' Infrastructure ΓåÆ Domain (implements interfaces)
+PrismaAgentRepo ..|> IAgentRepo : implements
+PrismaProjRepo ..|> IProjRepo : implements
+PrismaWFRepo ..|> IWFRepo : implements
+PrismaApprRepo ..|> IApprRepo : implements
+OpenAIProv ..|> ILLMProv : implements
+AnthropicProv ..|> ILLMProv : implements
+FileTool ..|> ITool : implements
+WebTool ..|> ITool : implements
+
+' Infrastructure ΓåÆ Domain (reads domain entities)
+PKG_PERSIST ..> PKG_AGENT_DOMAIN : persists
+PKG_PERSIST ..> PKG_PROJ_DOMAIN : persists
+PKG_PERSIST ..> PKG_WF_DOMAIN : persists
+PKG_PERSIST ..> PKG_APPR_DOMAIN : persists
+
+' Application uses Infrastructure services (via interfaces)
+PKG_AGENT_APP ..> PKG_LLM : uses ILLMService
+PKG_AGENT_APP ..> PKG_MEM : uses IMemoryService
+PKG_AGENT_APP ..> PKG_TOOLS : uses IToolExecutor
+PKG_WF_APP ..> PKG_MSG : uses IQueueService
+PKG_ORCH_APP ..> PKG_LLM : uses ILLMService
+
+' Shared Domain types used everywhere
+PKG_AGENT_DOMAIN ..> PKG_SHARED : extends
+PKG_WF_DOMAIN ..> PKG_SHARED : extends
+PKG_PROJ_DOMAIN ..> PKG_SHARED : extends
+PKG_APPR_DOMAIN ..> PKG_SHARED : extends
+
+note top of DOMAIN_LAYER
+  <b>SOLID: Dependency Inversion Principle</b>
+  Domain layer defines interfaces.
+  Infrastructure IMPLEMENTS them.
+  Application DEPENDS ON interfaces only.
+  
+  Domain layer has ZERO external dependencies.
+  Pure TypeScript ΓÇö no framework imports.
+end note
+
+note bottom of INFRA_LAYER
+  <b>Pattern: Repository Pattern</b>
+  PrismaXxxRepository implements domain
+  repository interfaces, shielding application
+  from ORM/database specifics.
+  
+  <b>Pattern: Abstract Factory</b>
+  LLMFactory creates provider-specific clients
+  based on runtime configuration.
+end note
+
+note right of APP_LAYER
+  <b>Pattern: Facade</b>
+  Application services expose simple
+  facade interfaces to presentation layer.
+  
+  <b>Pattern: Strategy</b>
+  Agent classes implement common interface
+  with role-specific reasoning strategies.
+end note
+
+note left of PRES_LAYER
+  <b>Allowed dependencies:</b>
+  Presentation ΓåÆ Application ΓåÆ Domain
+  Infrastructure ΓåÆ Domain
+  
+  <b>Prohibited dependencies:</b>
+  Domain ΓåÆ Application (NO)
+  Domain ΓåÆ Infrastructure (NO)
+  Application ΓåÆ Presentation (NO)
+  Infrastructure ΓåÆ Application (NO)
+end note
+
+@enduml

+@startuml Agent Aggregate - Detailed Class Diagram
+!theme plain
+title Agent Aggregate - State Machine, Strategy & Observer Patterns
+scale 1.0
+
+' ========================================
+' AGENT AGGREGATE
+' ========================================
+
+package "Agent Aggregate" <<aggregate>> {
+  
+  interface IAgent <<interface>> {
+    + getId(): string
+    + getRole(): AgentRole
+    + getState(): AgentState
+    + execute(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentResult>
+    + addEventListener(observer: IAgentObserver): void
+    + removeEventListener(observer: IAgentObserver): void
+  }
+  
+  class Agent <<aggregate-root>> {
+    - id: string
+    - role: AgentRole
+    - name: string
+    - systemPrompt: string
+    - modelRouteConfig: ModelRouteConfig
+    - isActive: boolean
+    - createdAt: DateTime
+    - updatedAt: DateTime
+    - stateMachine: AgentStateMachine
+    - eventEmitter: AgentEventEmitter
+    --
+    **Identity**
+    + getId(): string
+    + getRole(): AgentRole
+    + getName(): string
+    --
+    **State Management**
+    + getState(): AgentState
+    + transitionState(newState: AgentState, reason?: string): void
+    + canTransitionTo(state: AgentState): boolean
+    + getStateHistory(): StateTransition[]
+    --
+    **Execution**
+    + execute(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentResult>
+    + think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    + act(thought: AgentThought): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+    --
+    **Observer Management**
+    + addEventListener(observer: IAgentObserver): void
+    + removeEventListener(observer: IAgentObserver): void
+    + notifyObservers(event: AgentEvent): void
+    --
+    **Configuration**
+    + updateSystemPrompt(prompt: string): void
+    + updateModelConfig(config: ModelRouteConfig): void
+    + activate(): void
+    + deactivate(): void
+    --
+    **Validation**
+    + validate(): boolean
+    + isActive(): boolean
+    --
+    **Domain Events Emitted**
+    ~ emitStateChanged(): void
+    ~ emitThinkingStarted(): void
+    ~ emitThinkingCompleted(): void
+    ~ emitActionStarted(): void
+    ~ emitActionCompleted(): void
+  }
+  
+  abstract class BaseAgent {
+    # role: AgentRole
+    # systemPrompt: string
+    # modelConfig: ModelRouteConfig
+    # memoryService: MemoryService
+    # llmFactory: LLMProviderFactory
+    # toolRegistry: ToolRegistry
+    # sandboxExecutor: SandboxExecutor
+    # apiUsageService: ApiUsageService
+    # websocketGateway: AppWebSocketGateway
+    # logger: Logger
+    --
+    **Template Method Pattern**
+    + execute(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentResult>
+    # {abstract} think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    # {abstract} act(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+    --
+    **LLM Integration**
+    # callLLM(params: ChatParams, projectId?: string): Promise<ChatResult>
+    --
+    **Tool Execution**
+    # executeTool(toolName: string, command: string, params: object, limits?: ResourceLimits): Promise<ToolResult>
+    # executeSandboxCommand(command: string, limits?: ResourceLimits): Promise<ToolResult>
+    --
+    **Memory Management**
+    # loadMemory(namespace: string, query: string, limit?: number): Promise<MemorySearchResult[]>
+    # saveMemory(namespace: string, content: string, importance?: number, projectId?: string): Promise<string>
+    --
+    **Event Emission**
+    # emitThinking(projectId: string, thought: string): void
+    # emitAction(projectId: string, taskId: string, actionType: string, toolName: string, params?: any): void
+    # emitMessage(projectId: string, message: string, toAgent?: AgentRole): void
+  }
+  
+  class CEOAgent {
+    + think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    + act(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+    - generateStrategicPlan(context: AgentContext): string
+    - delegateToAgents(task: string): AgentAction[]
+  }
+  
+  class PMAgent {
+    + think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    + act(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+    - decomposeTask(task: string): string[]
+    - createTaskBreakdown(subtasks: string[]): AgentAction[]
+  }
+  
+  class DevAgent {
+    + think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    + act(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+    - analyzeCodeRequirements(task: string): object
+    - implementCode(spec: object): AgentAction[]
+  }
+  
+  class QAAgent {
+    + think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    + act(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+    - generateTestScenarios(task: string): string[]
+    - executeTests(scenarios: string[]): AgentAction[]
+  }
+  
+  class MarketingAgent {
+    + think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    + act(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+    - analyzeTargetAudience(task: string): object
+    - createCampaignPlan(analysis: object): AgentAction[]
+  }
+  
+  class BAAgent {
+    + think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    + act(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+    - gatherRequirements(task: string): Requirement[]
+    - documentSpecifications(requirements: Requirement[]): AgentAction[]
+  }
+  
+  class ArchitectAgent {
+    + think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    + act(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+    - designArchitecture(requirements: Requirement[]): ArchitectureDesign
+    - selectDesignPatterns(design: ArchitectureDesign): Pattern[]
+  }
+  
+  class DevOpsAgent {
+    + think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    + act(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+    - planDeployment(environment: string): DeploymentPlan
+    - executeDeployment(plan: DeploymentPlan): AgentAction[]
+  }
+  
+  class AgentStateMachine {
+    - currentState: AgentState
+    - history: StateTransition[]
+    - transitions: Map<AgentState, AgentState[]>
+    --
+    **State Queries**
+    + getCurrentState(): AgentState
+    + getPreviousState(): AgentState | null
+    + getValidTransitions(): AgentState[]
+    + getHistory(): StateTransition[]
+    + getTransitionCount(): number
+    --
+    **State Transitions**
+    + canTransitionTo(targetState: AgentState): boolean
+    + transitionTo(targetState: AgentState, reason?: string): void
+    --
+    **Utilities**
+    + getSummary(): object
+    + clearHistory(): void
+    + reset(): void
+  }
+  
+  class AgentEventEmitter {
+    - observers: Set<IAgentObserver>
+    --
+    **Observer Management**
+    + addObserver(observer: IAgentObserver): this
+    + removeObserver(observer: IAgentObserver): this
+    + removeAllObservers(): void
+    + getObserverCount(): number
+    + hasObserver(observer: IAgentObserver): boolean
+    --
+    **Event Emission**
+    + emitStateChanged(event: StateChangedEvent): void
+    + emitThinkingStarted(event: ThinkingEvent): void
+    + emitThinkingCompleted(event: ThinkingCompletedEvent): void
+    + emitActionStarted(event: ActionStartedEvent): void
+    + emitActionCompleted(event: ActionEvent): void
+    + emitApprovalRequested(event: ApprovalRequestedEvent): void
+    + emitApprovalReceived(event: ApprovalReceivedEvent): void
+    + emitError(event: ErrorEvent): void
+  }
+  
+  enum AgentState <<value-object>> {
+    IDLE
+    THINKING
+    ACTING
+    WAITING_APPROVAL
+    ERROR
+    COMPLETED
+    --
+    + isTerminal(): boolean
+    + canTransitionTo(target: AgentState): boolean
+  }
+  
+  enum AgentRole <<value-object>> {
+    CEO
+    PM
+    DEV
+    QA
+    MARKETING
+    BA
+    ARCHITECT
+    DEVOPS
+    --
+    + getDisplayName(): string
+    + getDescription(): string
+  }
+  
+  class ModelRouteConfig <<value-object>> {
+    + provider: string
+    + model: string
+    + fallbackProvider: string | null
+    + tags: string[]
+    --
+    + equals(other: ModelRouteConfig): boolean
+    + validate(): boolean
+    + toJSON(): object
+  }
+  
+  class AgentMemory <<entity>> {
+    - id: string
+    - agentId: string
+    - agentRole: AgentRole
+    - namespace: string
+    - content: string
+    - embedding: float[] | null
+    - importance: number
+    - metadata: JSON
+    - createdAt: DateTime
+    --
+    **Embedding**
+    + generateEmbedding(): Promise<float[]>
+    + hasEmbedding(): boolean
+    --
+    **Relevance**
+    + calculateRelevance(query: string): number
+    + calculateSimilarity(other: AgentMemory): number
+    --
+    **Validation**
+    + validate(): boolean
+    + validateImportance(): boolean
+  }
+  
+  class AgentMessage <<entity>> {
+    - id: string
+    - projectId: string
+    - fromAgentId: string
+    - fromRole: AgentRole
+    - toRole: AgentRole | null
+    - message: string
+    - messageType: MessageType
+    - metadata: JSON
+    - createdAt: DateTime
+    --
+    **Queries**
+    + isBroadcast(): boolean
+    + isDirectMessage(): boolean
+    + isFromAgent(role: AgentRole): boolean
+    + isToAgent(role: AgentRole): boolean
+    --
+    **Validation**
+    + validate(): boolean
+  }
+}
+
+' ========================================
+' STRATEGY PATTERN
+' ========================================
+
+package "Strategy Pattern" <<pattern>> {
+  
+  interface IThinkingStrategy <<interface>> {
+    + think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+  }
+  
+  interface IActionStrategy <<interface>> {
+    + act(context: AgentContext, thought: AgentThought): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+  }
+  
+  abstract class BaseThinkingStrategy {
+    # {abstract} generateThought(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    + think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    # validateContext(context: AgentContext): void
+    # createAction(toolName: string, params: object, rationale: string): AgentAction
+    # getMemoriesByNamespace(context: AgentContext, namespace: string): string[]
+  }
+  
+  class CEOThinkingStrategy {
+    + think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    - generateStrategicReasoning(task: string, projectContext: object, memories: any[]): string
+    - planStrategicActions(task: string, context: AgentContext): AgentAction[]
+    - assessConfidence(context: AgentContext): number
+    - identifyRisks(task: string): string[]
+  }
+  
+  class PMThinkingStrategy {
+    + think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    - decomposeTask(task: string): string[]
+    - generatePlanningReasoning(task: string, subtasks: string[], context: AgentContext): string
+    - planSubtaskActions(subtasks: string[], context: AgentContext): AgentAction[]
+    - determineTargetAgent(subtask: string): AgentRole
+    - determinePriority(task: string): Priority
+    - identifyDependencies(subtasks: string[]): string[]
+  }
+  
+  class DEVThinkingStrategy {
+    + think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    - generateTechnicalReasoning(task: string, memories: any[]): string
+    - planImplementationActions(task: string, tools: any[], context: AgentContext): AgentAction[]
+    - inferFilePath(task: string): string
+    - assessTechnicalConfidence(context: AgentContext): number
+    - getQualityChecklist(): string[]
+  }
+  
+  class QAThinkingStrategy {
+    + think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    - generateTestScenarios(task: string): string[]
+    - generateTestingReasoning(task: string, scenarios: string[]): string
+    - planTestingActions(scenarios: string[], context: AgentContext): AgentAction[]
+    - identifyCoverageAreas(task: string): string[]
+  }
+  
+  class MarketingThinkingStrategy {
+    + think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    - generateMarketingReasoning(task: string, projectContext: object): string
+    - planMarketingActions(task: string, context: AgentContext): AgentAction[]
+    - identifyTargetAudience(task: string, projectContext: object): string
+    - defineMetrics(task: string): string[]
+    - selectChannels(task: string): string[]
+  }
+}
+
+' ========================================
+' OBSERVER PATTERN
+' ========================================
+
+package "Observer Pattern" <<pattern>> {
+  
+  interface IAgentObserver <<interface>> {
+    + onStateChanged?(event: StateChangedEvent): void
+    + onThinkingStarted?(event: ThinkingEvent): void
+    + onThinkingCompleted?(event: ThinkingCompletedEvent): void
+    + onActionStarted?(event: ActionStartedEvent): void
+    + onActionCompleted?(event: ActionEvent): void
+    + onApprovalRequested?(event: ApprovalRequestedEvent): void
+    + onApprovalReceived?(event: ApprovalReceivedEvent): void
+    + onError?(event: ErrorEvent): void
+  }
+  
+  class WebSocketGateway {
+    + onStateChanged(event: StateChangedEvent): void
+    + onThinkingStarted(event: ThinkingEvent): void
+    + onActionCompleted(event: ActionEvent): void
+    - broadcast(message: object): void
+    - sendToProject(projectId: string, message: object): void
+  }
+  
+  class WorkflowEngine {
+    + onActionCompleted(event: ActionEvent): void
+    + onError(event: ErrorEvent): void
+    - executeNextStep(taskId: string): void
+    - failWorkflow(taskId: string, error: Error): void
+  }
+  
+  class AuditLogger {
+    + onStateChanged(event: StateChangedEvent): void
+    + onActionCompleted(event: ActionEvent): void
+    + onError(event: ErrorEvent): void
+    - logEvent(event: AgentEvent): void
+  }
+  
+  class MetricsCollector {
+    + onActionCompleted(event: ActionEvent): void
+    - recordMetric(metric: Metric): void
+  }
+}
+
+' ========================================
+' RELATIONSHIPS
+' ========================================
+
+' Interface implementation
+IAgent <|.. Agent : implements
+IAgent <|.. BaseAgent : implements
+
+' Inheritance hierarchy
+BaseAgent <|-- CEOAgent : extends
+BaseAgent <|-- PMAgent : extends
+BaseAgent <|-- DevAgent : extends
+BaseAgent <|-- QAAgent : extends
+BaseAgent <|-- MarketingAgent : extends
+BaseAgent <|-- BAAgent : extends
+BaseAgent <|-- ArchitectAgent : extends
+BaseAgent <|-- DevOpsAgent : extends
+
+' Agent composition
+Agent *-- AgentStateMachine : contains
+Agent *-- AgentEventEmitter : contains
+Agent "1" *-- "0..*" AgentMemory : contains
+Agent "1" *-- "0..*" AgentMessage : sends
+
+' Value object associations
+Agent --> AgentRole : has
+Agent --> AgentState : has
+Agent --> ModelRouteConfig : configured with
+AgentMemory --> AgentRole : belongs to
+
+' Strategy pattern
+IThinkingStrategy <|.. BaseThinkingStrategy : implements
+BaseThinkingStrategy <|-- CEOThinkingStrategy : extends
+BaseThinkingStrategy <|-- PMThinkingStrategy : extends
+BaseThinkingStrategy <|-- DEVThinkingStrategy : extends
+BaseThinkingStrategy <|-- QAThinkingStrategy : extends
+BaseThinkingStrategy <|-- MarketingThinkingStrategy : extends
+
+BaseAgent ..> IThinkingStrategy : uses
+BaseAgent ..> IActionStrategy : uses
+
+' Observer pattern
+AgentEventEmitter o-- "0..*" IAgentObserver : notifies
+IAgentObserver <|.. WebSocketGateway : implements
+IAgentObserver <|.. WorkflowEngine : implements
+IAgentObserver <|.. AuditLogger : implements
+IAgentObserver <|.. MetricsCollector : implements
+
+' ========================================
+' BUSINESS RULES
+' ========================================
+
+note bottom of Agent
+  **Aggregate Root Invariants**:
+  
+  1. Agent role must be unique (Req 17.1)
+  2. Agent can only be in one state at a time
+  3. State transitions must follow state machine rules
+  4. Only active agents can execute tasks (Rule 5)
+  5. Agent must have valid system prompt and model config
+  6. Model config must specify valid provider and model
+  
+  **Consistency Boundary**:
+  - All memories belong to exactly one agent
+  - All messages sent by one agent
+  - State transitions recorded in history
+end note
+
+note bottom of AgentStateMachine
+  **Valid State Transitions**:
+  
+  IDLE ΓåÆ THINKING
+  THINKING ΓåÆ ACTING | ERROR
+  ACTING ΓåÆ COMPLETED | WAITING_APPROVAL | ERROR
+  WAITING_APPROVAL ΓåÆ ACTING | COMPLETED | ERROR
+  ERROR ΓåÆ IDLE (recovery)
+  COMPLETED ΓåÆ IDLE (reuse)
+  
+  **Invariants**:
+  - Cannot transition to same state
+  - All transitions validated before execution
+  - History records all transitions with timestamps
+end note
+
+note bottom of AgentMemory
+  **Entity Invariants** (Req 17.3):
+  
+  1. Memory must belong to valid agent
+  2. Importance must be between 1 and 10 inclusive
+  3. Namespace must be non-empty
+  4. Content must be non-empty
+  5. Embedding is optional (generated on demand)
+  6. Importance determines retrieval priority
+  
+  **Vector Search**:
+  - Embedding stored as JSON array of floats
+  - Semantic search uses cosine similarity
+  - HNSW index for performance (pgvector)
+end note
+
+note top of BaseAgent
+  **Template Method Pattern**:
+  
+  execute() defines skeleton:
+  1. Transition to THINKING state
+  2. Call think() (abstract, role-specific)
+  3. Transition to ACTING state
+  4. Call act() (abstract, role-specific)
+  5. Transition to COMPLETED or ERROR
+  
+  Subclasses implement think() and act()
+  with role-specific behavior.
+end note
+
+note right of IThinkingStrategy
+  **Strategy Pattern Benefits**:
+  
+  - Runtime behavior swapping
+  - Multiple strategies per role possible
+  - Easy to test in isolation
+  - Follows Open/Closed Principle
+  - New strategies without modifying agents
+end note
+
+note right of IAgentObserver
+  **Observer Pattern Benefits**:
+  
+  - Decoupled event handling
+  - Multiple observers can subscribe
+  - Observers don't affect agent behavior
+  - Easy to add new observers
+  - Error in one observer doesn't affect others
+  
+  **All methods optional** (ISP):
+  Observers implement only needed methods.
+end note
+
+@enduml

+package "Agent Domain" <<domain>> {
+  
+  interface IAgent <<interface>> {
+    + getId(): string
+    + getRole(): AgentRole
+    + getState(): AgentState
+    + execute(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentResult>
+    + addEventListener(observer: IAgentObserver): void
+    + removeEventListener(observer: IAgentObserver): void
+  }
+  
+  interface IAgentObserver <<interface>> {
+    + onStateChanged?(event: StateChangedEvent): void
+    + onThinkingStarted?(event: ThinkingEvent): void
+    + onThinkingCompleted?(event: ThinkingCompletedEvent): void
+    + onActionStarted?(event: ActionStartedEvent): void
+    + onActionCompleted?(event: ActionEvent): void
+    + onApprovalRequested?(event: ApprovalRequestedEvent): void
+    + onApprovalReceived?(event: ApprovalReceivedEvent): void
+    + onError?(event: ErrorEvent): void
+  }
+  
+  interface IThinkingStrategy <<interface>> {
+    + think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+  }
+  
+  interface IActionStrategy <<interface>> {
+    + act(context: AgentContext, thought: AgentThought): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+  }
+  
+  class Agent <<aggregate-root>> {
+    - id: string
+    - role: AgentRole
+    - name: string
+    - systemPrompt: string
+    - modelRouteConfig: ModelRouteConfig
+    - isActive: boolean
+    - createdAt: DateTime
+    - updatedAt: DateTime
+    - stateMachine: AgentStateMachine
+    - eventEmitter: AgentEventEmitter
+    --
+    + getId(): string
+    + getRole(): AgentRole
+    + getState(): AgentState
+    + execute(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentResult>
+    + addEventListener(observer: IAgentObserver): void
+    + removeEventListener(observer: IAgentObserver): void
+    + transitionState(newState: AgentState): void
+  }
+  
+  abstract class BaseAgent {
+    # role: AgentRole
+    # systemPrompt: string
+    # modelConfig: ModelRouteConfig
+    # memoryService: MemoryService
+    # llmFactory: LLMProviderFactory
+    # toolRegistry: ToolRegistry
+    # sandboxExecutor: SandboxExecutor
+    --
+    + {abstract} think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    + {abstract} act(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+    + execute(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentResult>
+    # callLLM(params: ChatParams): Promise<ChatResult>
+    # executeTool(toolName: string, command: string, params: object): Promise<ToolResult>
+    # loadMemory(namespace: string, query: string): Promise<MemorySearchResult[]>
+    # saveMemory(namespace: string, content: string): Promise<string>
+    # emitThinking(projectId: string, thought: string): void
+    # emitAction(projectId: string, taskId: string, actionType: string, toolName: string): void
+  }
+

+    + act(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+  }

+    + emitActionStarted(event: ActionStartedEvent): void

+  }

+' ========================================
+' RELATIONSHIPS
+' ========================================
+
+' Agent relationships
+IAgent <|.. Agent : implements
+IAgent <|.. BaseAgent : implements
+BaseAgent <|-- CEOAgent : extends
+BaseAgent <|-- PMAgent : extends
+BaseAgent <|-- DevAgent : extends
+BaseAgent <|-- QAAgent : extends
+BaseAgent <|-- MarketingAgent : extends
+BaseAgent <|-- BAAgent : extends
+BaseAgent <|-- ArchitectAgent : extends
+BaseAgent <|-- DevOpsAgent : extends
+
+Agent *-- AgentStateMachine : contains
+Agent *-- AgentEventEmitter : contains
+Agent --> AgentRole : has
+Agent --> AgentState : has
+Agent --> ModelRouteConfig : configured with
+
+Agent "1" *-- "0..*" AgentMemory : contains
+Agent "1" *-- "0..*" AgentMessage : sends
+
+AgentEventEmitter o-- "0..*" IAgentObserver : notifies
+
+IThinkingStrategy <|.. CEOThinkingStrategy : implements
+IThinkingStrategy <|.. PMThinkingStrategy : implements
+IThinkingStrategy <|.. DEVThinkingStrategy : implements
+IThinkingStrategy <|.. QAThinkingStrategy : implements
+IThinkingStrategy <|.. MarketingThinkingStrategy : implements
+
+BaseAgent ..> IThinkingStrategy : uses
+BaseAgent ..> IActionStrategy : uses
+
+' Project relationships
+Project "1" *-- "0..*" Milestone : contains
+Project "1" *-- "0..*" Task : contains
+Milestone "1" o-- "0..*" Task : groups
+Project --> ProjectStatus : has
+Task --> TaskStatus : has
+Task --> Priority : has
+Task --> AgentRole : assigned to
+Project --> Money : has budget
+Task --> Money : has cost
+
+User "1" --> "0..*" Project : creates
+
+' Workflow relationships
+WorkflowRun "1" *-- "1..*" WorkflowStep : contains
+WorkflowRun "1" *-- "0..*" Approval : requests
+WorkflowRun --> WorkflowStatus : has
+WorkflowStep --> StepStatus : has
+Approval --> ApprovalStatus : has
+WorkflowRun --> Project : executes for
+WorkflowStep --> Task : executes
+Approval --> User : requests from
+
+' Tool relationships
+ITool <|.. WriteFileTool : implements
+ITool <|.. ReadFileTool : implements
+ITool <|.. RunTestsTool : implements
+IToolExecutor <|.. SandboxExecutor : implements
+ToolRegistry o-- "0..*" ITool : registers
+BaseAgent ..> ToolRegistry : uses
+BaseAgent ..> SandboxExecutor : uses
+
+' API Usage relationships
+ApiUsageLog --> Project : tracks for
+ApiUsageLog --> AgentRole : tracks
+ApiUsageLog --> Money : records cost
+
+' New enterprise feature relationships
+BARequirementSpec --> Project : documents
+ArchitectureDocument --> Project : designs
+DeploymentJob --> Project : deploys
+BAAgent ..> BARequirementSpec : creates
+ArchitectAgent ..> ArchitectureDocument : creates
+DevOpsAgent ..> DeploymentJob : triggers
+
+note top of Agent
+  **Aggregate Root**: Agent
+  
+  **Invariants**:
+  - Role is unique across all agents
+  - Can only be in one state at a time
+  - State transitions must be valid
+  
+  **Patterns**: Strategy, State, Observer
+end note
+
+note top of Project
+  **Aggregate Root**: Project
+  
+  **Invariants**:
+  - Budget must be positive when set
+  - Cost accrued <= budget
+  - Cannot delete with active workflows
+  
+  **Consistency Boundary**: Project + Milestones + Tasks
+end note
+
+note top of WorkflowRun
+  **Aggregate Root**: WorkflowRun
+  
+  **Invariants**:
+  - Status transitions must be valid
+  - Cannot modify completed/failed workflows
+  - Approval must resolve before proceeding
+  
+  **Consistency Boundary**: WorkflowRun + Steps + Approvals
+end note
+
+@enduml

+@startuml Factory Pattern - AI-CORP
+!theme plain
+title Design Patterns - Factory Method & Abstract Factory
+scale 1.0
+
+' ========================================
+' FACTORY METHOD PATTERN
+' ========================================
+
+package "Factory Method Pattern" <<Factory Method>> {
+  
+  note as N1
+    **Factory Method Pattern**
+    
+    **Intent**: Define an interface for creating objects,
+    but let subclasses decide which class to instantiate.
+    
+    **Benefits**:
+    - Eliminates need to bind application-specific classes
+    - Provides hooks for subclasses
+    - Connects parallel class hierarchies
+    
+    **Structure**:
+    - Creator: AgentFactory (abstract)
+    - ConcreteCreator: Specific agent factories
+    - Product: IAgent interface
+    - ConcreteProduct: CEOAgent, DevAgent, etc.
+  end note
+  
+  abstract class AgentFactory <<Factory Method>> {
+    # memoryService: MemoryService
+    # llmFactory: LLMProviderFactory
+    # toolRegistry: ToolRegistry
+    # sandboxExecutor: SandboxExecutor
+    # apiUsageService: ApiUsageService
+    # websocketGateway: AppWebSocketGateway
+    --
+    **Factory Method (abstract)**
+    + {abstract} createAgent(role: AgentRole): IAgent
+    + {abstract} getSystemPrompt(role: AgentRole): string
+    + {abstract} getModelConfig(role: AgentRole): ModelRouteConfig
+    --
+    **Template Method**
+    + createAgentWithConfig(role: AgentRole, config: AgentConfig): IAgent
+    --
+    **Utility Methods**
+    # loadAgentConfig(role: AgentRole): AgentConfig
+    # validateConfig(config: AgentConfig): boolean
+  }
+  
+  class ConcreteAgentFactory {
+    **Factory Method Implementation**
+    + createAgent(role: AgentRole): IAgent
+    + getSystemPrompt(role: AgentRole): string
+    + getModelConfig(role: AgentRole): ModelRouteConfig
+    --
+    **Role-specific Creation**
+    - createCEOAgent(): CEOAgent
+    - createPMAgent(): PMAgent
+    - createDevAgent(): DevAgent
+    - createQAAgent(): QAAgent
+    - createMarketingAgent(): MarketingAgent
+    - createBAAgent(): BAAgent
+    - createArchitectAgent(): ArchitectAgent
+    - createDevOpsAgent(): DevOpsAgent
+  }
+  
+  interface IAgent <<Product>> {
+    + getId(): string
+    + getRole(): AgentRole
+    + execute(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentResult>
+  }
+  
+  abstract class BaseAgent <<ConcreteProduct>> {
+    + execute(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentResult>
+    # {abstract} think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    # {abstract} act(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+  }
+  
+  class CEOAgent <<ConcreteProduct>> {
+    + think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    + act(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+  }
+  
+  class DevAgent <<ConcreteProduct>> {
+    + think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    + act(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+  }
+  
+  class QAAgent <<ConcreteProduct>> {
+    + think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    + act(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+  }
+  
+  ' Relationships
+  AgentFactory <|-- ConcreteAgentFactory : extends
+  AgentFactory ..> IAgent : creates
+  IAgent <|.. BaseAgent : implements
+  BaseAgent <|-- CEOAgent : extends
+  BaseAgent <|-- DevAgent : extends
+  BaseAgent <|-- QAAgent : extends
+  ConcreteAgentFactory ..> CEOAgent : instantiates
+  ConcreteAgentFactory ..> DevAgent : instantiates
+  ConcreteAgentFactory ..> QAAgent : instantiates
+}
+
+' ========================================
+' ABSTRACT FACTORY PATTERN
+' ========================================
+
+package "Abstract Factory Pattern" <<Abstract Factory>> {
+  
+  note as N2
+    **Abstract Factory Pattern**
+    
+    **Intent**: Provide an interface for creating families
+    of related or dependent objects without specifying
+    their concrete classes.
+    
+    **Benefits**:
+    - Isolates concrete classes
+    - Makes exchanging product families easy
+    - Promotes consistency among products
+    
+    **Structure**:
+    - AbstractFactory: ILLMProviderFactory
+    - ConcreteFactory: NineRouterFactory, AnthropicFactory
+    - AbstractProduct: ILLMProvider
+    - ConcreteProduct: NineRouterProvider, AnthropicProvider
+  end note
+  
+  interface ILLMProviderFactory <<AbstractFactory>> {
+    + createProvider(config: ModelRouteConfig): ILLMProvider
+    + supportsModel(model: string): boolean
+    + getFallbackProvider(): ILLMProvider
+  }
+  
+  class LLMProviderFactory <<ConcreteFactory>> {
+    - providers: Map<string, ILLMProvider>
+    - fallbackProviders: Map<string, ILLMProvider>
+    --
+    + createProvider(config: ModelRouteConfig): ILLMProvider
+    + supportsModel(model: string): boolean
+    + getFallbackProvider(): ILLMProvider
+    + getProvider(config: ModelRouteConfig): ILLMProvider
+    --
+    **Registration**
+    + registerProvider(name: string, provider: ILLMProvider): void
+    + registerFallback(name: string, provider: ILLMProvider): void
+    --
+    **Circuit Breaker Integration**
+    - checkCircuitBreaker(providerName: string): boolean
+    - triggerFallback(config: ModelRouteConfig): ILLMProvider
+  }
+  
+  interface ILLMProvider <<AbstractProduct>> {
+    + chat(params: ChatParams): Promise<ChatResult>
+    + getProviderName(): string
+    + supportsModel(model: string): boolean
+    + healthCheck(): Promise<boolean>
+  }
+  
+  class NineRouterProvider <<ConcreteProduct>> {
+    - apiKey: string
+    - baseUrl: string
+    - circuitBreaker: CircuitBreaker
+    --
+    + chat(params: ChatParams): Promise<ChatResult>
+    + getProviderName(): string
+    + supportsModel(model: string): boolean
+    + healthCheck(): Promise<boolean>
+    --
+    **9Router Specific**
+    - handleRTKCompression(params: ChatParams): ChatParams
+    - calculateTokenSaved(original: number, compressed: number): number
+  }
+  
+  class AnthropicProvider <<ConcreteProduct>> {
+    - apiKey: string
+    - baseUrl: string
+    --
+    + chat(params: ChatParams): Promise<ChatResult>
+    + getProviderName(): string
+    + supportsModel(model: string): boolean
+    + healthCheck(): Promise<boolean>
+    --
+    **Anthropic Specific**
+    - formatMessages(messages: Message[]): object
+    - parseResponse(response: object): ChatResult
+  }
+  
+  class OpenAIProvider <<ConcreteProduct>> {
+    - apiKey: string
+    - baseUrl: string
+    --
+    + chat(params: ChatParams): Promise<ChatResult>
+    + getProviderName(): string
+    + supportsModel(model: string): boolean
+    + healthCheck(): Promise<boolean>
+    --
+    **OpenAI Specific**
+    - handleFunctionCalling(params: ChatParams): ChatParams
+    - parseToolCalls(response: object): ToolCall[]
+  }
+  
+  ' Relationships
+  ILLMProviderFactory <|.. LLMProviderFactory : implements
+  LLMProviderFactory ..> ILLMProvider : creates
+  ILLMProvider <|.. NineRouterProvider : implements
+  ILLMProvider <|.. AnthropicProvider : implements
+  ILLMProvider <|.. OpenAIProvider : implements
+}
+
+' ========================================
+' FACTORY USAGE EXAMPLE
+' ========================================
+
+package "Client Code" <<client>> {
+  
+  class AgentService {
+    - agentFactory: AgentFactory
+    - llmFactory: LLMProviderFactory
+    --
+    + createAndExecuteAgent(role: AgentRole, context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentResult>
+    + getAgent(role: AgentRole): IAgent
+  }
+  
+  ' Usage relationships
+  AgentService --> AgentFactory : uses
+  AgentService --> IAgent : receives
+  AgentService --> LLMProviderFactory : uses
+  AgentService --> ILLMProvider : receives
+}
+
+note bottom of AgentService
+  **Client Code Example**:
+  
+  ```typescript
+  // Factory Method usage
+  const agentFactory = new ConcreteAgentFactory(dependencies);
+  const devAgent: IAgent = agentFactory.createAgent(AgentRole.DEV);
+  const result = await devAgent.execute(context);
+  
+  // Abstract Factory usage
+  const llmFactory = new LLMProviderFactory();
+  const provider: ILLMProvider = llmFactory.getProvider({
+    provider: 'ninerouter',
+    model: 'claude-3-sonnet'
+  });
+  const chatResult = await provider.chat(chatParams);
+  
+  // Fallback handling
+  if (provider.healthCheck() === false) {
+    const fallback = llmFactory.getFallbackProvider();
+    const chatResult = await fallback.chat(chatParams);
+  }
+  ```
+  
+  **Benefits in AI-CORP**:
+  - Easy to add new agent types
+  - Easy to swap LLM providers
+  - Centralized configuration
+  - Fallback provider management
+  - Circuit breaker integration
+end note
+
+' ========================================
+' PARALLEL CLASS HIERARCHIES
+' ========================================
+
+note right of ConcreteAgentFactory
+  **Parallel Hierarchies**:
+  
+  Creator Hierarchy:
+  - AgentFactory (abstract)
+  - ConcreteAgentFactory
+  
+  Product Hierarchy:
+  - IAgent (interface)
+  - BaseAgent (abstract)
+  - CEOAgent, DevAgent, etc. (concrete)
+  
+  Factory Method connects these hierarchies:
+  - Factory creates products
+  - Products implement common interface
+  - Easy to extend both hierarchies
+end note
+
+note right of LLMProviderFactory
+  **Family of Related Products**:
+  
+  LLM Provider Family:
+  - NineRouterProvider (primary with RTK)
+  - AnthropicProvider (fallback)
+  - OpenAIProvider (fallback)
+  
+  All providers:
+  - Implement ILLMProvider interface
+  - Support chat() method
+  - Return ChatResult
+  - Can be swapped at runtime
+  
+  Factory ensures:
+  - Consistent interface
+  - Proper configuration
+  - Fallback handling
+  - Circuit breaker integration
+end note
+
+' ========================================
+' BENEFITS SUMMARY
+' ========================================
+
+note top of AgentFactory
+  **Factory Method Benefits in AI-CORP**:
+  
+  1. **Decoupling**: Client code doesn't know concrete agent classes
+  2. **Extensibility**: Add new agents without modifying existing code
+  3. **Configuration**: Centralized agent configuration
+  4. **Testing**: Easy to mock/stub factories for testing
+  5. **Consistency**: All agents created with proper dependencies
+end note
+
+note top of ILLMProviderFactory
+  **Abstract Factory Benefits in AI-CORP**:
+  
+  1. **Provider Independence**: Switch providers without code changes
+  2. **Fallback Management**: Automatic fallback on provider failure
+  3. **Circuit Breaker**: Integrated health checking and failover
+  4. **Consistency**: All providers implement same interface
+  5. **Cost Optimization**: Route to cheapest provider with same capabilities
+end note
+
+@enduml

+@startuml Observer Pattern - AI-CORP
+!theme plain
+title Design Pattern - Observer Pattern (Agent Event System)
+scale 1.0
+
+' ========================================
+' OBSERVER PATTERN OVERVIEW
+' ========================================
+
+note as N1
+  **Observer Pattern (Gang of Four)**
+  
+  **Intent**: Define a one-to-many dependency between objects
+  so that when one object changes state, all its dependents
+  are notified and updated automatically.
+  
+  **Benefits**:
+  - Decouples subject from observers
+  - Supports broadcast communication
+  - Dynamic relationships (add/remove observers at runtime)
+  - Loose coupling between sender and receivers
+  
+  **Structure**:
+  - Subject: AgentEventEmitter
+  - Observer: IAgentObserver interface
+  - ConcreteObservers: WebSocketGateway, WorkflowEngine, etc.
+  
+  **Applied in AI-CORP**:
+  - Agents emit lifecycle events
+  - Multiple subsystems listen to events
+  - Decoupled notification system
+end note
+
+' ========================================
+' SUBJECT (AGENT EVENT EMITTER)
+' ========================================
+
+package "Subject" <<Subject>> {
+  
+  class AgentEventEmitter <<Subject>> {
+    - observers: Set<IAgentObserver>
+    --
+    **Observer Management**
+    + addObserver(observer: IAgentObserver): this
+    + removeObserver(observer: IAgentObserver): this
+    + removeAllObservers(): void
+    + getObserverCount(): number
+    + hasObserver(observer: IAgentObserver): boolean
+    --
+    **Event Emission (Notify)**
+    + emitStateChanged(event: StateChangedEvent): void
+    + emitThinkingStarted(event: ThinkingEvent): void
+    + emitThinkingCompleted(event: ThinkingCompletedEvent): void
+    + emitActionStarted(event: ActionStartedEvent): void
+    + emitActionCompleted(event: ActionEvent): void
+    + emitApprovalRequested(event: ApprovalRequestedEvent): void
+    + emitApprovalReceived(event: ApprovalReceivedEvent): void
+    + emitError(event: ErrorEvent): void
+    --
+    **Error Handling**
+    - handleObserverError(observer: IAgentObserver, error: Error): void
+  }
+  
+  class Agent {
+    - id: string
+    - role: AgentRole
+    - eventEmitter: AgentEventEmitter
+    --
+    **Subject Interface**
+    + addEventListener(observer: IAgentObserver): void
+    + removeEventListener(observer: IAgentObserver): void
+    --
+    **State Changes Trigger Events**
+    + transitionState(newState: AgentState): void
+    + execute(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentResult>
+    --
+    **Internal Event Emission**
+    - notifyStateChanged(previous: AgentState, current: AgentState): void
+    - notifyThinkingStarted(taskId: string): void
+    - notifyActionCompleted(action: AgentAction): void
+  }
+  
+  Agent *-- AgentEventEmitter : contains
+}
+
+' ========================================
+' OBSERVER INTERFACE
+' ========================================
+
+package "Observer Interface" <<Observer>> {
+  
+  interface IAgentObserver <<Observer>> {
+    **State Events**
+    + onStateChanged?(event: StateChangedEvent): void
+    --
+    **Thinking Events**
+    + onThinkingStarted?(event: ThinkingEvent): void
+    + onThinkingCompleted?(event: ThinkingCompletedEvent): void
+    --
+    **Action Events**
+    + onActionStarted?(event: ActionStartedEvent): void
+    + onActionCompleted?(event: ActionEvent): void
+    --
+    **Approval Events**
+    + onApprovalRequested?(event: ApprovalRequestedEvent): void
+    + onApprovalReceived?(event: ApprovalReceivedEvent): void
+    --
+    **Error Events**
+    + onError?(event: ErrorEvent): void
+  }
+  
+  note right of IAgentObserver
+    **Interface Segregation Principle**:
+    
+    All methods are optional (TypeScript ?).
+    Observers implement only the methods they need.
+    
+    This allows partial implementation:
+    - WebSocketGateway: All events (broadcasting)
+    - WorkflowEngine: Action + error events only
+    - AuditLogger: State + action + error events
+    - MetricsCollector: Action events only
+    
+    Benefits:
+    - No forced implementation of unused methods
+    - Clear intent (observer only handles what it cares about)
+    - Easy to add new observers
+  end note
+}
+
+' ========================================
+' CONCRETE OBSERVERS
+' ========================================
+
+package "Concrete Observers" <<ConcreteObserver>> {
+  
+  class WebSocketGateway <<ConcreteObserver>> {
+    - server: Server
+    - connectedClients: Map<string, Socket>
+    --
+    **Observer Implementation (All Events)**
+    + onStateChanged(event: StateChangedEvent): void
+    + onThinkingStarted(event: ThinkingEvent): void
+    + onThinkingCompleted(event: ThinkingCompletedEvent): void
+    + onActionStarted(event: ActionStartedEvent): void
+    + onActionCompleted(event: ActionEvent): void
+    + onApprovalRequested(event: ApprovalRequestedEvent): void
+    + onApprovalReceived(event: ApprovalReceivedEvent): void
+    + onError(event: ErrorEvent): void
+    --
+    **WebSocket Broadcasting**
+    - broadcast(message: object): void
+    - sendToProject(projectId: string, message: object): void
+    - sendToUser(userId: string, message: object): void
+    --
+    **Purpose**: Real-time updates to frontend
+  }
+  
+  class WorkflowEngine <<ConcreteObserver>> {
+    - workflowRepository: IWorkflowRepository
+    - agentFactory: AgentFactory
+    --
+    **Observer Implementation (Selective)**
+    + onActionCompleted(event: ActionEvent): void
+    + onError(event: ErrorEvent): void
+    --
+    **Workflow Orchestration**
+    - executeNextStep(taskId: string): void
+    - failWorkflow(taskId: string, error: Error): void
+    - pauseWorkflow(workflowId: string): void
+    --
+    **Purpose**: Coordinate workflow execution
+    
+    **Listens For**:
+    - Action completed ΓåÆ Execute next step
+    - Error ΓåÆ Fail workflow or retry
+  }
+  
+  class AuditLogger <<ConcreteObserver>> {
+    - loggingService: LoggingService
+    - auditRepository: IAuditRepository
+    --
+    **Observer Implementation (Audit Trail)**
+    + onStateChanged(event: StateChangedEvent): void
+    + onActionCompleted(event: ActionEvent): void
+    + onApprovalRequested(event: ApprovalRequestedEvent): void
+    + onApprovalReceived(event: ApprovalReceivedEvent): void
+    + onError(event: ErrorEvent): void
+    --
+    **Audit Logging**
+    - logEvent(event: AgentEvent): void
+    - persistAuditTrail(event: AgentEvent): Promise<void>
+    - generateAuditReport(): AuditReport
+    --
+    **Purpose**: Compliance and audit trail
+    
+    **Listens For**:
+    - State changes ΓåÆ Track agent lifecycle
+    - Actions ΓåÆ Track what agents did
+    - Approvals ΓåÆ Track approval decisions
+    - Errors ΓåÆ Track failures for investigation
+  }
+  
+  class MetricsCollector <<ConcreteObserver>> {
+    - metricsService: MetricsService
+    - prometheusRegistry: Registry
+    --
+    **Observer Implementation (Metrics Only)**
+    + onActionCompleted(event: ActionEvent): void
+    + onError(event: ErrorEvent): void
+    --
+    **Metrics Collection**
+    - recordActionMetric(event: ActionEvent): void
+    - recordErrorMetric(event: ErrorEvent): void
+    - incrementCounter(metric: string): void
+    - recordHistogram(metric: string, value: number): void
+    --
+    **Metrics Tracked**:
+    - Agent action count by role
+    - Agent action duration by tool
+    - Agent error rate
+    - Agent success rate
+    --
+    **Purpose**: Performance monitoring and analytics
+  }
+  
+  class MemoryObserver <<ConcreteObserver>> {
+    - memoryService: MemoryService
+    - embeddingService: EmbeddingService
+    --
+    **Observer Implementation (Learning)**
+    + onActionCompleted(event: ActionEvent): void
+    + onThinkingCompleted(event: ThinkingCompletedEvent): void
+    --
+    **Memory Persistence**
+    - saveThoughtAsMemory(event: ThinkingCompletedEvent): Promise<void>
+    - saveActionResultAsMemory(event: ActionEvent): Promise<void>
+    - calculateImportance(event: AgentEvent): number
+    --
+    **Purpose**: Agent learning and memory formation
+    
+    **Listens For**:
+    - Thinking completed ΓåÆ Save reasoning as memory
+    - Action completed ΓåÆ Save successful patterns as memory
+  }
+  
+  class NotificationService <<ConcreteObserver>> {
+    - emailService: EmailService
+    - slackService: SlackService
+    --
+    **Observer Implementation (Notifications)**
+    + onApprovalRequested(event: ApprovalRequestedEvent): void
+    + onError(event: ErrorEvent): void
+    --
+    **Notification Sending**
+    - sendEmailNotification(userId: string, message: string): Promise<void>
+    - sendSlackNotification(channel: string, message: string): Promise<void>
+    --
+    **Purpose**: External notifications
+    
+    **Listens For**:
+    - Approval requested ΓåÆ Notify user via email/Slack
+    - Error ΓåÆ Notify team on critical errors
+  }
+}
+
+' ========================================
+' RELATIONSHIPS
+' ========================================
+
+' Observer pattern relationships
+AgentEventEmitter o-- "0..*" IAgentObserver : notifies
+IAgentObserver <|.. WebSocketGateway : implements
+IAgentObserver <|.. WorkflowEngine : implements
+IAgentObserver <|.. AuditLogger : implements
+IAgentObserver <|.. MetricsCollector : implements
+IAgentObserver <|.. MemoryObserver : implements
+IAgentObserver <|.. NotificationService : implements
+
+' Agent uses event emitter
+Agent ..> IAgentObserver : notifies via emitter
+
+' ========================================
+' EVENT TYPES
+' ========================================
+
+package "Event Types" <<domain-event>> {
+  
+  class StateChangedEvent <<event>> {
+    + agentId: string
+    + agentRole: AgentRole
+    + previousState: AgentState
+    + currentState: AgentState
+    + timestamp: Date
+    + reason?: string
+  }
+  
+  class ThinkingEvent <<event>> {
+    + agentId: string
+    + agentRole: AgentRole
+    + taskId: string
+    + message: string
+    + timestamp: Date
+  }
+  
+  class ThinkingCompletedEvent <<event>> {
+    + agentId: string
+    + agentRole: AgentRole
+    + taskId: string
+    + thought: AgentThought
+    + confidence: number
+    + timestamp: Date
+  }
+  
+  class ActionEvent <<event>> {
+    + agentId: string
+    + agentRole: AgentRole
+    + taskId: string
+    + actionType: string
+    + toolName: string
+    + parameters: Record<string, unknown>
+    + result?: AgentActionResult
+    + timestamp: Date
+  }
+  
+  class ActionStartedEvent <<event>> {
+    + agentId: string
+    + agentRole: AgentRole
+    + taskId: string
+    + actionType: string
+    + toolName: string
+    + parameters: Record<string, unknown>
+    + timestamp: Date
+  }
+  
+  class ApprovalRequestedEvent <<event>> {
+    + agentId: string
+    + agentRole: AgentRole
+    + taskId: string
+    + approvalType: string
+    + data: unknown
+    + timestamp: Date
+  }
+  
+  class ApprovalReceivedEvent <<event>> {
+    + agentId: string
+    + agentRole: AgentRole
+    + taskId: string
+    + approved: boolean
+    + reason?: string
+    + timestamp: Date
+  }
+  
+  class ErrorEvent <<event>> {
+    + agentId: string
+    + agentRole: AgentRole
+    + taskId?: string
+    + error: Error | string
+    + timestamp: Date
+    + context?: Record<string, unknown>
+  }
+}
+
+' ========================================
+' USAGE EXAMPLE
+' ========================================
+
+note bottom of Agent
+  **Observer Pattern Usage Example**:
+  
+  ```typescript
+  // Setup: Register observers on agent initialization
+  const agent = agentFactory.createAgent(AgentRole.DEV);
+  
+  agent.addEventListener(websocketGateway);
+  agent.addEventListener(workflowEngine);
+  agent.addEventListener(auditLogger);
+  agent.addEventListener(metricsCollector);
+  agent.addEventListener(memoryObserver);
+  
+  // Agent execution triggers events automatically
+  const result = await agent.execute(context);
+  
+  // Internal flow:
+  // 1. agent.transitionState(THINKING)
+  //    ΓåÆ emitter.emitStateChanged(event)
+  //    ΓåÆ websocketGateway.onStateChanged(event)
+  //    ΓåÆ auditLogger.onStateChanged(event)
+  //
+  // 2. agent.think()
+  //    ΓåÆ emitter.emitThinkingStarted(event)
+  //    ΓåÆ websocketGateway.onThinkingStarted(event)
+  //
+  // 3. agent.act()
+  //    ΓåÆ emitter.emitActionCompleted(event)
+  //    ΓåÆ websocketGateway.onActionCompleted(event)
+  //    ΓåÆ workflowEngine.onActionCompleted(event)  // Triggers next step
+  //    ΓåÆ auditLogger.onActionCompleted(event)
+  //    ΓåÆ metricsCollector.onActionCompleted(event)
+  //    ΓåÆ memoryObserver.onActionCompleted(event)
+  //
+  // 4. If error occurs:
+  //    ΓåÆ emitter.emitError(event)
+  //    ΓåÆ All observers with onError() receive notification
+  ```
+  
+  **Benefits**:
+  - Agent doesn't know about WebSocket, Workflow, Audit, etc.
+  - Easy to add new observers without modifying agent
+  - Observers can be added/removed at runtime
+  - Each observer handles events independently
+  - Error in one observer doesn't affect others
+end note
+
+' ========================================
+' BENEFITS
+' ========================================
+
+note top of AgentEventEmitter
+  **Observer Pattern Benefits in AI-CORP**:
+  
+  1. **Loose Coupling**: Agent doesn't know concrete observers
+  2. **Open/Closed**: Add observers without modifying agent
+  3. **Multiple Subscribers**: Many observers for same event
+  4. **Runtime Configuration**: Add/remove observers dynamically
+  5. **Broadcast Communication**: One event, many receivers
+  6. **Error Isolation**: Observer error doesn't affect agent or other observers
+  7. **Separation of Concerns**: Each observer has single responsibility
+  
+  **Real-world Use Cases**:
+  - WebSocket: Real-time UI updates
+  - Workflow: Orchestration trigger
+  - Audit: Compliance logging
+  - Metrics: Performance monitoring
+  - Memory: Agent learning
+  - Notifications: User alerts
+end note
+
+note bottom of IAgentObserver
+  **Design Principles Applied**:
+  
+  **Single Responsibility Principle (SRP)**:
+  - Each observer has one reason to change
+  - WebSocketGateway: UI updates
+  - WorkflowEngine: Orchestration
+  - AuditLogger: Compliance
+  
+  **Open/Closed Principle (OCP)**:
+  - Open for extension (new observers)
+  - Closed for modification (agent unchanged)
+  
+  **Interface Segregation Principle (ISP)**:
+  - All methods optional
+  - Observers implement only what they need
+  
+  **Dependency Inversion Principle (DIP)**:
+  - Agent depends on IAgentObserver abstraction
+  - Not on concrete WebSocketGateway, etc.
+end note
+
+' ========================================
+' PUSH VS PULL MODEL
+' ========================================
+
+note right of AgentEventEmitter
+  **Push Model (Used in AI-CORP)**:
+  
+  Subject (AgentEventEmitter) pushes full event data to observers.
+  
+  **Advantages**:
+  - Observers have all needed data
+  - No extra queries back to subject
+  - Simpler observer implementation
+  - Better performance (no back-and-forth)
+  
+  **Event Data Includes**:
+  - agentId, agentRole (identity)
+  - taskId, projectId (context)
+  - Relevant data (action result, error, etc.)
+  - timestamp (when)
+  
+  **Alternative: Pull Model**:
+  Subject notifies with minimal data, observers query back.
+  Not used in AI-CORP because:
+  - Extra queries slow down system
+  - More coupling (observers need subject reference)
+  - More complex implementation
+end note
+
+@enduml
