+@startuml Repository Pattern - AI-CORP
+!theme plain
+title Design Pattern - Repository Pattern (Domain-Driven Design)
+scale 1.0
+
+' ========================================
+' REPOSITORY PATTERN OVERVIEW
+' ========================================
+
+note as N1
+  **Repository Pattern (Domain-Driven Design)**
+  
+  **Intent**: Mediate between the domain and data mapping layers
+  using a collection-like interface for accessing domain objects.
+  
+  **Benefits**:
+  - Decouples domain layer from persistence layer
+  - Provides collection-like interface (illusion of in-memory collection)
+  - Centralizes data access logic
+  - Easy to swap persistence technologies
+  - Facilitates testing with mock repositories
+  
+  **Structure in AI-CORP**:
+  - Domain Layer: Defines repository interfaces
+  - Infrastructure Layer: Implements repositories with Prisma
+  - Dependency Direction: Infrastructure depends on domain (DIP)
+end note
+
+' ========================================
+' DOMAIN LAYER (INTERFACES)
+' ========================================
+
+package "Domain Layer" <<domain>> {
+  
+  interface IRepository<T> <<Repository Interface>> {
+    + findById(id: string): Promise<T | null>
+    + findAll(): Promise<T[]>
+    + save(entity: T): Promise<T>
+    + update(entity: T): Promise<T>
+    + delete(id: string): Promise<void>
+  }
+  
+  interface IAgentRepository {
+    **Base CRUD**
+    + findById(id: string): Promise<Agent | null>
+    + findAll(): Promise<Agent[]>
+    + save(agent: Agent): Promise<Agent>
+    + update(agent: Agent): Promise<Agent>
+    + delete(id: string): Promise<void>
+    --
+    **Agent-Specific Queries**
+    + findByRole(role: AgentRole): Promise<Agent | null>
+    + findActive(): Promise<Agent[]>
+    + findByRoles(roles: AgentRole[]): Promise<Agent[]>
+    --
+    **Memory Operations**
+    + saveMemory(memory: AgentMemory): Promise<void>
+    + findMemories(agentId: string, namespace?: string): Promise<AgentMemory[]>
+    + searchMemoriesByEmbedding(agentId: string, query: string, limit: number): Promise<AgentMemory[]>
+    + deleteMemory(memoryId: string): Promise<void>
+    --
+    **Message Operations**
+    + saveMessage(message: AgentMessage): Promise<void>
+    + findMessages(projectId: string, limit?: number): Promise<AgentMessage[]>
+    + findMessagesByAgent(agentId: string): Promise<AgentMessage[]>
+  }
+  
+  interface IProjectRepository {
+    **Base CRUD**
+    + findById(id: string): Promise<Project | null>
+    + findAll(): Promise<Project[]>
+    + save(project: Project): Promise<Project>
+    + update(project: Project): Promise<Project>
+    + delete(id: string): Promise<void>
+    --
+    **Project-Specific Queries**
+    + findByUser(userId: string): Promise<Project[]>
+    + findByStatus(status: ProjectStatus): Promise<Project[]>
+    + findActive(): Promise<Project[]>
+    --
+    **Aggregate Navigation**
+    + findMilestones(projectId: string): Promise<Milestone[]>
+    + findTasks(projectId: string): Promise<Task[]>
+    + findTasksByStatus(projectId: string, status: TaskStatus): Promise<Task[]>
+    --
+    **Cost Tracking**
+    + calculateTotalCost(projectId: string): Promise<Money>
+    + getRemainingBudget(projectId: string): Promise<Money>
+  }
+  
+  interface IWorkflowRepository {
+    **Base CRUD**
+    + findById(id: string): Promise<WorkflowRun | null>
+    + findAll(): Promise<WorkflowRun[]>
+    + save(workflow: WorkflowRun): Promise<WorkflowRun>
+    + update(workflow: WorkflowRun): Promise<WorkflowRun>
+    + delete(id: string): Promise<void>
+    --
+    **Workflow-Specific Queries**
+    + findByProject(projectId: string): Promise<WorkflowRun[]>
+    + findByStatus(status: WorkflowStatus): Promise<WorkflowRun[]>
+    + findActive(): Promise<WorkflowRun[]>
+    --
+    **Step Operations**
+    + findSteps(workflowRunId: string): Promise<WorkflowStep[]>
+    + findStepById(stepId: string): Promise<WorkflowStep | null>
+    + updateStep(step: WorkflowStep): Promise<WorkflowStep>
+    --
+    **Approval Operations**
+    + findApprovals(workflowRunId: string): Promise<Approval[]>
+    + findPendingApprovals(workflowRunId: string): Promise<Approval[]>
+    + updateApproval(approval: Approval): Promise<Approval>
+  }
+  
+  interface ITaskRepository {
+    **Base CRUD**
+    + findById(id: string): Promise<Task | null>
+    + findAll(): Promise<Task[]>
+    + save(task: Task): Promise<Task>
+    + update(task: Task): Promise<Task>
+    + delete(id: string): Promise<void>
+    --
+    **Task-Specific Queries**
+    + findByProject(projectId: string): Promise<Task[]>
+    + findByMilestone(milestoneId: string): Promise<Task[]>
+    + findByStatus(projectId: string, status: TaskStatus): Promise<Task[]>
+    + findByAgent(agentRole: AgentRole): Promise<Task[]>
+    + findByPriority(projectId: string, priority: Priority): Promise<Task[]>
+    --
+    **Status Management**
+    + updateStatus(taskId: string, status: TaskStatus): Promise<void>
+    + assignAgent(taskId: string, agentRole: AgentRole): Promise<void>
+  }
+  
+  interface IApprovalRepository {
+    **Base CRUD**
+    + findById(id: string): Promise<Approval | null>
+    + findAll(): Promise<Approval[]>
+    + save(approval: Approval): Promise<Approval>
+    + update(approval: Approval): Promise<Approval>
+    + delete(id: string): Promise<void>
+    --
+    **Approval-Specific Queries**
+    + findByWorkflow(workflowRunId: string): Promise<Approval[]>
+    + findByUser(userId: string): Promise<Approval[]>
+    + findPending(userId: string): Promise<Approval[]>
+    + findByStatus(status: ApprovalStatus): Promise<Approval[]>
+    --
+    **Approval Actions**
+    + approve(approvalId: string, userId: string, comment?: string): Promise<void>
+    + reject(approvalId: string, userId: string, reason: string): Promise<void>
+  }
+  
+  ' Generic repository relationship
+  IAgentRepository --|> IRepository : extends
+  IProjectRepository --|> IRepository : extends
+  IWorkflowRepository --|> IRepository : extends
+  ITaskRepository --|> IRepository : extends
+  IApprovalRepository --|> IRepository : extends
+}
+
+' ========================================
+' INFRASTRUCTURE LAYER (IMPLEMENTATIONS)
+' ========================================
+
+package "Infrastructure Layer" <<infrastructure>> {
+  
+  class PrismaService {
+    - prisma: PrismaClient
+    --
+    + getClient(): PrismaClient
+    + connect(): Promise<void>
+    + disconnect(): Promise<void>
+    + transaction<T>(fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T>
+  }
+  
+  class AgentRepository {
+    - prisma: PrismaService
+    --
+    **Base CRUD Implementation**
+    + findById(id: string): Promise<Agent | null>
+    + findAll(): Promise<Agent[]>
+    + save(agent: Agent): Promise<Agent>
+    + update(agent: Agent): Promise<Agent>
+    + delete(id: string): Promise<void>
+    --
+    **Agent-Specific Implementation**
+    + findByRole(role: AgentRole): Promise<Agent | null>
+    + findActive(): Promise<Agent[]>
+    --
+    **Memory Implementation**
+    + saveMemory(memory: AgentMemory): Promise<void>
+    + findMemories(agentId: string, namespace?: string): Promise<AgentMemory[]>
+    + searchMemoriesByEmbedding(agentId: string, query: string, limit: number): Promise<AgentMemory[]>
+    --
+    **Mapping Methods**
+    - mapToEntity(dbAgent: DbAgent): Agent
+    - mapToDb(agent: Agent): DbAgent
+  }
+  
+  class ProjectRepository {
+    - prisma: PrismaService
+    --
+    **Base CRUD Implementation**
+    + findById(id: string): Promise<Project | null>
+    + findAll(): Promise<Project[]>
+    + save(project: Project): Promise<Project>
+    + update(project: Project): Promise<Project>
+    + delete(id: string): Promise<void>
+    --
+    **Project-Specific Implementation**
+    + findByUser(userId: string): Promise<Project[]>
+    + findActive(): Promise<Project[]>
+    + findMilestones(projectId: string): Promise<Milestone[]>
+    + findTasks(projectId: string): Promise<Task[]>
+    + calculateTotalCost(projectId: string): Promise<Money>
+    --
+    **Mapping Methods**
+    - mapToEntity(dbProject: DbProject): Project
+    - mapToDb(project: Project): DbProject
+    - mapMoney(dbMoney: Decimal): Money
+  }
+  
+  class WorkflowRepository {
+    - prisma: PrismaService
+    --
+    **Base CRUD Implementation**
+    + findById(id: string): Promise<WorkflowRun | null>
+    + save(workflow: WorkflowRun): Promise<WorkflowRun>
+    + update(workflow: WorkflowRun): Promise<WorkflowRun>
+    --
+    **Workflow-Specific Implementation**
+    + findByProject(projectId: string): Promise<WorkflowRun[]>
+    + findByStatus(status: WorkflowStatus): Promise<WorkflowRun[]>
+    + findSteps(workflowRunId: string): Promise<WorkflowStep[]>
+    + findPendingApprovals(workflowRunId: string): Promise<Approval[]>
+    --
+    **Mapping Methods**
+    - mapToEntity(dbWorkflow: DbWorkflow): WorkflowRun
+    - mapToDb(workflow: WorkflowRun): DbWorkflow
+  }
+  
+  class TaskRepository {
+    - prisma: PrismaService
+    --
+    **Base CRUD Implementation**
+    + findById(id: string): Promise<Task | null>
+    + save(task: Task): Promise<Task>
+    + update(task: Task): Promise<Task>
+    + delete(id: string): Promise<void>
+    --
+    **Task-Specific Implementation**
+    + findByProject(projectId: string): Promise<Task[]>
+    + findByStatus(projectId: string, status: TaskStatus): Promise<Task[]>
+    + findByAgent(agentRole: AgentRole): Promise<Task[]>
+    + updateStatus(taskId: string, status: TaskStatus): Promise<void>
+    --
+    **Mapping Methods**
+    - mapToEntity(dbTask: DbTask): Task
+    - mapToDb(task: Task): DbTask
+  }
+  
+  class ApprovalRepository {
+    - prisma: PrismaService
+    --
+    **Base CRUD Implementation**
+    + findById(id: string): Promise<Approval | null>
+    + save(approval: Approval): Promise<Approval>
+    + update(approval: Approval): Promise<Approval>
+    --
+    **Approval-Specific Implementation**
+    + findByWorkflow(workflowRunId: string): Promise<Approval[]>
+    + findPending(userId: string): Promise<Approval[]>
+    + approve(approvalId: string, userId: string, comment?: string): Promise<void>
+    + reject(approvalId: string, userId: string, reason: string): Promise<void>
+    --
+    **Mapping Methods**
+    - mapToEntity(dbApproval: DbApproval): Approval
+    - mapToDb(approval: Approval): DbApproval
+  }
+  
+  ' Implementation relationships
+  IAgentRepository <|.. AgentRepository : implements
+  IProjectRepository <|.. ProjectRepository : implements
+  IWorkflowRepository <|.. WorkflowRepository : implements
+  ITaskRepository <|.. TaskRepository : implements
+  IApprovalRepository <|.. ApprovalRepository : implements
+  
+  ' Prisma dependency
+  AgentRepository --> PrismaService : uses
+  ProjectRepository --> PrismaService : uses
+  WorkflowRepository --> PrismaService : uses
+  TaskRepository --> PrismaService : uses
+  ApprovalRepository --> PrismaService : uses
+}
+
+' ========================================
+' APPLICATION LAYER (SERVICES)
+' ========================================
+
+package "Application Layer" <<application>> {
+  
+  class AgentService {
+    - agentRepository: IAgentRepository
+    - memoryService: MemoryService
+    --
+    + getAgent(id: string): Promise<Agent>
+    + getAgentByRole(role: AgentRole): Promise<Agent>
+    + createAgent(data: CreateAgentDto): Promise<Agent>
+    + updateAgent(id: string, data: UpdateAgentDto): Promise<Agent>
+    + deleteAgent(id: string): Promise<void>
+    + loadMemoriesForContext(agentId: string, query: string): Promise<AgentMemory[]>
+  }
+  
+  class ProjectService {
+    - projectRepository: IProjectRepository
+    - taskRepository: ITaskRepository
+    --
+    + getProject(id: string): Promise<Project>
+    + createProject(data: CreateProjectDto): Promise<Project>
+    + updateProject(id: string, data: UpdateProjectDto): Promise<Project>
+    + deleteProject(id: string): Promise<void>
+    + getProjectTasks(projectId: string): Promise<Task[]>
+    + calculateProjectCost(projectId: string): Promise<Money>
+  }
+  
+  class WorkflowService {
+    - workflowRepository: IWorkflowRepository
+    - agentService: AgentService
+    --
+    + getWorkflow(id: string): Promise<WorkflowRun>
+    + createWorkflow(projectId: string, workflowDefId: string): Promise<WorkflowRun>
+    + startWorkflow(id: string): Promise<void>
+    + pauseWorkflow(id: string): Promise<void>
+    + completeWorkflow(id: string): Promise<void>
+    + getWorkflowSteps(workflowId: string): Promise<WorkflowStep[]>
+  }
+  
+  ' Service dependencies on repositories
+  AgentService --> IAgentRepository : uses
+  ProjectService --> IProjectRepository : uses
+  ProjectService --> ITaskRepository : uses
+  WorkflowService --> IWorkflowRepository : uses
+}
+
+' ========================================
+' DEPENDENCY DIRECTION
+' ========================================
+
+note top of IRepository
+  **Dependency Inversion Principle (DIP)**:
+  
+  Domain Layer defines interfaces (abstractions)
+  Γåæ
+  Infrastructure Layer implements interfaces
+  Γåæ
+  Application Layer depends on abstractions
+  
+  **Benefits**:
+  - Domain layer independent of persistence
+  - Easy to swap implementations (Prisma ΓåÆ TypeORM)
+  - Easy to test with mock repositories
+  - Infrastructure details hidden from domain
+end note
+
+note bottom of PrismaService
+  **Infrastructure Layer Responsibilities**:
+  
+  1. Implement repository interfaces
+  2. Handle database connections (Prisma)
+  3. Map between domain entities and DB models
+  4. Execute SQL queries
+  5. Handle transactions
+  6. Optimize database operations
+  
+  **Mapping Strategy**:
+  - DB Model ΓåÆ Domain Entity (read)
+  - Domain Entity ΓåÆ DB Model (write)
+  - Handle complex types (Money, JSON, enums)
+end note
+
+note bottom of AgentService
+  **Application Layer Responsibilities**:
+  
+  1. Orchestrate business logic
+  2. Use repositories through interfaces
+  3. Coordinate multiple repositories
+  4. Handle transactions across aggregates
+  5. Enforce business rules
+  6. Map between DTOs and domain entities
+  
+  **Does NOT**:
+  - Know about database (Prisma)
+  - Execute SQL directly
+  - Handle persistence details
+end note
+
+' ========================================
+' USAGE EXAMPLE
+' ========================================
+
+note right of AgentService
+  **Repository Pattern Usage Example**:
+  
+  ```typescript
+  @Injectable()
+  class AgentService {
+    constructor(
+      private readonly agentRepository: IAgentRepository,
+      private readonly memoryService: MemoryService
+    ) {}
+    
+    async executeAgent(role: AgentRole, context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentResult> {
+      // Repository provides collection-like interface
+      const agent = await this.agentRepository.findByRole(role);
+      if (!agent) throw new Error('Agent not found');
+      
+      // Load memories using repository
+      const memories = await this.agentRepository.searchMemoriesByEmbedding(
+        agent.getId(),
+        context.task,
+        5
+      );
+      
+      context.relevantMemories = memories;
+      
+      // Execute agent
+      const result = await agent.execute(context);
+      
+      // Save new memory
+      const memory = new AgentMemory(...);
+      await this.agentRepository.saveMemory(memory);
+      
+      return result;
+    }
+  }
+  ```
+  
+  **Testing with Mock Repository**:
+  
+  ```typescript
+  describe('AgentService', () => {
+    it('should execute agent', async () => {
+      const mockRepo: IAgentRepository = {
+        findByRole: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(mockAgent),
+        searchMemoriesByEmbedding: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue([]),
+        saveMemory: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined),
+        // ... other methods
+      };
+      
+      const service = new AgentService(mockRepo, mockMemoryService);
+      const result = await service.executeAgent(AgentRole.DEV, context);
+      
+      expect(result.success).toBe(true);
+      expect(mockRepo.findByRole).toHaveBeenCalledWith(AgentRole.DEV);
+    });
+  });
+  ```
+end note
+
+' ========================================
+' BENEFITS
+' ========================================
+
+note top of IAgentRepository
+  **Repository Pattern Benefits in AI-CORP**:
+  
+  1. **Persistence Ignorance**: Domain doesn't know about Prisma
+  2. **Testability**: Easy to mock repositories for testing
+  3. **Flexibility**: Can swap Prisma for TypeORM without changing domain
+  4. **Centralization**: All data access logic in one place
+  5. **Collection Interface**: Feels like in-memory collection
+  6. **Query Optimization**: Repository can optimize queries internally
+  7. **Aggregate Boundaries**: Repository enforces aggregate access rules
+  
+  **DDD Principles**:
+  - Access aggregates by root only
+  - Repository per aggregate root
+  - Hides persistence complexity
+  - Maintains domain model purity
+end note
+
+@enduml

+@startuml Strategy Pattern - AI-CORP
+!theme plain
+title Design Pattern - Strategy Pattern (Thinking & Action Strategies)
+scale 1.0
+
+' ========================================
+' STRATEGY PATTERN OVERVIEW
+' ========================================
+
+note as N1
+  **Strategy Pattern (Gang of Four)**
+  
+  **Intent**: Define a family of algorithms, encapsulate each one,
+  and make them interchangeable. Strategy lets the algorithm vary
+  independently from clients that use it.
+  
+  **Benefits**:
+  - Eliminates conditional statements
+  - Runtime algorithm selection
+  - Encapsulates algorithm variations
+  - Easy to add new strategies
+  
+  **Structure**:
+  - Context: BaseAgent
+  - Strategy: IThinkingStrategy, IActionStrategy
+  - ConcreteStrategy: CEOThinkingStrategy, DevActionStrategy, etc.
+  
+  **Applied in AI-CORP**:
+  - Thinking strategies: How agents analyze and plan
+  - Action strategies: How agents execute planned actions
+end note
+
+' ========================================
+' THINKING STRATEGY
+' ========================================
+
+package "Thinking Strategy" <<Strategy Pattern>> {
+  
+  interface IThinkingStrategy <<Strategy>> {
+    + think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+  }
+  
+  abstract class BaseThinkingStrategy {
+    **Template Method**
+    + think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    --
+    **Abstract Methods**
+    # {abstract} generateThought(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    --
+    **Helper Methods**
+    # validateContext(context: AgentContext): void
+    # createAction(toolName: string, params: object, rationale: string): AgentAction
+    # getMemoriesByNamespace(context: AgentContext, namespace: string): string[]
+  }
+  
+  class CEOThinkingStrategy <<ConcreteStrategy>> {
+    **Strategic, High-level Thinking**
+    + think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    --
+    # generateThought(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    - generateStrategicReasoning(task: string, projectContext: object, memories: any[]): string
+    - planStrategicActions(task: string, context: AgentContext): AgentAction[]
+    - assessConfidence(context: AgentContext): number
+    - identifyRisks(task: string): string[]
+    --
+    **Focus**: Strategy, delegation, risk management
+  }
+  
+  class PMThinkingStrategy <<ConcreteStrategy>> {
+    **Task Decomposition & Planning**
+    + think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    --
+    # generateThought(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    - decomposeTask(task: string): string[]
+    - generatePlanningReasoning(task: string, subtasks: string[], context: AgentContext): string
+    - planSubtaskActions(subtasks: string[], context: AgentContext): AgentAction[]
+    - determineTargetAgent(subtask: string): AgentRole
+    - determinePriority(task: string): Priority
+    - identifyDependencies(subtasks: string[]): string[]
+    --
+    **Focus**: Planning, coordination, dependencies
+  }
+  
+  class DEVThinkingStrategy <<ConcreteStrategy>> {
+    **Technical Implementation Planning**
+    + think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    --
+    # generateThought(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    - generateTechnicalReasoning(task: string, memories: any[]): string
+    - planImplementationActions(task: string, tools: any[], context: AgentContext): AgentAction[]
+    - inferFilePath(task: string): string
+    - assessTechnicalConfidence(context: AgentContext): number
+    - getQualityChecklist(): string[]
+    --
+    **Focus**: Code quality, best practices, implementation
+  }
+  
+  class QAThinkingStrategy <<ConcreteStrategy>> {
+    **Test Planning & Quality Assurance**
+    + think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    --
+    # generateThought(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    - generateTestScenarios(task: string): string[]
+    - generateTestingReasoning(task: string, scenarios: string[]): string
+    - planTestingActions(scenarios: string[], context: AgentContext): AgentAction[]
+    - identifyCoverageAreas(task: string): string[]
+    --
+    **Focus**: Testing, edge cases, quality verification
+  }
+  
+  class MarketingThinkingStrategy <<ConcreteStrategy>> {
+    **Campaign Planning & Market Analysis**
+    + think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    --
+    # generateThought(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    - generateMarketingReasoning(task: string, projectContext: object): string
+    - planMarketingActions(task: string, context: AgentContext): AgentAction[]
+    - identifyTargetAudience(task: string, projectContext: object): string
+    - defineMetrics(task: string): string[]
+    - selectChannels(task: string): string[]
+    --
+    **Focus**: Audience, campaigns, metrics, channels
+  }
+  
+  class BAThinkingStrategy <<ConcreteStrategy>> {
+    **Requirements Analysis & Documentation**
+    + think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    --
+    # generateThought(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    - gatherRequirements(task: string): Requirement[]
+    - generateAnalysisReasoning(requirements: Requirement[]): string
+    - planDocumentationActions(requirements: Requirement[], context: AgentContext): AgentAction[]
+    - identifyStakeholders(task: string): string[]
+    - defineAcceptanceCriteria(requirements: Requirement[]): Criterion[]
+    --
+    **Focus**: Requirements, specifications, acceptance criteria
+  }
+  
+  class ArchitectThinkingStrategy <<ConcreteStrategy>> {
+    **Architecture Design & Pattern Selection**
+    + think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    --
+    # generateThought(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    - analyzeQualityAttributes(task: string): Attribute[]
+    - generateArchitectureReasoning(attributes: Attribute[]): string
+    - selectArchitectureStyle(attributes: Attribute[]): string
+    - selectDesignPatterns(requirements: object): Pattern[]
+    - planArchitectureActions(design: object, context: AgentContext): AgentAction[]
+    --
+    **Focus**: Quality attributes, patterns, architecture style
+  }
+  
+  class DevOpsThinkingStrategy <<ConcreteStrategy>> {
+    **Deployment Planning & Infrastructure**
+    + think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    --
+    # generateThought(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    - analyzeDeploymentRequirements(task: string): object
+    - generateInfrastructureReasoning(requirements: object): string
+    - planDeploymentActions(environment: string, context: AgentContext): AgentAction[]
+    - selectDeploymentStrategy(requirements: object): string
+    - defineRollbackPlan(deployment: object): object
+    --
+    **Focus**: Deployment, infrastructure, rollback, monitoring
+  }
+  
+  ' Strategy relationships
+  IThinkingStrategy <|.. BaseThinkingStrategy : implements
+  BaseThinkingStrategy <|-- CEOThinkingStrategy : extends
+  BaseThinkingStrategy <|-- PMThinkingStrategy : extends
+  BaseThinkingStrategy <|-- DEVThinkingStrategy : extends
+  BaseThinkingStrategy <|-- QAThinkingStrategy : extends
+  BaseThinkingStrategy <|-- MarketingThinkingStrategy : extends
+  BaseThinkingStrategy <|-- BAThinkingStrategy : extends
+  BaseThinkingStrategy <|-- ArchitectThinkingStrategy : extends
+  BaseThinkingStrategy <|-- DevOpsThinkingStrategy : extends
+}
+
+' ========================================
+' ACTION STRATEGY
+' ========================================
+
+package "Action Strategy" <<Strategy Pattern>> {
+  
+  interface IActionStrategy <<Strategy>> {
+    + act(context: AgentContext, thought: AgentThought): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+  }
+  
+  abstract class BaseActionStrategy {
+    **Template Method**
+    + act(context: AgentContext, thought: AgentThought): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+    --
+    **Abstract Methods**
+    # {abstract} executeActions(context: AgentContext, actions: AgentAction[]): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+    --
+    **Helper Methods**
+    # validateThought(thought: AgentThought): void
+    # executeSingleAction(action: AgentAction): Promise<any>
+    # handleActionError(action: AgentAction, error: Error): void
+    # combineResults(results: any[]): AgentActionResult
+  }
+  
+  class CEOActionStrategy <<ConcreteStrategy>> {
+    **Delegation & Oversight Actions**
+    + act(context: AgentContext, thought: AgentThought): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+    --
+    # executeActions(context: AgentContext, actions: AgentAction[]): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+    - delegateToAgent(targetAgent: AgentRole, task: string): Promise<any>
+    - createStrategicMilestone(projectId: string, data: object): Promise<any>
+    - monitorProgress(projectId: string): Promise<any>
+    --
+    **Focus**: High-level delegation, milestone creation
+  }
+  
+  class DevActionStrategy <<ConcreteStrategy>> {
+    **Code Implementation Actions**
+    + act(context: AgentContext, thought: AgentThought): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+    --
+    # executeActions(context: AgentContext, actions: AgentAction[]): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+    - readFile(path: string): Promise<string>
+    - writeFile(path: string, content: string): Promise<void>
+    - runTests(testPath: string): Promise<TestResult>
+    - formatCode(code: string): string
+    - lintCode(code: string): LintResult
+    --
+    **Focus**: File I/O, code generation, testing
+  }
+  
+  class QAActionStrategy <<ConcreteStrategy>> {
+    **Testing & Verification Actions**
+    + act(context: AgentContext, thought: AgentThought): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+    --
+    # executeActions(context: AgentContext, actions: AgentAction[]): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+    - writeTestCase(testFile: string, scenarios: string[]): Promise<void>
+    - runTestSuite(testPath: string): Promise<TestResult>
+    - generateCoverageReport(testResult: TestResult): Promise<CoverageReport>
+    - createBugReport(issue: object): Promise<string>
+    --
+    **Focus**: Test execution, coverage, bug reporting
+  }
+  
+  class MarketingActionStrategy <<ConcreteStrategy>> {
+    **Campaign Execution Actions**
+    + act(context: AgentContext, thought: AgentThought): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+    --
+    # executeActions(context: AgentContext, actions: AgentAction[]): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+    - analyzeAudience(projectId: string): Promise<AudienceData>
+    - createCampaignContent(theme: string): Promise<Content>
+    - setupAnalytics(projectId: string, metrics: string[]): Promise<void>
+    - launchCampaign(campaign: object): Promise<CampaignResult>
+    --
+    **Focus**: Content creation, analytics, campaign launch
+  }
+  
+  class DevOpsActionStrategy <<ConcreteStrategy>> {
+    **Deployment & Infrastructure Actions**
+    + act(context: AgentContext, thought: AgentThought): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+    --
+    # executeActions(context: AgentContext, actions: AgentAction[]): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+    - createDeploymentJob(projectId: string, environment: string): Promise<DeploymentJob>
+    - executeDeployment(job: DeploymentJob): Promise<DeploymentResult>
+    - runHealthCheck(environment: string): Promise<HealthStatus>
+    - setupMonitoring(environment: string): Promise<void>
+    - rollbackDeployment(jobId: string): Promise<void>
+    --
+    **Focus**: Deployment, health checks, rollback, monitoring
+  }
+  
+  ' Strategy relationships
+  IActionStrategy <|.. BaseActionStrategy : implements
+  BaseActionStrategy <|-- CEOActionStrategy : extends
+  BaseActionStrategy <|-- DevActionStrategy : extends
+  BaseActionStrategy <|-- QAActionStrategy : extends
+  BaseActionStrategy <|-- MarketingActionStrategy : extends
+  BaseActionStrategy <|-- DevOpsActionStrategy : extends
+}
+
+' ========================================
+' CONTEXT (AGENT)
+' ========================================
+
+package "Context" <<Context>> {
+  
+  abstract class BaseAgent <<Context>> {
+    - thinkingStrategy: IThinkingStrategy
+    - actionStrategy: IActionStrategy
+    --
+    **Strategy Usage**
+    + execute(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentResult>
+    # think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    # act(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+    --
+    **Strategy Management**
+    + setThinkingStrategy(strategy: IThinkingStrategy): void
+    + setActionStrategy(strategy: IActionStrategy): void
+  }
+  
+  class CEOAgent {
+    + think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    + act(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+  }
+  
+  class DevAgent {
+    + think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    + act(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+  }
+  
+  class QAAgent {
+    + think(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentThought>
+    + act(context: AgentContext): Promise<AgentActionResult>
+  }
+  
+  ' Context relationships
+  BaseAgent o-- IThinkingStrategy : uses
+  BaseAgent o-- IActionStrategy : uses
+  BaseAgent <|-- CEOAgent : extends
+  BaseAgent <|-- DevAgent : extends
+  BaseAgent <|-- QAAgent : extends
+  
+  CEOAgent ..> CEOThinkingStrategy : delegates to
+  CEOAgent ..> CEOActionStrategy : delegates to
+  DevAgent ..> DEVThinkingStrategy : delegates to
+  DevAgent ..> DevActionStrategy : delegates to
+  QAAgent ..> QAThinkingStrategy : delegates to
+  QAAgent ..> QAActionStrategy : delegates to
+}
+
+' ========================================
+' USAGE EXAMPLE
+' ========================================
+
+note bottom of BaseAgent
+  **Strategy Pattern Usage Example**:
+  
+  ```typescript
+  // Strategy injection (Dependency Injection)
+  const devAgent = new DevAgent(
+    new DEVThinkingStrategy(memoryService, llmProvider),
+    new DevActionStrategy(toolExecutor)
+  );
+  
+  // Execute uses strategies internally
+  const result = await devAgent.execute(context);
+  
+  // Strategies are called:
+  // 1. thinkingStrategy.think(context) ΓåÆ AgentThought
+  // 2. actionStrategy.act(context, thought) ΓåÆ AgentActionResult
+  
+  // Runtime strategy swapping (if needed)
+  devAgent.setThinkingStrategy(new AdvancedDevThinkingStrategy());
+  
+  // Benefits:
+  // - Agent behavior changes without modifying agent class
+  // - Easy to test strategies in isolation
+  // - Easy to add new thinking/action variations
+  // - Follows Open/Closed Principle
+  ```
+end note
+
+' ========================================
+' BENEFITS & DESIGN PRINCIPLES
+' ========================================
+
+note top of IThinkingStrategy
+  **Strategy Pattern Benefits**:
+  
+  1. **Runtime Flexibility**: Change behavior at runtime
+  2. **Eliminates Conditionals**: No switch/if-else for behavior selection
+  3. **Encapsulation**: Each strategy is self-contained
+  4. **Open/Closed**: Add strategies without modifying context
+  5. **Single Responsibility**: Each strategy has one job
+  6. **Testability**: Test strategies independently
+  
+  **SOLID Principles Applied**:
+  - SRP: Each strategy does one thing
+  - OCP: New strategies without modifying agents
+  - LSP: All strategies substitutable
+  - ISP: Focused interface (one method)
+  - DIP: Agent depends on abstraction
+end note
+
+note top of IActionStrategy
+  **Action Strategy Variations**:
+  
+  Each agent role needs different action execution:
+  - CEO: Delegation, strategic oversight
+  - PM: Task creation, coordination
+  - DEV: Code writing, testing
+  - QA: Test execution, reporting
+  - Marketing: Content, campaigns, analytics
+  - BA: Requirements documentation
+  - Architect: Architecture documentation, diagrams
+  - DevOps: Deployment, monitoring, rollback
+  
+  Strategy pattern allows each role to have
+  custom action execution without tight coupling.
+end note
+
+note bottom of CEOThinkingStrategy
+  **CEO Strategy Characteristics**:
+  
+  **Thinking Focus**:
+  - High-level strategic reasoning
+  - Risk identification and mitigation
+  - Resource allocation decisions
+  - Delegation to appropriate agents
+  
+  **Action Focus**:
+  - Create strategic milestones
+  - Delegate tasks to other agents
+  - Monitor overall project progress
+  - Make high-level decisions
+  
+  **Example Output**:
+  - Thought: "Strategic analysis reveals need for phased approach..."
+  - Actions: [DelegateToAgent(PM), CreateMilestone("Phase 1")]
+end note
+
+note bottom of DEVThinkingStrategy
+  **DEV Strategy Characteristics**:
+  
+  **Thinking Focus**:
+  - Technical implementation analysis
+  - Code quality considerations
+  - Best practices application
+  - Tool and library selection
+  
+  **Action Focus**:
+  - Read existing code
+  - Write new code/modify files
+  - Run tests
+  - Check code quality (lint, format)
+  
+  **Example Output**:
+  - Thought: "Technical analysis: Need authentication endpoint..."
+  - Actions: [WriteFile("auth.ts", code), RunTests()]
+end note
+
+@enduml

+@startuml Project Aggregate - Detailed Class Diagram
+!theme plain
+title Project Aggregate - Detailed Structure
+scale 1.0

+' ========================================
+' PROJECT AGGREGATE
+' ========================================
+
+package "Project Aggregate" <<aggregate>> {
+  
+  class Project <<aggregate-root>> {
+    - id: string
+    - name: string
+    - description: string
+    - goal: string
+    - status: ProjectStatus
+    - budget: Money | null
+    - costAccrued: Money
+    - createdById: string
+    - createdAt: DateTime
+    - updatedAt: DateTime
+    --
+    **Lifecycle Methods**
+    + create(data: ProjectData): Project
+    + updateStatus(status: ProjectStatus): void
+    + pause(): void
+    + resume(): void
+    + complete(): void
+    + cancel(): void
+    --
+    **Milestone Management**
+    + addMilestone(milestone: Milestone): void
+    + removeMilestone(milestoneId: string): void
+    + getMilestones(): Milestone[]
+    + getMilestoneById(id: string): Milestone | null
+    --
+    **Task Management**
+    + addTask(task: Task): void
+    + removeTask(taskId: string): void
+    + getTasks(): Task[]
+    + getTasksByStatus(status: TaskStatus): Task[]
+    + assignTask(taskId: string, agent: AgentRole): void
+    --
+    **Cost Management**
+    + setBudget(budget: Money): void
+    + recordCost(cost: Money): void
+    + calculateTotalCost(): Money
+    + validateBudget(): boolean
+    + getRemainingBudget(): Money
+    --
+    **Progress Tracking**
+    + calculateProgress(): number
+    + getCompletionPercentage(): number
+    + isOnTrack(): boolean
+    --
+    **Domain Events Emitted**
+    ~ emitProjectCreated(): void
+    ~ emitProjectStatusChanged(): void
+    ~ emitBudgetExceeded(): void
+  }
+  
+  class Milestone <<entity>> {
+    - id: string
+    - projectId: string
+    - name: string
+    - description: string
+    - status: MilestoneStatus
+    - dueDate: DateTime | null
+    - createdAt: DateTime
+    - updatedAt: DateTime
+    --
+    **Lifecycle Methods**
+    + create(data: MilestoneData): Milestone
+    + updateStatus(status: MilestoneStatus): void
+    + start(): void
+    + complete(): void
+    --
+    **Task Management**
+    + addTask(task: Task): void
+    + removeTask(taskId: string): void
+    + getTasks(): Task[]
+    --
+    **Progress Tracking**
+    + calculateProgress(): number
+    + getCompletionPercentage(): number
+    + isOverdue(): boolean
+    + getDaysUntilDue(): number
+    --
+    **Validation**
+    + validate(): boolean
+    + canComplete(): boolean
+    --
+    **Domain Events Emitted**
+    ~ emitMilestoneCreated(): void
+    ~ emitMilestoneCompleted(): void
+  }
+  
+  class Task <<entity>> {
+    - id: string
+    - projectId: string
+    - milestoneId: string | null
+    - title: string
+    - description: string
+    - status: TaskStatus
+    - priority: Priority
+    - assignedAgent: AgentRole | null
+    - estimatedCost: Money | null
+    - actualCost: Money
+    - createdAt: DateTime
+    - updatedAt: DateTime
+    --
+    **Lifecycle Methods**
+    + create(data: TaskData): Task
+    + updateStatus(newStatus: TaskStatus): void
+    + transitionStatus(newStatus: TaskStatus): void
+    --
+    **Assignment**
+    + assignToAgent(role: AgentRole): void
+    + unassign(): void
+    + isAssigned(): boolean
+    --
+    **Status Transitions (Kanban)**
+    + moveToInProgress(): void
+    + moveToReview(): void
+    + moveToDone(): void
+    + moveBackToInProgress(): void
+    --
+    **Cost Management**
+    + setEstimatedCost(cost: Money): void
+    + recordCost(cost: Money): void
+    + getActualCost(): Money
+    + isOverBudget(): boolean
+    --
+    **Validation**
+    + validate(): boolean
+    + canTransitionTo(status: TaskStatus): boolean
+    + isValidTransition(from: TaskStatus, to: TaskStatus): boolean
+    --
+    **Domain Events Emitted**
+    ~ emitTaskCreated(): void
+    ~ emitTaskStatusChanged(): void
+    ~ emitTaskAssigned(): void
+  }
+  
+  enum ProjectStatus <<value-object>> {
+    active
+    paused
+    completed
+    cancelled
+    --
+    + isActive(): boolean
+    + isTerminal(): boolean
+  }
+  
+  enum MilestoneStatus <<value-object>> {
+    pending
+    in_progress
+    completed
+    --
+    + canTransitionTo(target: MilestoneStatus): boolean
+  }
+  
+  enum TaskStatus <<value-object>> {
+    todo
+    in_progress
+    review
+    done
+    --
+    **Kanban Transition Rules**
+    + canTransitionTo(target: TaskStatus): boolean
+    + getValidTransitions(): TaskStatus[]
+  }
+  
+  enum Priority <<value-object>> {
+    low
+    medium
+    high
+    critical
+    --
+    + getValue(): number
+    + compare(other: Priority): number
+  }
+  
+  class Money <<value-object>> {
+    + amount: Decimal
+    + currency: string
+    --
+    **Operations**
+    + add(other: Money): Money
+    + subtract(other: Money): Money
+    + multiply(factor: number): Money
+    + divide(factor: number): Money
+    --
+    **Comparison**
+    + equals(other: Money): boolean
+    + greaterThan(other: Money): boolean
+    + lessThan(other: Money): boolean
+    --
+    **Conversion**
+    + format(): string
+    + toJSON(): object
+    --
+    **Validation**
+    + isPositive(): boolean
+    + isZero(): boolean
+    + isNegative(): boolean
+    + validate(): boolean
+  }
+}
+
+' ========================================
+' RELATIONSHIPS
+' ========================================
+
+' Aggregate composition
+Project "1" *-- "0..*" Milestone : contains
+Project "1" *-- "0..*" Task : contains
+Milestone "1" o-- "0..*" Task : groups
+
+' Value object associations
+Project --> ProjectStatus : has
+Milestone --> MilestoneStatus : has
+Task --> TaskStatus : has
+Task --> Priority : has
+Project --> Money : budget
+Task --> Money : cost
+
+' External references
+Project --> User : created by
+Task --> Agent : assigned to
+
+' Domain Events
+Project ..> ProjectCreatedEvent : emits
+Project ..> ProjectStatusChangedEvent : emits
+Milestone ..> MilestoneCreatedEvent : emits
+Milestone ..> MilestoneCompletedEvent : emits
+Task ..> TaskCreatedEvent : emits
+Task ..> TaskStatusChangedEvent : emits
+Task ..> TaskAssignedEvent : emits
+
+' ========================================
+' MULTIPLICITIES
+' ========================================
+
+note left of Project::getMilestones
+  **Multiplicity**: 0..*
+  A project can have zero or more milestones
+end note
+
+note left of Project::getTasks
+  **Multiplicity**: 0..*
+  A project can have zero or more tasks
+end note
+
+note left of Milestone::getTasks
+  **Multiplicity**: 0..*
+  A milestone can group zero or more tasks
+end note
+
+' ========================================
+' BUSINESS RULES
+' ========================================
+
+note bottom of Project
+  **Aggregate Root Invariants**:
+  
+  1. Project name must be unique per user
+  2. Budget must be positive when provided
+  3. Cost accrued cannot exceed budget (warning threshold at 90%)
+  4. Cannot delete project with active tasks or workflows
+  5. Project completion requires all milestones completed
+  6. Status transitions:
+     - active Γåö paused
+     - active ΓåÆ completed | cancelled
+     - paused ΓåÆ completed | cancelled
+  
+  **Consistency Boundary**:
+  - All milestones and tasks belong to exactly one project
+  - Project cost is sum of all task actual costs
+  - Project progress is weighted average of milestone progress
+end note
+
+note bottom of Milestone
+  **Entity Invariants**:
+  
+  1. Milestone must belong to valid project
+  2. Due date must be in future when created
+  3. Cannot complete milestone with incomplete tasks
+  4. Status transitions:
+     - pending ΓåÆ in_progress ΓåÆ completed
+  5. Name must be unique within project
+end note
+
+note bottom of Task
+  **Entity Invariants** (Kanban Rules):
+  
+  1. Task must belong to valid project
+  2. Valid status transitions (Req 12, 17):
+     - todo ΓåÆ in_progress ΓåÆ review ΓåÆ done
+     - todo ΓåÆ done (simple tasks)
+     - review ΓåÆ in_progress (rework)
+  3. Cannot transition backwards (except review ΓåÆ in_progress)
+  4. Estimated cost must be non-negative
+  5. Actual cost cannot be negative
+  6. Can only be assigned to active agents
+  7. High priority tasks should be worked first
+  8. Cannot delete task in review or done status
+end note
+
+note bottom of TaskStatus
+  **Valid Kanban Transitions**:
+  
+  todo ΓåÆ in_progress
+  in_progress ΓåÆ review
+  review ΓåÆ done
+  review ΓåÆ in_progress (rework only)
+  todo ΓåÆ done (fast-track)
+  
+  **Invalid Transitions**:
+  - done ΓåÆ * (cannot reopen)
+  - * ΓåÆ todo (no backwards except reviewΓåÆin_progress)
+end note
+
+note top of Money
+  **Value Object Characteristics**:
+  
+  - Immutable: All operations return new Money instance
+  - Value equality: Two Money objects equal if amount and currency match
+  - Self-validating: Cannot create invalid Money
+  - Currency must match for arithmetic operations
+  - Uses Decimal for precision (no floating point)
+end note
+
+' ========================================
+' DOMAIN EVENTS
+' ========================================
+
+class ProjectCreatedEvent <<domain-event>> {
+  + eventId: string
+  + eventType: 'ProjectCreated'
+  + projectId: string
+  + projectName: string
+  + createdBy: string
+  + timestamp: DateTime
+  + metadata: object
+}
+
+class ProjectStatusChangedEvent <<domain-event>> {
+  + eventId: string
+  + eventType: 'ProjectStatusChanged'
+  + projectId: string
+  + previousStatus: ProjectStatus
+  + newStatus: ProjectStatus
+  + reason: string | null
+  + timestamp: DateTime
+}
+
+class MilestoneCreatedEvent <<domain-event>> {
+  + eventId: string
+  + eventType: 'MilestoneCreated'
+  + milestoneId: string
+  + projectId: string
+  + milestoneName: string
+  + dueDate: DateTime | null
+  + timestamp: DateTime
+}
+
+class MilestoneCompletedEvent <<domain-event>> {
+  + eventId: string
+  + eventType: 'MilestoneCompleted'
+  + milestoneId: string
+  + projectId: string
+  + completionPercentage: number
+  + timestamp: DateTime
+}
+
+class TaskCreatedEvent <<domain-event>> {
+  + eventId: string
+  + eventType: 'TaskCreated'
+  + taskId: string
+  + projectId: string
+  + title: string
+  + assignedAgent: AgentRole | null
+  + priority: Priority
+  + timestamp: DateTime
+}
+
+class TaskStatusChangedEvent <<domain-event>> {
+  + eventId: string
+  + eventType: 'TaskStatusChanged'
+  + taskId: string
+  + projectId: string
+  + previousStatus: TaskStatus
+  + newStatus: TaskStatus
+  + changedBy: string
+  + timestamp: DateTime
+}
+
+class TaskAssignedEvent <<domain-event>> {
+  + eventId: string
+  + eventType: 'TaskAssigned'
+  + taskId: string
+  + projectId: string
+  + agentRole: AgentRole
+  + timestamp: DateTime
+}

+@startuml Workflow Aggregate - Detailed Class Diagram
+!theme plain
+title Workflow Aggregate - Orchestration & Approval
+scale 1.0
+
+' ========================================
+' WORKFLOW AGGREGATE
+' ========================================
+
+package "Workflow Aggregate" <<aggregate>> {
+  
+  class WorkflowRun <<aggregate-root>> {
+    - id: string
+    - projectId: string
+    - workflowDefId: string
+    - status: WorkflowStatus
+    - currentNodeId: string | null
+    - context: JSON
+    - startedAt: DateTime
+    - completedAt: DateTime | null
+    --
+    **Lifecycle Methods**
+    + create(projectId: string, workflowDefId: string): WorkflowRun
+    + start(): void
+    + pause(): void
+    + resume(): void
+    + cancel(): void
+    + complete(): void
+    + fail(error: Error): void
+    --
+    **Step Management**
+    + addStep(step: WorkflowStep): void
+    + getSteps(): WorkflowStep[]
+    + getCurrentStep(): WorkflowStep | null
+    + executeStep(nodeId: string): Promise<void>
+    + moveToNextStep(): void
+    --
+    **Approval Management**
+    + requestApproval(data: ApprovalData): Promise<Approval>
+    + getPendingApprovals(): Approval[]
+    + handleApprovalResponse(approvalId: string, approved: boolean): void
+    --
+    **Context Management**
+    + setVariable(key: string, value: any): void
+    + getVariable(key: string): any
+    + updateContext(data: object): void
+    + getContext(): JSON
+    --
+    **Status Queries**
+    + isRunning(): boolean
+    + isPaused(): boolean
+    + isCompleted(): boolean
+    + isFailed(): boolean
+    + canResume(): boolean
+    + canExecuteNextStep(): boolean
+    --
+    **Progress Tracking**
+    + calculateProgress(): number
+    + getExecutionDuration(): number
+    + getStepCount(): number
+    + getCompletedStepCount(): number
+    --
+    **Validation**
+    + validate(): boolean
+    + canTransitionTo(status: WorkflowStatus): boolean
+    --
+    **Domain Events Emitted**
+    ~ emitWorkflowStarted(): void
+    ~ emitWorkflowStepCompleted(step: WorkflowStep): void
+    ~ emitWorkflowCompleted(): void
+    ~ emitWorkflowFailed(error: Error): void
+  }
+  
+  class WorkflowStep <<entity>> {
+    - id: string
+    - workflowRunId: string
+    - taskId: string | null
+    - nodeId: string
+    - agentRole: AgentRole | null
+    - status: StepStatus
+    - input: JSON | null
+    - output: JSON | null
+    - error: string | null
+    - retryCount: number
+    - startedAt: DateTime | null
+    - completedAt: DateTime | null
+    - durationMs: number | null
+    --
+    **Lifecycle Methods**
+    + create(data: StepData): WorkflowStep
+    + start(): void
+    + complete(output: JSON): void
+    + fail(error: Error): void
+    + skip(reason: string): void
+    --
+    **Retry Management**
+    + retry(): void
+    + canRetry(): boolean
+    + getMaxRetries(): number
+    + incrementRetryCount(): void
+    --
+    **Input/Output**
+    + setInput(input: JSON): void
+    + getInput(): JSON | null
+    + setOutput(output: JSON): void
+    + getOutput(): JSON | null
+    --
+    **Status Queries**
+    + isPending(): boolean
+    + isRunning(): boolean
+    + isCompleted(): boolean
+    + isFailed(): boolean
+    + isSkipped(): boolean
+    --
+    **Duration Tracking**
+    + calculateDuration(): number
+    + getDurationMs(): number | null
+    --
+    **Validation**
+    + validate(): boolean
+    + validateTimestamps(): boolean
+    --
+    **Domain Events Emitted**
+    ~ emitStepStarted(): void
+    ~ emitStepCompleted(): void
+    ~ emitStepFailed(): void
+  }
+  
+  class Approval <<entity>> {
+    - id: string
+    - workflowRunId: string
+    - userId: string
+    - approvalType: string
+    - status: ApprovalStatus
+    - requestData: JSON
+    - comment: string | null
+    - requestedAt: DateTime
+    - respondedAt: DateTime | null
+    --
+    **Lifecycle Methods**
+    + create(data: ApprovalData): Approval
+    + approve(userId: string, comment?: string): void
+    + reject(userId: string, reason: string): void
+    --
+    **Status Queries**
+    + isPending(): boolean
+    + isApproved(): boolean
+    + isRejected(): boolean
+    + isResolved(): boolean
+    --
+    **User Validation**
+    + canBeApprovedBy(userId: string): boolean
+    + validateUser(userId: string): boolean
+    --
+    **Expiration**
+    + isExpired(): boolean
+    + setExpiration(duration: number): void
+    + getTimeUntilExpiration(): number
+    --
+    **Response Time**
+    + getResponseTime(): number | null
+    + calculateResponseDuration(): number
+    --
+    **Validation**
+    + validate(): boolean
+    + canTransitionTo(status: ApprovalStatus): boolean
+    --
+    **Domain Events Emitted**
+    ~ emitApprovalRequested(): void
+    ~ emitApprovalGranted(): void
+    ~ emitApprovalRejected(): void
+  }
+  
+  enum WorkflowStatus <<value-object>> {
+    running
+    paused
+    completed
+    failed
+    cancelled
+    --
+    + isTerminal(): boolean
+    + canTransitionTo(target: WorkflowStatus): boolean
+    + getValidTransitions(): WorkflowStatus[]
+  }
+  
+  enum StepStatus <<value-object>> {
+    pending
+    running
+    completed
+    failed
+    skipped
+    --
+    + isTerminal(): boolean
+    + canRetry(): boolean
+  }
+  
+  enum ApprovalStatus <<value-object>> {
+    pending
+    approved
+    rejected
+    --
+    **One-way Transition Rule (Req 17.7)**
+    + canTransitionTo(target: ApprovalStatus): boolean
+  }
+}
+
+' ========================================
+' WORKFLOW ENGINE SERVICE
+' ========================================
+
+package "Workflow Services" <<service>> {
+  
+  class WorkflowEngine {
+    - workflowRepository: IWorkflowRepository
+    - agentFactory: AgentFactory
+    - eventBus: EventBus
+    --
+    **Execution**
+    + executeWorkflow(workflowRunId: string): Promise<void>
+    + executeStep(stepId: string): Promise<void>
+    + handleStepCompletion(stepId: string, output: JSON): void
+    + handleStepFailure(stepId: string, error: Error): void
+    --
+    **Orchestration**
+    + determineNextStep(workflowRun: WorkflowRun): WorkflowStep | null
+    + canExecuteStep(step: WorkflowStep): boolean
+    + scheduleStep(step: WorkflowStep): void
+    --
+    **Approval Handling**
+    + waitForApproval(approvalId: string): Promise<boolean>
+    + processApprovalResponse(approvalId: string, approved: boolean): void
+    --
+    **Error Handling**
+    + handleWorkflowError(workflowRunId: string, error: Error): void
+    + retryFailedStep(stepId: string): Promise<void>
+  }
+  
+  class WorkflowStateMachine {
+    - currentStatus: WorkflowStatus
+    - transitions: Map<WorkflowStatus, WorkflowStatus[]>
+    --
+    + getCurrentStatus(): WorkflowStatus
+    + canTransitionTo(target: WorkflowStatus): boolean
+    + transitionTo(target: WorkflowStatus): void
+    + getValidTransitions(): WorkflowStatus[]
+  }
+}
+
+' ========================================
+' RELATIONSHIPS
+' ========================================
+
+' Aggregate composition
+WorkflowRun "1" *-- "1..*" WorkflowStep : contains
+WorkflowRun "1" *-- "0..*" Approval : requests
+
+' Value object associations
+WorkflowRun --> WorkflowStatus : has
+WorkflowStep --> StepStatus : has
+Approval --> ApprovalStatus : has
+
+' External references
+WorkflowRun --> Project : executes for
+WorkflowStep --> Task : executes
+WorkflowStep --> Agent : assigned to
+Approval --> User : requests from
+
+' Service dependencies
+WorkflowEngine --> WorkflowRun : orchestrates
+WorkflowEngine ..> WorkflowStateMachine : uses
+WorkflowEngine --> AgentFactory : creates agents
+WorkflowEngine --> EventBus : publishes events
+
+' Domain Events
+WorkflowRun ..> WorkflowStartedEvent : emits
+WorkflowRun ..> WorkflowStepCompletedEvent : emits
+WorkflowRun ..> WorkflowCompletedEvent : emits
+WorkflowRun ..> WorkflowFailedEvent : emits
+WorkflowStep ..> StepStartedEvent : emits
+WorkflowStep ..> StepCompletedEvent : emits
+WorkflowStep ..> StepFailedEvent : emits
+Approval ..> ApprovalRequestedEvent : emits
+Approval ..> ApprovalGrantedEvent : emits
+Approval ..> ApprovalRejectedEvent : emits
+
+' ========================================
+' BUSINESS RULES
+' ========================================
+
+note bottom of WorkflowRun
+  **Aggregate Root Invariants**:
+  
+  1. Workflow must be associated with valid project
+  2. Status transitions must be valid:
+     - running ΓåÆ paused | completed | failed | cancelled
+     - paused ΓåÆ running | cancelled
+     - Terminal states (completed, failed, cancelled) are final
+  3. Cannot resume workflow that isn't paused
+  4. Cannot modify completed or failed workflows (Req 16)
+  5. CompletedAt must be after startedAt when both set
+  6. All steps must complete for workflow completion
+  7. Failed step can trigger workflow failure
+  8. Approval blocks must be resolved before continuation
+  
+  **Consistency Boundary**:
+  - All steps belong to exactly one workflow run
+  - All approvals belong to exactly one workflow run
+  - Steps execute in order defined by workflow definition
+  - Context is shared across all steps in workflow
+end note
+
+note bottom of WorkflowStep
+  **Entity Invariants**:
+  
+  1. Step must belong to valid workflow run
+  2. CompletedAt must be after startedAt when both set (Req 17.6)
+  3. Duration must match timestamps when all are set
+  4. Cannot retry after max retry limit (default: 3)
+  5. Retry count must be non-negative
+  6. Input must be valid JSON when provided
+  7. Output required for completed status
+  8. Error message required for failed status
+  9. Status transitions:
+     - pending ΓåÆ running ΓåÆ completed | failed
+     - pending ΓåÆ skipped (conditional)
+     - failed ΓåÆ pending (retry)
+end note
+
+note bottom of Approval
+  **Entity Invariants** (Req 17.7):
+  
+  1. Status can ONLY transition:
+     - pending ΓåÆ approved
+     - pending ΓåÆ rejected
+  2. Cannot change status after approval/rejection
+  3. RespondedAt must be after requestedAt
+  4. Only assigned user can approve/reject
+  5. Comment required for rejection
+  6. Request data must be valid JSON
+  7. Approval type must be recognized by system
+  
+  **One-way Transition Rule**:
+  This ensures decision finality and audit trail integrity.
+  Once approved or rejected, status is immutable.
+end note
+
+note bottom of WorkflowStatus
+  **Valid Workflow Status Transitions**:
+  
+  running ΓåÆ paused
+  running ΓåÆ completed
+  running ΓåÆ failed
+  running ΓåÆ cancelled
+  paused ΓåÆ running
+  paused ΓåÆ cancelled
+  
+  **Terminal States** (no outgoing transitions):
+  - completed
+  - failed
+  - cancelled
+end note
+
+note bottom of ApprovalStatus
+  **One-Way Approval Transition (Req 17.7)**:
+  
+  pending ΓåÆ approved (one way)
+  pending ΓåÆ rejected (one way)
+  
+  **No Reverse Transitions**:
+  Once approved or rejected, cannot change.
+  This ensures:
+  - Audit trail integrity
+  - Decision finality
+  - Compliance with approval process
+end note
+
+' ========================================
+' DOMAIN EVENTS
+' ========================================
+
+class WorkflowStartedEvent <<domain-event>> {
+  + eventId: string
+  + eventType: 'WorkflowStarted'
+  + workflowRunId: string
+  + projectId: string
+  + workflowDefId: string
+  + timestamp: DateTime
+}
+
+class WorkflowStepCompletedEvent <<domain-event>> {
+  + eventId: string
+  + eventType: 'WorkflowStepCompleted'
+  + workflowRunId: string
+  + stepId: string
+  + nodeId: string
+  + agentRole: AgentRole | null
+  + output: JSON
+  + durationMs: number
+  + timestamp: DateTime
+}
+
+class WorkflowCompletedEvent <<domain-event>> {
+  + eventId: string
+  + eventType: 'WorkflowCompleted'
+  + workflowRunId: string
+  + projectId: string
+  + totalDurationMs: number
+  + totalSteps: number
+  + timestamp: DateTime
+}
+
+class WorkflowFailedEvent <<domain-event>> {
+  + eventId: string
+  + eventType: 'WorkflowFailed'
+  + workflowRunId: string
+  + projectId: string
+  + failedStepId: string
+  + error: string
+  + timestamp: DateTime
+}
+
+class ApprovalRequestedEvent <<domain-event>> {
+  + eventId: string
+  + eventType: 'ApprovalRequested'
+  + approvalId: string
+  + workflowRunId: string
+  + userId: string
+  + approvalType: string
+  + requestData: JSON
+  + timestamp: DateTime
+}
+
+class ApprovalGrantedEvent <<domain-event>> {
+  + eventId: string
+  + eventType: 'ApprovalGranted'
+  + approvalId: string
+  + workflowRunId: string
+  + approvedBy: string
+  + comment: string | null
+  + timestamp: DateTime
+}
+
+class ApprovalRejectedEvent <<domain-event>> {
+  + eventId: string
+  + eventType: 'ApprovalRejected'
+  + approvalId: string
+  + workflowRunId: string
+  + rejectedBy: string
+  + reason: string
+  + timestamp: DateTime
+}
+
+' ========================================
+' WORKFLOW EXECUTION FLOW
+' ========================================
+
+note right of WorkflowEngine
+  **Workflow Execution Flow**:
+  
+  1. Create WorkflowRun
+  2. Load workflow definition
+  3. Create initial WorkflowSteps from nodes
+  4. Start execution:
+     a. Get next pending step
+     b. Check if step is approval node
+        - If yes: Create Approval, wait for response
+        - If no: Execute step with assigned agent
+     c. Handle step result (success/failure)
+     d. Update workflow context with outputs
+     e. Move to next step or complete workflow
+  5. Emit domain events at each stage
+  6. Handle errors with retry logic
+  
+  **Error Handling**:
+  - Step failure ΓåÆ retry (up to max retries)
+  - Max retries exceeded ΓåÆ workflow failure
+  - Critical error ΓåÆ immediate workflow failure
+  - Approval rejection ΓåÆ workflow failure or branch
+end note
+
+@enduml

+@startuml SD-001: Project Creation Workflow
+!theme plain
+skinparam sequenceMessageAlign center
+skinparam responseMessageBelowArrow true
+
+title SD-001: Project Creation Workflow
+subtitle Multi-Agent Enterprise Workflow Automation System
+
+actor "Board Member" as BoardMember #LightBlue
+participant "AuthService" as Auth #LightGreen
+participant "ProjectController" as Controller #LightYellow
+participant "ProjectService" as ProjectSvc #LightYellow
+participant "PrismaDB" as DB #LightGray
+participant "WorkflowEngine" as Engine #Orange
+participant "CEOAgent" as CEO #Pink
+participant "PMAgent" as PM #Pink
+participant "WebSocket" as WS #LightCyan
+
+== Authentication & Project Creation ==
+
+BoardMember -> Auth: POST /api/auth/login(email, password)
+activate Auth
+Auth -> DB: findUser(email)
+activate DB
+DB --> Auth: User
+deactivate DB
+Auth -> Auth: validatePassword()
+Auth --> BoardMember: JWT token
+deactivate Auth
+
+BoardMember -> Controller: POST /api/projects(name, description, goal, budget)
+activate Controller
+note right of Controller: **Design Pattern: Template Method**\nController defines the skeleton,\nservice implements business logic
+
+Controller -> Controller: sanitizeInput()
+Controller -> ProjectSvc: createProject(data)
+activate ProjectSvc
+
+ProjectSvc -> ProjectSvc: validate(name, description, goal)
+note right of ProjectSvc: **Business Rules:**\n- Name: 3-200 chars\n- Budget > 0 if provided\n- Name unique per user
+
+ProjectSvc -> DB: project.create({...})
+activate DB
+DB --> ProjectSvc: Project { id, name, status: 'active' }
+deactivate DB
+
+== Workflow Initialization ==
+
+ProjectSvc -> Engine: startWorkflow(projectId, 'default_workflow', context)
+activate Engine
+note right of Engine: **Design Pattern: State Machine**\nWorkflow transitions through states:\nPENDING ΓåÆ RUNNING ΓåÆ COMPLETED
+
+Engine -> DB: workflowRun.create({projectId, status: 'running', currentNodeId: 'ceo_plan'})
+activate DB
+DB --> Engine: WorkflowRun { id, status: 'running' }
+deactivate DB
+
+Engine -> WS: sendToProject(projectId, WorkflowStateChangedEvent)
+activate WS
+WS -->> BoardMember: WebSocket: workflow:state_changed
+deactivate WS
+
+Engine ->> Engine: processCurrentNode(runId)
+note right of Engine: **Asynchronous Processing:**\nWorkflow execution continues\nin background via BullMQ job
+
+== CEO Agent Strategic Planning ==
+
+Engine -> CEO: execute(context)
+activate CEO
+note right of CEO: **Design Pattern: Strategy Pattern**\nCEO uses ThinkingStrategy\nand ActionStrategy
+
+CEO -> CEO: think(context)
+note right of CEO: **Agent Think Phase:**\nLoad semantic memories,\nanalyze project goal
+
+CEO -> DB: searchMemories(agentRole='CEO', namespace='strategy', query=goal)
+activate DB
+DB --> CEO: MemorySearchResult[]
+deactivate DB
+
+CEO -> CEO: callLLM({messages: [...], model: 'gpt-4'})
+note right of CEO: **LLM Integration:**\nCircuit breaker pattern\nwith fallback models
+
+CEO -> WS: emitThinking(projectId, 'Analyzing project goals...')
+WS -->> BoardMember: WebSocket: agent:thinking
+
+CEO -> CEO: act(context)
+note right of CEO: **Agent Act Phase:**\nGenerate milestones,\nstore results
+
+CEO -> DB: milestone.createMany([...])
+activate DB
+DB --> CEO: Milestone[] (2-4 milestones)
+deactivate DB
+
+CEO -> DB: saveMemory(agentId, namespace='strategy', content='Created milestones for: ...')
+activate DB
+DB --> CEO: memoryId
+deactivate DB
+
+CEO --> Engine: { thought: {...}, action: { action: 'planMilestones', output: [...] } }
+deactivate CEO
+
+Engine -> WS: sendToProject(projectId, AgentActionEvent)
+WS -->> BoardMember: WebSocket: agent:action
+
+== PM Agent Task Breakdown ==
+
+Engine -> Engine: transitionToNextNode(runId, 'onSuccess')
+Engine -> DB: workflowRun.update({currentNodeId: 'pm_breakdown'})
+activate DB
+DB --> Engine: Updated
+deactivate DB
+
+Engine -> PM: execute(context)
+activate PM
+
+PM -> PM: think(context)
+note right of PM: **PM loads CEO milestones**\nand breaks them into tasks
+
+PM -> DB: milestone.findMany({projectId})
+activate DB
+DB --> PM: Milestone[]
+deactivate DB
+
+PM -> PM: callLLM({messages: [...], model: 'gpt-3.5-turbo'})
+PM -> WS: emitThinking(projectId, 'Breaking down milestones into tasks...')
+WS -->> BoardMember: WebSocket: agent:thinking
+
+PM -> PM: act(context)
+
+PM -> DB: task.createMany([...])
+activate DB
+DB --> PM: Task[] (5-20 tasks per milestone)
+deactivate DB
+
+PM --> Engine: { thought: {...}, action: { action: 'breakdownTasks', output: [...] } }
+deactivate PM
+
+== Completion & Notification ==
+
+Engine -> Engine: transitionToNextNode(runId, 'onSuccess')
+Engine -> DB: workflowRun.update({status: 'completed', completedAt: now()})
+activate DB
+DB --> Engine: Updated
+deactivate DB
+
+Engine -> WS: sendToProject(projectId, WorkflowStateChangedEvent)
+WS -->> BoardMember: WebSocket: workflow:state_changed { status: 'completed' }
+
+Engine --> ProjectSvc: WorkflowRun { status: 'completed' }
+deactivate Engine
+
+ProjectSvc --> Controller: Project { id, name, status: 'active' }
+deactivate ProjectSvc
+
+Controller --> BoardMember: 201 Created { project: {...}, workflowRunId: '...' }
+deactivate Controller
+
+note over BoardMember, WS: **Design Patterns Used:**\n- **Template Method**: Controller ΓåÆ Service flow\n- **State Machine**: Workflow state transitions\n- **Strategy Pattern**: Agent thinking/acting strategies\n- **Observer Pattern**: WebSocket event broadcasting\n- **Factory Pattern**: LLMProviderFactory creates providers\n- **Repository Pattern**: PrismaService abstracts data access
+
+@enduml

+@startuml SD-002: Agent Think-Act Cycle
+!theme plain
+skinparam sequenceMessageAlign center
+skinparam responseMessageBelowArrow true
+
+title SD-002: Agent Think-Act Cycle
+subtitle Demonstrating State Machine, Strategy, and Observer Patterns
+
+participant "WorkflowEngine" as Engine #Orange
+participant "BaseAgent" as Agent #Pink
+participant "AgentStateMachine" as StateMachine #LightGreen
+participant "ThinkingStrategy" as ThinkStrategy #LightYellow
+participant "ActionStrategy" as ActStrategy #LightYellow
+participant "LLMProvider" as LLM #LightBlue
+participant "ToolExecutor" as Tool #LightGray
+participant "AgentEventEmitter" as Emitter #LightCyan
+participant "Observer" as Observer #LightCyan
+
+== Workflow Node Execution Trigger ==
+
+Engine -> Agent: execute(context)
+activate Agent
+note right of Agent: **Design Pattern: Template Method**\nexecute() defines skeleton:\n1. think()\n2. act()\n3. return result
+
+== State Transition: IDLE ΓåÆ THINKING ==
+
+Agent -> StateMachine: transitionTo(AgentState.THINKING)
+activate StateMachine
+note right of StateMachine: **Design Pattern: State Pattern**\nValidates transition rules:\n- IDLE ΓåÆ THINKING: valid\n- IDLE ΓåÆ ACTING: invalid
+
+StateMachine -> StateMachine: validateTransition(IDLE, THINKING)
+note right of StateMachine: **State Transition Rules:**\nIDLE ΓåÆ THINKING ΓåÆ ACTING ΓåÆ COMPLETED\nAny state ΓåÆ ERROR (on failure)\nWAITING_APPROVAL ΓåÆ THINKING (on approval)
+
+StateMachine -> StateMachine: currentState = THINKING
+StateMachine -> StateMachine: history.push({from: IDLE, to: THINKING, timestamp})
+
+StateMachine -> Emitter: emit(StateChangedEvent)
+activate Emitter
+Emitter -> Observer: onStateChanged(event)
+activate Observer
+note right of Observer: **Design Pattern: Observer**\nObservers (WebSocket, Logger, Metrics)\nreact to agent state changes
+Observer --> Emitter: acknowledged
+deactivate Observer
+deactivate Emitter
+
+StateMachine --> Agent: transitionSuccess
+deactivate StateMachine
+
+== Think Phase: Strategy Pattern ==
+
+Agent -> ThinkStrategy: think(context)
+activate ThinkStrategy
+note right of ThinkStrategy: **Design Pattern: Strategy Pattern**\nEach agent role has custom\nthinking strategy:\n- CEOThinkingStrategy\n- PMThinkingStrategy\n- DevThinkingStrategy
+
+ThinkStrategy -> ThinkStrategy: analyzeContext(context)
+note right of ThinkStrategy: **Thinking Process:**\n1. Load semantic memories\n2. Identify key constraints\n3. Formulate reasoning prompt
+
+ThinkStrategy -> Agent: loadMemory(namespace, query, limit=5)
+activate Agent
+Agent -> Agent: memoryService.searchMemories(...)
+Agent --> ThinkStrategy: MemorySearchResult[] (top 5 by similarity)
+deactivate Agent
+
+ThinkStrategy -> LLM: chat({messages: [...], model: 'gpt-4', maxTokens: 2000})
+activate LLM
+note right of LLM: **External Boundary:**\nLLM Gateway (9Router)\nwith circuit breaker\nand fallback models
+
+LLM -> LLM: processRequest()
+note right of LLM: **Circuit Breaker States:**\n- CLOSED: normal operation\n- OPEN: fallback to secondary model\n- HALF_OPEN: testing recovery
+
+LLM --> ThinkStrategy: ChatResult { content, tokenUsage, actualModelUsed }
+deactivate LLM
+
+ThinkStrategy -> ThinkStrategy: parseThought(llmResponse)
+ThinkStrategy --> Agent: ThinkingResult { reasoning, nextAction, confidence }
+deactivate ThinkStrategy
+
+Agent -> Emitter: emit(ThinkingEvent)
+activate Emitter
+Emitter -> Observer: onThinking(event)
+activate Observer
+Observer --> Emitter: acknowledged
+deactivate Observer
+deactivate Emitter
+
+== State Transition: THINKING ΓåÆ ACTING ==
+
+Agent -> StateMachine: transitionTo(AgentState.ACTING)
+activate StateMachine
+StateMachine -> StateMachine: validateTransition(THINKING, ACTING)
+StateMachine -> StateMachine: currentState = ACTING
+StateMachine -> Emitter: emit(StateChangedEvent)
+activate Emitter
+Emitter -> Observer: onStateChanged(event)
+activate Observer
+Observer --> Emitter: acknowledged
+deactivate Observer
+deactivate Emitter
+StateMachine --> Agent: transitionSuccess
+deactivate StateMachine
+
+== Act Phase: Strategy Pattern ==
+
+Agent -> ActStrategy: act(thought, context)
+activate ActStrategy
+note right of ActStrategy: **Design Pattern: Strategy Pattern**\nAction strategies vary by agent:\n- CEO: create milestones\n- PM: breakdown tasks\n- DEV: write code in sandbox
+
+ActStrategy -> ActStrategy: determineTools(thought)
+note right of ActStrategy: **Tool Selection:**\nBased on agent role\nand task requirements
+
+ActStrategy -> Agent: executeTool(toolName, command, params)
+activate Agent
+
+Agent -> Agent: toolRegistry.isToolAuthorizedForRole(toolName, agentRole)
+note right of Agent: **Security Check:**\nRole-Based Tool Access Control\n- DEV: can use 'code_execution'\n- QA: can use 'static_analysis'\n- CEO: cannot use 'code_execution'
+
+Agent -> Agent: validateParameters(params, toolSchema)
+
+Agent -> Tool: executeInSandbox(toolName, command, resourceLimits)
+activate Tool
+note right of Tool: **Sandbox Isolation:**\nDocker container (node:18-alpine)\nResource limits:\n- 256MB RAM\n- 50% CPU\n- 30s timeout
+
+Tool -> Tool: spawnDockerContainer()
+Tool -> Tool: runCommandInContainer()
+Tool -> Tool: captureOutput()
+Tool -> Tool: sanitizeOutput()
+Tool --> Agent: ToolResult { success, output, executionTimeMs, resourceUsage }
+deactivate Tool
+
+Agent --> ActStrategy: ToolResult
+deactivate Agent
+
+ActStrategy -> ActStrategy: interpretToolResult(result)
+
+ActStrategy -> LLM: chat({messages: [thought, toolResult, 'Summarize outcome'], model: 'gpt-3.5-turbo'})
+activate LLM
+LLM --> ActStrategy: ChatResult { content: 'Task completed successfully...' }
+deactivate LLM
+
+ActStrategy --> Agent: ActionResult { action: 'createMilestones', output: {...}, status: 'success' }
+deactivate ActStrategy
+
+Agent -> Emitter: emit(ActionEvent)
+activate Emitter
+Emitter -> Observer: onAction(event)
+activate Observer
+Observer --> Emitter: acknowledged
+deactivate Observer
+deactivate Emitter
+
+== State Transition: ACTING ΓåÆ COMPLETED ==
+
+Agent -> StateMachine: transitionTo(AgentState.COMPLETED)
+activate StateMachine
+StateMachine -> StateMachine: validateTransition(ACTING, COMPLETED)
+StateMachine -> StateMachine: currentState = COMPLETED
+StateMachine -> Emitter: emit(StateChangedEvent)
+activate Emitter
+Emitter -> Observer: onStateChanged(event)
+activate Observer
+note right of Observer: **Observer Actions:**\n- WebSocket: notify frontend\n- Logger: structured log\n- Metrics: record duration\n- Memory: save lesson learned
+Observer --> Emitter: acknowledged
+deactivate Observer
+deactivate Emitter
+StateMachine --> Agent: transitionSuccess
+deactivate StateMachine
+
+== Return Result ==
+
+Agent -> Agent: saveMemory(namespace, content='Completed task: ...', importance=8)
+note right of Agent: **Learning Cycle:**\nAgent stores experience\nfor future context retrieval
+
+Agent --> Engine: AgentResult { thought, action, state: COMPLETED }
+deactivate Agent
+
+note over Engine, Observer: **Design Patterns Summary:**\n- **Template Method**: BaseAgent.execute() defines think ΓåÆ act skeleton\n- **State Pattern**: AgentStateMachine validates transitions with rules\n- **Strategy Pattern**: ThinkingStrategy & ActionStrategy (interface-based polymorphism)\n- **Observer Pattern**: AgentEventEmitter notifies multiple observers\n- **Circuit Breaker**: LLMProvider handles external service failures\n- **Factory Pattern**: ToolRegistry.getTool() creates tool executors
+
+note over Engine, Observer: **Timing Constraints:**\n- Think phase: < 10 seconds (LLM timeout)\n- Act phase: < 30 seconds (sandbox timeout)\n- Total execution: < 60 seconds per agent\n- State transitions: atomic (database transaction)
+
+@enduml

+@startuml SD-003: Human Approval Flow
+!theme plain
+skinparam sequenceMessageAlign center
+skinparam responseMessageBelowArrow true
+
+title SD-003: Human Approval Flow
+subtitle Human-in-the-Loop with State Pattern and Real-Time Notifications
+
+participant "WorkflowEngine" as Engine #Orange
+participant "ApprovalService" as ApprovalSvc #LightYellow
+participant "WebSocketGateway" as WS #LightCyan
+participant "BoardMember" as User #LightBlue
+participant "ApprovalController" as Controller #LightYellow
+participant "PrismaDB" as DB #LightGray
+
+== Workflow Reaches Approval Node ==
+
+Engine -> Engine: processCurrentNode(runId)
+activate Engine
+note right of Engine: **Current Node Type:**\nhuman_approval\nRequires explicit user decision
+
+Engine -> Engine: getCurrentNode(runId)
+Engine -> Engine: node.type == 'human_approval'
+
+Engine -> ApprovalSvc: createApprovalRequest({workflowRunId, userId, approvalType, requestData})
+activate ApprovalSvc
+
+ApprovalSvc -> DB: workflowRun.findUnique({id: workflowRunId})
+activate DB
+DB --> ApprovalSvc: WorkflowRun { projectId, status: 'running' }
+deactivate DB
+
+== Create Approval Record ==
+
+ApprovalSvc -> DB: approval.create({workflowRunId, userId, approvalType, requestData, status: 'pending'})
+activate DB
+note right of DB: **Design Pattern: State Pattern**\nApproval has 3 states:\n- pending\n- approved\n- rejected\n\n**State Transition Rules:**\npending ΓåÆ approved (valid)\npending ΓåÆ rejected (valid)\napproved ΓåÆ rejected (INVALID)\nrejected ΓåÆ approved (INVALID)
+
+DB --> ApprovalSvc: Approval { id, status: 'pending', requestedAt }
+deactivate DB
+
+== Real-Time Notification to User ==
+
+ApprovalSvc -> WS: sendToUser(userId, HumanApprovalRequiredEvent)
+activate WS
+note right of WS: **WebSocket Event:**\ntype: HUMAN_APPROVAL_REQUIRED\ndata: {\n  approvalId, workflowRunId,\n  projectId, approvalType,\n  requestData, requestedAt\n}
+
+WS -> User: WebSocket: human_approval_required
+activate User
+note right of User: **Frontend Behavior:**\n1. Display notification badge\n2. Show approval modal\n3. Render requestData\n4. Enable Approve/Reject buttons
+
+User --> WS: acknowledged (WebSocket ACK)
+deactivate User
+deactivate WS
+
+ApprovalSvc --> Engine: Approval { id, status: 'pending' }
+deactivate ApprovalSvc
+
+== Workflow Pauses ==
+
+Engine -> Engine: pauseWorkflow(runId)
+note right of Engine: **State Transition:**\nWorkflowStatus: running ΓåÆ paused\n\n**Design Pattern: State Machine**\nPaused state prevents further\nnode execution until resumed
+
+Engine -> Engine: saveWorkflowState(runId)
+note right of Engine: **State Persistence:**\n- currentNodeId saved\n- context variables saved\n- execution stack saved\n\n**Enables crash recovery**
+
+Engine -> DB: workflowRun.update({id: runId, status: 'paused'})
+activate DB
+DB --> Engine: Updated
+deactivate DB
+
+Engine -> WS: sendToProject(projectId, WorkflowStateChangedEvent)
+activate WS
+WS -->> User: WebSocket: workflow:state_changed { status: 'paused' }
+deactivate WS
+
+Engine --> Engine: Workflow paused, awaiting human decision
+deactivate Engine
+
+note over Engine, User: **Timing Note:**\nWorkflow can remain paused indefinitely\nNo timeout enforced (human decision required)
+
+== User Reviews Approval Request ==
+
+User -> User: Review approval data
+note right of User: **Approval Context:**\n- Project summary\n- Agent recommendations\n- Cost implications\n- Risk assessment
+
+User -> User: Make decision (Approve / Reject)
+
+User -> Controller: POST /api/approvals/:id/respond { status: 'approved', comment: 'Looks good' }
+activate Controller
+activate User
+
+Controller -> Controller: authenticate(jwt)
+Controller -> Controller: validateInput(status Γêê ['approved', 'rejected'])
+
+== Handle Approval Response ==
+
+Controller -> ApprovalSvc: handleApprovalResponse(approvalId, status, comment)
+activate ApprovalSvc
+
+ApprovalSvc -> DB: approval.findUnique({id: approvalId})
+activate DB
+DB --> ApprovalSvc: Approval { id, status: 'pending', workflowRunId }
+deactivate DB
+
+== Validate State Transition ==
+
+ApprovalSvc -> ApprovalSvc: validateTransition(currentStatus='pending', newStatus='approved')
+note right of ApprovalSvc: **Business Rule:**\nRequirement 17.7 states:\n"Approval can only transition\nfrom 'pending' to 'approved'\nor 'rejected'"\n\n**State Pattern Enforcement:**\nif (currentStatus != 'pending')\n  throw Error('Invalid transition')
+
+alt Current Status is 'pending' (Valid)
+    ApprovalSvc -> DB: approval.update({id, status: 'approved', comment, respondedAt: now()})
+    activate DB
+    DB --> ApprovalSvc: Approval { status: 'approved', respondedAt }
+    deactivate DB
+    
+else Current Status is NOT 'pending' (Invalid)
+    ApprovalSvc --> Controller: Error: Cannot transition from '${currentStatus}'
+    Controller --> User: 400 Bad Request { error: 'Invalid state transition' }
+    deactivate User
+end
+
+== Resume Workflow ==
+
+ApprovalSvc -> Engine: resumeWorkflow(runId, skipProcessNode=true)
+activate Engine
+note right of Engine: **skipProcessNode=true:**\nDon't re-process approval node,\ninstead transition to next node
+
+Engine -> DB: workflowRun.update({id: runId, status: 'running'})
+activate DB
+DB --> Engine: Updated
+deactivate DB
+
+Engine -> WS: sendToProject(projectId, WorkflowStateChangedEvent)
+activate WS
+WS -->> User: WebSocket: workflow:state_changed { status: 'running' }
+deactivate WS
+
+== Conditional Edge Transition ==
+
+ApprovalSvc -> Engine: transitionToNextNode(runId, edgeCondition='onApprove')
+note right of Engine: **Design Pattern: State Machine**\nWorkflow edges have conditions:\n- 'onApprove' ΓåÆ continue to success path\n- 'onReject' ΓåÆ transition to fallback/abort
+
+Engine -> Engine: findOutgoingEdges(currentNodeId, condition='onApprove')
+note right of Engine: **Edge Resolution:**\nWorkflowEdge[] filtered by:\n1. from === currentNodeId\n2. condition === 'onApprove'
+
+Engine -> DB: workflowRun.update({currentNodeId: nextNodeId, context: {..., approval_result: 'approved'}})
+activate DB
+DB --> Engine: Updated
+deactivate DB
+
+Engine -> WS: sendToProject(projectId, WorkflowStateChangedEvent)
+activate WS
+WS -->> User: WebSocket: workflow:state_changed { currentNodeId: nextNodeId }
+deactivate WS
+
+== Continue Workflow Execution ==
+
+Engine -> Engine: processCurrentNode(runId)
+note right of Engine: **Asynchronous Processing:**\nWorkflow continues with next node\n(e.g., DevOps deployment step)
+
+Engine --> ApprovalSvc: Success
+deactivate Engine
+
+ApprovalSvc --> Controller: Approval { status: 'approved', respondedAt }
+deactivate ApprovalSvc
+
+Controller --> User: 200 OK { approval: {...}, workflowResumed: true }
+deactivate Controller
+deactivate User
+
+note over Engine, User: **Alternative Flow: Rejection**\nIf user rejects:\n1. edgeCondition = 'onReject'\n2. Workflow transitions to abort/rollback node\n3. Compensating transactions execute\n4. Workflow status ΓåÆ 'cancelled'
+
+note over Engine, User: **Design Patterns Used:**\n- **State Pattern**: Approval state transitions (pending ΓåÆ approved/rejected)\n- **State Machine**: Workflow pause/resume lifecycle\n- **Observer Pattern**: WebSocket broadcasts state changes\n- **Command Pattern**: ApprovalCommand encapsulates decision\n- **Memento Pattern**: saveWorkflowState() captures recovery point
+
+note over Engine, User: **Timing Constraints:**\n- Notification delivery: < 500ms (WebSocket)\n- State persistence: < 200ms (database write)\n- Workflow resume: < 1 second\n- No timeout on user decision (indefinite pause)
+
+note over Engine, User: **Key Invariants:**\n1. Only one pending approval per workflow run\n2. Approval status can only transition once\n3. Workflow cannot proceed until approval resolved\n4. All state changes are atomic (DB transaction)
+
+@enduml
