+@startuml SD-004: Task Delegation Chain
+!theme plain
+skinparam sequenceMessageAlign center
+skinparam responseMessageBelowArrow true
+
+title SD-004: Task Delegation Chain (Multi-Agent Hierarchy)
+subtitle Demonstrating Chain of Responsibility Pattern
+
+actor "Board Member" as User #LightBlue
+participant "WorkflowEngine" as Engine #Orange
+participant "CEOAgent" as CEO #Pink
+participant "PMAgent" as PM #Pink
+participant "BAAgent" as BA #LightGreen
+participant "ArchitectAgent" as Architect #LightGreen
+participant "DEVAgent" as DEV #LightGreen
+participant "QAAgent" as QA #LightGreen
+participant "DevOpsAgent" as DevOps #LightGreen
+participant "PrismaDB" as DB #LightGray
+
+== Project Initialization ==
+
+User -> Engine: startWorkflow(projectId, workflowDefId='enterprise_workflow', context)
+activate Engine
+activate User
+
+Engine -> DB: workflowRun.create({projectId, status: 'running', currentNodeId: 'ceo_strategic_planning'})
+activate DB
+DB --> Engine: WorkflowRun { id, status: 'running' }
+deactivate DB
+
+note right of Engine: **Design Pattern: Chain of Responsibility**\nEach agent in the chain:\n1. Processes the request\n2. Delegates to next agent\n3. Returns result upward
+
+== CEO: Strategic Analysis ==
+
+Engine -> CEO: execute({projectId, goal, description, budget})
+activate CEO
+
+CEO -> CEO: think(context)
+note right of CEO: **CEO Responsibilities:**\n1. Define strategic vision\n2. Identify high-level milestones\n3. Assess market fit\n4. Allocate budget to phases
+
+CEO -> CEO: callLLM('Analyze project goal and create strategic roadmap...')
+CEO -> CEO: act(context)
+
+CEO -> DB: milestone.createMany([{name: 'Requirements', ...}, {name: 'Design', ...}, {name: 'Implementation', ...}, {name: 'Testing', ...}, {name: 'Deployment', ...}])
+activate DB
+DB --> CEO: Milestone[] (5 milestones)
+deactivate DB
+
+CEO -> CEO: saveMemory(namespace='strategy', content='Created 5-phase roadmap for AI platform')
+
+CEO -> PM: delegate({task: 'breakdown_requirements', context: {milestones, goal}})
+note right of CEO: **Delegation Message:**\n{\n  fromAgent: 'CEO',\n  toAgent: 'PM',\n  taskType: 'breakdown_requirements',\n  context: {...},\n  priority: 'high'\n}
+
+CEO --> Engine: { action: 'strategic_plan_created', delegatedTo: 'PM' }
+deactivate CEO
+
+== PM: Sprint Planning ==
+
+Engine -> PM: execute({projectId, milestones, goal})
+activate PM
+
+PM -> PM: think(context)
+note right of PM: **PM Responsibilities:**\n1. Break milestones into tasks\n2. Estimate effort (story points)\n3. Prioritize backlog\n4. Identify dependencies
+
+PM -> DB: milestone.findMany({projectId})
+activate DB
+DB --> PM: Milestone[] (5 milestones)
+deactivate DB
+
+PM -> PM: callLLM('Create detailed tasks for each milestone with priorities...')
+PM -> PM: act(context)
+
+PM -> DB: task.createMany([{title: 'Gather user requirements', milestoneId, priority: 1, ...}, ...])
+activate DB
+DB --> PM: Task[] (20 tasks across milestones)
+deactivate DB
+
+PM -> BA: delegate({task: 'analyze_requirements', context: {tasks: tasksForRequirementsPhase}})
+note right of PM: **Delegation to BA:**\nPM identifies first milestone\nneeds requirements analysis\n\n**Chain continues:**\nCEO ΓåÆ PM ΓåÆ BA
+
+PM --> Engine: { action: 'tasks_created', delegatedTo: 'BA', taskCount: 20 }
+deactivate PM
+
+== BA: Requirements Analysis ==
+
+Engine -> BA: execute({projectId, tasks, goal, stakeholderInputs})
+activate BA
+
+BA -> BA: think(context)
+note right of BA: **BA Responsibilities (NEW):**\n1. Elicit functional requirements\n2. Elicit non-functional requirements\n3. Create use case diagrams\n4. Document acceptance criteria\n5. Identify constraints
+
+BA -> DB: task.findMany({projectId, milestoneId: requirementsMilestoneId})
+activate DB
+DB --> BA: Task[] (requirements phase tasks)
+deactivate DB
+
+BA -> BA: callLLM('Extract functional and non-functional requirements from project goal...')
+note right of BA: **LLM Prompt Includes:**\n- Project goal\n- Stakeholder inputs\n- Industry standards (ISO, IEEE)\n- Similar project patterns from memory
+
+BA -> BA: act(context)
+
+BA -> BA: generateUseCaseDiagram(requirements)
+BA -> BA: generateRequirementsSpec(requirements)
+
+BA -> DB: projectDocument.create({projectId, type: 'requirements_spec', content: {...}, format: 'markdown'})
+activate DB
+DB --> BA: ProjectDocument { id, type: 'requirements_spec' }
+deactivate DB
+
+BA -> BA: saveMemory(namespace='requirements', content='Documented 15 functional requirements for AI platform')
+
+BA -> Architect: delegate({task: 'design_architecture', context: {requirements, constraints}})
+note right of BA: **Delegation to Architect:**\nBA hands off complete\nrequirements specification\n\n**Chain continues:**\nCEO ΓåÆ PM ΓåÆ BA ΓåÆ Architect
+
+BA --> Engine: { action: 'requirements_documented', delegatedTo: 'Architect', requirementsCount: 15 }
+deactivate BA
+
+== Architect: System Design ==
+
+Engine -> Architect: execute({projectId, requirements, constraints})
+activate Architect
+
+Architect -> Architect: think(context)
+note right of Architect: **Architect Responsibilities (NEW):**\n1. Design system architecture\n2. Select technology stack\n3. Design component interfaces\n4. Write Architecture Decision Records\n5. Generate architecture diagrams
+
+Architect -> DB: projectDocument.findUnique({type: 'requirements_spec'})
+activate DB
+DB --> Architect: ProjectDocument { content: requirements }
+deactivate DB
+
+Architect -> Architect: callLLM('Design scalable architecture for multi-agent system with these requirements...')
+note right of Architect: **LLM Context Includes:**\n- Functional requirements\n- Non-functional requirements (scalability, security)\n- Technology constraints\n- Best practices from memory
+
+Architect -> Architect: act(context)
+
+Architect -> Architect: evaluateTechnologyOptions(['NestJS vs Express', 'PostgreSQL vs MongoDB', ...])
+Architect -> Architect: generateComponentDiagram(components)
+Architect -> Architect: generateDeploymentDiagram(infrastructure)
+Architect -> Architect: writeADR(decision='Use NestJS + PostgreSQL', rationale='...')
+
+Architect -> DB: projectDocument.createMany([{type: 'architecture_spec', ...}, {type: 'component_diagram', ...}, {type: 'adr', ...}])
+activate DB
+DB --> Architect: ProjectDocument[] (3 documents)
+deactivate DB
+
+Architect -> Architect: saveMemory(namespace='architecture', content='Designed microservices architecture with event-driven communication')
+
+Architect -> PM: delegateBack({task: 'create_implementation_tasks', context: {architecture, components}})
+note right of Architect: **Return to PM:**\nArchitect returns to PM\nfor implementation planning\n\n**Chain forks:**\nPM creates tasks for each component
+
+Architect --> Engine: { action: 'architecture_designed', delegatedTo: 'PM', componentsCount: 8 }
+deactivate Architect
+
+== PM: Implementation Planning ==
+
+Engine -> PM: execute({projectId, architecture, components})
+activate PM
+
+PM -> PM: think(context)
+note right of PM: **PM Re-Entry:**\nPM receives architecture\nand creates implementation tasks
+
+PM -> PM: callLLM('Break down components into development tasks with dependencies...')
+PM -> PM: act(context)
+
+PM -> DB: task.createMany([{title: 'Implement AuthService', component: 'auth', assignedTo: 'DEV', ...}, ...])
+activate DB
+DB --> PM: Task[] (implementation tasks per component)
+deactivate DB
+
+PM -> DEV: delegate({task: 'implement_component', context: {taskId, component: 'AuthService', spec: {...}}})
+note right of PM: **Delegation to DEV:**\nPM assigns first task to DEV\n\n**Chain continues:**\nCEO ΓåÆ PM ΓåÆ BA ΓåÆ Architect ΓåÆ PM ΓåÆ DEV
+
+PM --> Engine: { action: 'implementation_tasks_created', delegatedTo: 'DEV' }
+deactivate PM
+
+== DEV: Code Implementation ==
+
+Engine -> DEV: execute({projectId, taskId, componentSpec, codingConventions})
+activate DEV
+
+DEV -> DEV: think(context)
+note right of DEV: **DEV Responsibilities:**\n1. Implement component logic\n2. Write unit tests\n3. Follow coding conventions\n4. Document code
+
+DEV -> DB: task.findUnique({id: taskId})
+activate DB
+DB --> DEV: Task { title, description, componentSpec }
+deactivate DB
+
+DEV -> DEV: callLLM('Implement AuthService with JWT authentication following NestJS best practices...')
+
+DEV -> DEV: act(context)
+DEV -> DEV: executeTool('code_execution', 'npm test', {code: generatedCode})
+note right of DEV: **Sandbox Execution:**\nCode runs in isolated\nDocker container
+
+DEV -> DB: task.update({id: taskId, status: 'review', code: generatedCode})
+activate DB
+DB --> DEV: Task { status: 'review' }
+deactivate DB
+
+DEV -> DEV: saveMemory(namespace='coding', content='Implemented JWT auth with refresh token rotation')
+
+DEV -> QA: delegate({task: 'review_code', context: {taskId, code: generatedCode, tests: testResults}})
+note right of DEV: **Delegation to QA:**\nDEV hands off code for review\n\n**Chain continues:**\nCEO ΓåÆ PM ΓåÆ BA ΓåÆ Architect ΓåÆ PM ΓåÆ DEV ΓåÆ QA
+
+DEV --> Engine: { action: 'code_implemented', delegatedTo: 'QA', taskId }
+deactivate DEV
+
+== QA: Code Review & Testing ==
+
+Engine -> QA: execute({projectId, taskId, code, tests})
+activate QA
+
+QA -> QA: think(context)
+note right of QA: **QA Responsibilities:**\n1. Static code analysis\n2. Security review\n3. Integration testing\n4. Performance testing\n5. Approve or reject
+
+QA -> DB: task.findUnique({id: taskId})
+activate DB
+DB --> QA: Task { code, tests, status: 'review' }
+deactivate DB
+
+QA -> QA: callLLM('Review this code for security vulnerabilities, code smells, and best practices...')
+
+QA -> QA: act(context)
+QA -> QA: runStaticAnalysis(code)
+QA -> QA: runSecurityScan(code)
+QA -> QA: executeTool('integration_test', 'npm run test:e2e')
+
+alt Code Approved
+    QA -> DB: task.update({id: taskId, status: 'done', qa_approved: true, qa_feedback: 'LGTM'})
+    activate DB
+    DB --> QA: Task { status: 'done' }
+    deactivate DB
+    
+    QA -> DevOps: delegate({task: 'deploy_component', context: {taskId, code, environment: 'staging'}})
+    note right of QA: **Delegation to DevOps:**\nQA approves and hands off\nfor deployment\n\n**Chain continues:**\nCEO ΓåÆ PM ΓåÆ BA ΓåÆ Architect ΓåÆ\nPM ΓåÆ DEV ΓåÆ QA ΓåÆ DevOps
+    
+    QA --> Engine: { action: 'code_approved', delegatedTo: 'DevOps', taskId }
+    
+else Code Rejected
+    QA -> DB: task.update({id: taskId, status: 'in_progress', qa_approved: false, qa_feedback: 'Security issue: SQL injection risk'})
+    activate DB
+    DB --> QA: Task { status: 'in_progress' }
+    deactivate DB
+    
+    QA -> DEV: delegateBack({task: 'fix_issues', context: {taskId, feedback: 'Security issue: SQL injection risk'}})
+    note right of QA: **Return to DEV:**\nQA rejects and returns\nto DEV for fixes\n\n**Chain loops back:**\nDEV fixes ΓåÆ QA reviews again
+    
+    QA --> Engine: { action: 'code_rejected', delegatedTo: 'DEV', taskId }
+end
+
+deactivate QA
+
+== DevOps: Deployment ==
+
+Engine -> DevOps: execute({projectId, taskId, code, environment: 'staging'})
+activate DevOps
+
+DevOps -> DevOps: think(context)
+note right of DevOps: **DevOps Responsibilities (NEW):**\n1. Generate deployment configs\n2. Build Docker images\n3. Deploy to environment\n4. Run health checks\n5. Monitor metrics
+
+DevOps -> DB: task.findUnique({id: taskId})
+activate DB
+DB --> DevOps: Task { code, status: 'done' }
+deactivate DB
+
+DevOps -> DevOps: callLLM('Generate Dockerfile and docker-compose.yml for NestJS service...')
+
+DevOps -> DevOps: act(context)
+DevOps -> DevOps: generateDockerfile(code)
+DevOps -> DevOps: generateDockerCompose(services)
+
+DevOps -> DevOps: executeTool('docker_build', 'docker build -t auth-service:latest .')
+DevOps -> DevOps: executeTool('docker_deploy', 'docker-compose up -d')
+note right of DevOps: **Sandbox Deployment:**\nDeployment scripts run\nin isolated environment
+
+DevOps -> DevOps: runHealthChecks(serviceUrl)
+
+alt Deployment Successful
+    DevOps -> DB: deployment.create({projectId, taskId, environment: 'staging', status: 'success', url: '...'})
+    activate DB
+    DB --> DevOps: Deployment { status: 'success' }
+    deactivate DB
+    
+    DevOps -> DevOps: saveMemory(namespace='deployment', content='Deployed AuthService to staging with zero downtime')
+    
+    DevOps --> Engine: { action: 'deployment_successful', environment: 'staging', url: 'https://staging.ai-corp.com' }
+    
+else Deployment Failed
+    DevOps -> DB: deployment.create({projectId, taskId, environment: 'staging', status: 'failed', error: '...'})
+    activate DB
+    DB --> DevOps: Deployment { status: 'failed' }
+    deactivate DB
+    
+    DevOps --> Engine: { action: 'deployment_failed', error: 'Health check timeout' }
+end
+
+deactivate DevOps
+
+== Workflow Completion ==
+
+Engine -> DB: workflowRun.update({id: runId, status: 'completed', completedAt: now()})
+activate DB
+DB --> Engine: Updated
+deactivate DB
+
+Engine --> User: WorkflowCompleted { projectId, totalTasks: 20, deployed: true }
+deactivate Engine
+deactivate User
+
+note over User, DevOps: **Chain of Responsibility Pattern:**\nEach agent:\n1. Handles its responsibility\n2. Delegates to appropriate next agent\n3. Can return to previous agent on failure\n4. Maintains context through delegation\n\n**Complete Chain:**\nBoard Member ΓåÆ CEO ΓåÆ PM ΓåÆ BA ΓåÆ Architect ΓåÆ PM ΓåÆ DEV ΓåÆ QA ΓåÆ DevOps
+
+note over User, DevOps: **Delegation Context:**\nEach delegation includes:\n- fromAgent: AgentRole\n- toAgent: AgentRole\n- taskType: string\n- context: AgentContext\n- priority: Priority\n- metadata: Record<string, any>
+
+note over User, DevOps: **Key Characteristics:**\n1. Bidirectional delegation (forward and backward)\n2. Context preservation across chain\n3. Each agent has single responsibility\n4. Loose coupling via AgentOrchestrator\n5. All delegations logged for audit trail
+
+@enduml

+@startuml SD-005: Agent Learning Cycle
+!theme plain
+skinparam sequenceMessageAlign center
+skinparam responseMessageBelowArrow true
+
+title SD-005: Agent Learning Cycle
+subtitle Semantic Memory Storage and Retrieval with Vector Embeddings
+
+participant "BaseAgent" as Agent #Pink
+participant "TaskResult" as Result #LightYellow
+participant "MemoryService" as MemorySvc #LightGreen
+participant "EmbeddingService" as EmbeddingSvc #LightBlue
+participant "VectorStore\n(pgvector)" as VectorDB #LightGray
+participant "SemanticSearchService" as SearchSvc #LightCyan
+
+== Agent Completes Task ==
+
+Agent -> Agent: execute(context)
+activate Agent
+
+Agent -> Agent: think(context)
+Agent -> Agent: act(context)
+
+Agent -> Result: evaluateResult(action, context)
+activate Result
+note right of Result: **Evaluation Criteria:**\n1. Task success/failure\n2. Execution time\n3. Resource usage\n4. Output quality\n5. Novel patterns learned
+
+Result -> Result: calculateImportance(result)
+note right of Result: **Importance Score (1-10):**\n- 10: Critical insight, major breakthrough\n- 8: Significant learning, reusable pattern\n- 5: Routine completion, standard approach\n- 3: Minor observation\n- 1: Trivial fact
+
+Result --> Agent: TaskEvaluation { success: true, importance: 8, lesson: 'Using factory pattern simplified code generation' }
+deactivate Result
+
+== Extract Lesson Learned ==
+
+Agent -> Agent: extractLesson(taskEvaluation)
+note right of Agent: **Lesson Extraction:**\nAgent synthesizes:\n- What worked well\n- What could improve\n- Patterns identified\n- Context for future reuse
+
+Agent -> Agent: formulateLessonContent()
+note right of Agent: **Lesson Content Format:**\n"When implementing [context],\nI used [approach],\nwhich resulted in [outcome].\nKey insight: [learning]."
+
+== Generate Embedding ==
+
+Agent -> MemorySvc: saveMemory(agentId, agentRole, namespace, content, metadata)
+activate MemorySvc
+note right of MemorySvc: **Parameters:**\n- agentId: UUID from Agent table\n- agentRole: 'DEV'\n- namespace: 'coding_patterns'\n- content: lesson text\n- metadata: { importance: 8, projectId, tags: ['factory-pattern'] }
+
+MemorySvc -> MemorySvc: validate(agentId, agentRole, namespace, content)
+note right of MemorySvc: **Preconditions:**\n- agentRole is valid (CEO, PM, DEV, QA, ...)\n- namespace is non-empty\n- content is non-empty\n- importance Γêê [1, 10]
+
+MemorySvc -> EmbeddingSvc: generateEmbedding(content)
+activate EmbeddingSvc
+note right of EmbeddingSvc: **Embedding Generation:**\nUses text-embedding-ada-002\nor local model (BGE-small-en-v1.5)\nto convert text ΓåÆ 1536-dimensional vector
+
+EmbeddingSvc -> EmbeddingSvc: callEmbeddingAPI(content)
+note right of EmbeddingSvc: **API Call:**\nPOST /embeddings\n{\n  model: 'text-embedding-ada-002',\n  input: content\n}
+
+EmbeddingSvc --> MemorySvc: embedding: number[] (1536 dimensions)
+deactivate EmbeddingSvc
+
+== Store in Vector Database ==
+
+MemorySvc -> VectorDB: agentMemory.create({agentId, agentRole, namespace, content, embedding, importance, metadata})
+activate VectorDB
+note right of VectorDB: **pgvector Storage:**\nCREATE TABLE agent_memory (\n  id UUID PRIMARY KEY,\n  agent_id UUID REFERENCES agent(id),\n  agent_role VARCHAR NOT NULL,\n  namespace VARCHAR NOT NULL,\n  content TEXT NOT NULL,\n  embedding VECTOR(1536),\n  importance INTEGER CHECK (importance BETWEEN 1 AND 10),\n  metadata JSONB,\n  created_at TIMESTAMP\n);\n\nCREATE INDEX ON agent_memory\nUSING ivfflat (embedding vector_cosine_ops)\nWITH (lists = 100);
+
+VectorDB -> VectorDB: INSERT INTO agent_memory VALUES (...)
+VectorDB -> VectorDB: Update vector index (IVFFlat)
+note right of VectorDB: **Index Type: IVFFlat**\n- Approximate nearest neighbor\n- Cosine similarity metric\n- Trade-off: speed vs accuracy
+
+VectorDB --> MemorySvc: AgentMemory { id, createdAt }
+deactivate VectorDB
+
+MemorySvc -> MemorySvc: logStructured({event: 'memory_save', agentRole, namespace, importance, contentLength, embeddingTimeMs})
+
+MemorySvc --> Agent: memoryId: string
+deactivate MemorySvc
+
+Agent --> Agent: Memory saved successfully
+deactivate Agent
+
+note over Agent, VectorDB: **Memory Stored:**\nThe agent's lesson is now in\nvector database, ready for\nsemantic retrieval by any agent
+
+== Next Task Starts ==
+
+Agent -> Agent: execute(newContext)
+activate Agent
+note right of Agent: **New Task:**\nImplement authentication system\nwith token management
+
+== Query Semantic Memory ==
+
+Agent -> Agent: think(newContext)
+
+Agent -> MemorySvc: searchMemories(agentRole, namespace, query, limit=5)
+activate MemorySvc
+note right of MemorySvc: **Parameters:**\n- agentRole: 'DEV' (namespace isolation)\n- namespace: 'coding_patterns'\n- query: 'token management authentication'\n- limit: 5 (top-k results)
+
+MemorySvc -> EmbeddingSvc: generateEmbedding(query)
+activate EmbeddingSvc
+EmbeddingSvc -> EmbeddingSvc: callEmbeddingAPI('token management authentication')
+EmbeddingSvc --> MemorySvc: queryEmbedding: number[] (1536 dimensions)
+deactivate EmbeddingSvc
+
+== Semantic Similarity Search ==
+
+MemorySvc -> VectorDB: searchSimilarMemories(agentRole, namespace, queryEmbedding, limit)
+activate VectorDB
+note right of VectorDB: **pgvector Query:**\nSELECT\n  id, agent_role, namespace, content,\n  importance, created_at,\n  1 - (embedding <=> $1) AS similarity\nFROM agent_memory\nWHERE agent_role = $2\n  AND namespace = $3\nORDER BY embedding <=> $1\nLIMIT $4;\n\n**Operator <=>: Cosine distance**\n- 0 = identical vectors\n- 2 = opposite vectors\n- similarity = 1 - distance
+
+VectorDB -> VectorDB: IVFFlat index scan
+VectorDB -> VectorDB: Calculate cosine similarity for candidates
+VectorDB -> VectorDB: Filter by similarity >= 0.7 (threshold)
+note right of VectorDB: **Similarity Threshold:**\nRequirement 7.6 specifies:\nOnly return memories with\nsimilarity >= 0.7 (70% match)\n\n**Prevents low-quality retrieval**
+
+VectorDB --> MemorySvc: RawMemoryResult[] { id, content, importance, similarity }
+deactivate VectorDB
+
+== Rank Results ==
+
+MemorySvc -> MemorySvc: rankResults(rawResults)
+note right of MemorySvc: **Ranking Formula:**\nrank = similarity ├ù 0.7 + (importance / 10) ├ù 0.3\n\n**Example:**\nMemory A: similarity=0.9, importance=8\n  rank = 0.9 ├ù 0.7 + 0.8 ├ù 0.3 = 0.87\n\nMemory B: similarity=0.8, importance=10\n  rank = 0.8 ├ù 0.7 + 1.0 ├ù 0.3 = 0.86\n\n**A ranks higher despite lower importance**
+
+MemorySvc -> MemorySvc: sortByRankDescending(rankedResults)
+
+MemorySvc -> MemorySvc: logStructured({event: 'memory_search', agentRole, namespace, resultCount, topSimilarity, topRank, threshold: 0.7})
+
+MemorySvc --> Agent: MemorySearchResult[] [\n  { id, agentRole, namespace, content, importance, similarity: 0.92, rank: 0.88 },\n  { id, agentRole, namespace, content, importance, similarity: 0.85, rank: 0.81 },\n  { id, agentRole, namespace, content, importance, similarity: 0.78, rank: 0.72 },\n  ...\n]
+deactivate MemorySvc
+
+== Enrich Context with Memories ==
+
+Agent -> Agent: enrichContextWithMemories(newContext, memories)
+note right of Agent: **Context Enhancement:**\nAgent adds retrieved memories\nto LLM prompt:\n\n"You are implementing authentication.\n\n**Relevant past experiences:**\n1. [Memory 1 content with similarity 0.92]\n2. [Memory 2 content with similarity 0.85]\n...\n\nUse these insights to guide your implementation."
+
+Agent -> Agent: callLLM({messages: [systemPrompt, memoryContext, userTask], model: 'gpt-4'})
+note right of Agent: **LLM Benefits from Memory:**\n- Consistent patterns across tasks\n- Avoids repeating mistakes\n- Reuses proven solutions\n- Faster, better quality output
+
+Agent -> Agent: act(enrichedContext)
+
+Agent -> Agent: Better decision making due to semantic memory
+note right of Agent: **Learning Feedback Loop:**\n1. Complete task\n2. Extract lesson\n3. Store with embedding\n4. Retrieve on similar future task\n5. Apply lesson ΓåÆ better outcome\n6. Store new lesson\nΓåÆ Continuous improvement
+
+Agent --> Agent: Task completed with higher quality
+deactivate Agent
+
+note over Agent, VectorDB: **Memory Isolation (Requirement 8):**\n- Each agent has separate namespace\n- DEV agent cannot access CEO memories\n- searchMemories() filters by agentRole\n- Prevents cross-role information leakage\n- Ensures role-specific learning
+
+note over Agent, VectorDB: **Key Algorithm: Semantic Search**\n\n**Input:** query string, agentRole, namespace, limit\n**Output:** Top-k memories ranked by relevance\n\n**Steps:**\n1. Generate query embedding (1536-dim vector)\n2. Query pgvector with cosine similarity\n3. Filter by agentRole (namespace isolation)\n4. Filter by namespace (e.g., 'coding_patterns')\n5. Filter by similarity >= 0.7 (quality threshold)\n6. Rank by: similarity ├ù 0.7 + importance ├ù 0.3\n7. Sort descending, return top-k\n\n**Complexity:**\n- Embedding generation: O(1) API call\n- Vector search: O(log n) with IVFFlat index\n- Ranking: O(k) where k = limit\n- Total: O(log n + k) Γëê O(log n)
+
+note over Agent, VectorDB: **Timing Constraints:**\n- Embedding generation: < 500ms\n- Vector search: < 200ms\n- Total memory retrieval: < 1 second\n- Memory save: < 300ms (async, non-blocking)
+
+note over Agent, VectorDB: **Design Patterns:**\n- **Repository Pattern**: MemoryService abstracts storage\n- **Strategy Pattern**: EmbeddingService supports multiple embedding models\n- **Observer Pattern**: Memory events trigger analytics\n- **Lazy Loading**: Embeddings generated on-demand
+
+note over Agent, VectorDB: **Postconditions:**\n1. All memories have embeddings (non-null constraint)\n2. Importance Γêê [1, 10] (CHECK constraint)\n3. Similarity scores Γêê [0, 1] (cosine similarity bounds)\n4. Results always filtered by agentRole (namespace isolation)\n5. Search returns at most 'limit' results\n6. Results sorted by rank descending
+
+@enduml

+@startuml SD-006: Workflow Rollback (Compensating Transactions)
+!theme plain
+skinparam sequenceMessageAlign center
+skinparam responseMessageBelowArrow true
+
+title SD-006: Workflow Rollback (Compensating Transactions)
+subtitle Implementing Saga Pattern for Distributed Transaction Management
+
+participant "WorkflowEngine" as Engine #Orange
+participant "WorkflowStep" as Step #LightYellow
+participant "CompensationService" as CompSvc #LightGreen
+participant "PrismaDB" as DB #LightGray
+participant "CEOAgent" as CEO #Pink
+participant "PMAgent" as PM #Pink
+participant "DEVAgent" as DEV #Pink
+participant "WebSocketGateway" as WS #LightCyan
+participant "BoardMember" as User #LightBlue
+
+== Workflow Execution in Progress ==
+
+Engine -> Engine: processCurrentNode(runId)
+activate Engine
+
+Engine -> Step: executeNode(nodeId='dev_implementation')
+activate Step
+note right of Step: **Workflow Context:**\n- 3 steps completed:\n  1. CEO created milestones\n  2. PM created tasks\n  3. DEV started implementation\n- Current step: DEV writes code\n- Next step: QA review
+
+Step -> DEV: execute(context)
+activate DEV
+
+DEV -> DEV: think(context)
+DEV -> DEV: act(context)
+
+DEV -> DEV: executeTool('code_execution', 'npm test')
+note right of DEV: **Sandbox Execution Failure:**\nDocker container timeout\nafter 30 seconds
+
+DEV --> Step: Error: Sandbox timeout - container unresponsive
+deactivate DEV
+
+Step --> Engine: NodeResult { status: 'failure', error: 'Sandbox timeout - container unresponsive' }
+deactivate Step
+
+== Error Detection & Retry Logic ==
+
+Engine -> Engine: handleNodeFailure(runId, nodeId, error)
+note right of Engine: **Requirement 19:**\nRetry with exponential backoff:\n- Attempt 1: immediate\n- Attempt 2: 2^1 = 2 seconds\n- Attempt 3: 2^2 = 4 seconds\n- Attempt 4: 2^3 = 8 seconds
+
+Engine -> Engine: retryCount = step.retryCount + 1
+Engine -> Engine: maxRetries = node.maxRetries || 3
+
+alt retryCount <= maxRetries
+    Engine -> Engine: backoffDelay = 2^retryCount seconds
+    Engine -> WS: sendToProject(projectId, WorkflowRetryEvent)
+    activate WS
+    WS -->> User: WebSocket: workflow:retrying { attempt: 2, maxRetries: 3, delay: 2000 }
+    deactivate WS
+    
+    Engine -> Engine: delay(backoffDelay)
+    Engine -> Step: executeNode(nodeId='dev_implementation')
+    note right of Engine: **Retry attempt 2...**
+    
+else retryCount > maxRetries
+    Engine -> Engine: Max retries exceeded, initiating rollback
+    note right of Engine: **Rollback Decision:**\nAfter 3 failed retries,\nworkflow cannot proceed.\n\n**Saga Pattern:**\nExecute compensating transactions\nin reverse order to undo\ncompleted steps.
+end
+
+== Identify Compensation Actions ==
+
+Engine -> CompSvc: identifyCompensationActions(runId)
+activate CompSvc
+
+CompSvc -> DB: workflowStep.findMany({workflowRunId: runId, status: 'completed'})
+activate DB
+DB --> CompSvc: WorkflowStep[] [\n  { id, nodeId: 'ceo_plan', status: 'completed', agentRole: 'CEO', output: {...} },\n  { id, nodeId: 'pm_breakdown', status: 'completed', agentRole: 'PM', output: {...} },\n]
+deactivate DB
+
+CompSvc -> CompSvc: reverseSteps(completedSteps)
+note right of CompSvc: **Reverse Order Execution:**\nCompensating transactions must\nexecute in reverse to maintain\ndata consistency:\n\n**Forward:** CEO ΓåÆ PM ΓåÆ DEV\n**Rollback:** DEV ΓåÆ PM ΓåÆ CEO
+
+CompSvc -> CompSvc: buildCompensationPlan(reversedSteps)
+note right of CompSvc: **Compensation Plan:**\n[\n  { stepId, agentRole: 'PM', action: 'deleteTasks', data: taskIds },\n  { stepId, agentRole: 'CEO', action: 'deleteMilestones', data: milestoneIds },\n]
+
+CompSvc --> Engine: CompensationPlan { actions: [...], totalSteps: 2 }
+deactivate CompSvc
+
+== Execute Compensating Transactions ==
+
+Engine -> WS: sendToProject(projectId, WorkflowRollbackStartedEvent)
+activate WS
+WS -->> User: WebSocket: workflow:rollback_started { totalSteps: 2, reason: 'Step failure after max retries' }
+deactivate WS
+
+loop For each compensation action (reverse order)
+    Engine -> CompSvc: executeCompensation(action)
+    activate CompSvc
+    
+    alt action.agentRole == 'PM'
+        CompSvc -> CompSvc: Compensate PM step: delete tasks
+        note right of CompSvc: **Compensation: Delete Tasks**\nUndo PM agent's work\nby removing created tasks
+        
+        CompSvc -> DB: task.deleteMany({id: {in: action.data.taskIds}})
+        activate DB
+        note right of DB: **Transactional Delete:**\nAll tasks deleted atomically\nwithin database transaction\n\n**Referential Integrity:**\nCascade deletes to:\n- TaskAssignments\n- TaskComments\n- TaskAttachments
+        
+        DB --> CompSvc: DeleteResult { count: 15 }
+        deactivate DB
+        
+        CompSvc -> PM: notifyRollback(stepId, reason)
+        activate PM
+        note right of PM: **Agent Notification:**\nInform PM that its work\nwas rolled back
+        
+        PM -> PM: saveMemory(namespace='rollback', content='Tasks rolled back due to downstream failure', importance=7)
+        PM --> CompSvc: acknowledged
+        deactivate PM
+        
+        CompSvc -> DB: compensationLog.create({workflowRunId, action: 'delete_tasks', status: 'success', taskIds: [...]})
+        activate DB
+        DB --> CompSvc: CompensationLog { id, status: 'success' }
+        deactivate DB
+        
+    else action.agentRole == 'CEO'
+        CompSvc -> CompSvc: Compensate CEO step: delete milestones
+        note right of CompSvc: **Compensation: Delete Milestones**\nUndo CEO agent's work\nby removing created milestones
+        
+        CompSvc -> DB: milestone.deleteMany({id: {in: action.data.milestoneIds}})
+        activate DB
+        note right of DB: **Cascade Constraint Check:**\nVerify no tasks reference\nthese milestones before delete\n\n(Already deleted by PM compensation)
+        
+        DB --> CompSvc: DeleteResult { count: 3 }
+        deactivate DB
+        
+        CompSvc -> CEO: notifyRollback(stepId, reason)
+        activate CEO
+        CEO -> CEO: saveMemory(namespace='rollback', content='Milestones rolled back due to downstream failure', importance=7)
+        CEO --> CompSvc: acknowledged
+        deactivate CEO
+        
+        CompSvc -> DB: compensationLog.create({workflowRunId, action: 'delete_milestones', status: 'success', milestoneIds: [...]})
+        activate DB
+        DB --> CompSvc: CompensationLog { id, status: 'success' }
+        deactivate DB
+    end
+    
+    CompSvc --> Engine: CompensationResult { action, status: 'success' }
+    deactivate CompSvc
+    
+    Engine -> WS: sendToProject(projectId, CompensationStepCompletedEvent)
+    activate WS
+    WS -->> User: WebSocket: workflow:compensation_completed { action: action.action, agentRole: action.agentRole }
+    deactivate WS
+end
+
+== Update Workflow Status ==
+
+Engine -> DB: workflowRun.update({id: runId, status: 'failed', failedAt: now(), failureReason: 'Step failure after max retries', compensationCompleted: true})
+activate DB
+DB --> Engine: WorkflowRun { status: 'failed', compensationCompleted: true }
+deactivate DB
+
+Engine -> DB: project.update({id: projectId, status: 'failed', failureReason: 'Workflow rollback completed'})
+activate DB
+DB --> Engine: Project { status: 'failed' }
+deactivate DB
+
+== Notify Stakeholders ==
+
+Engine -> WS: sendToProject(projectId, WorkflowRollbackCompletedEvent)
+activate WS
+WS -> User: WebSocket: workflow:rollback_completed {\n  workflowRunId,\n  projectId,\n  originalError: 'Sandbox timeout',\n  compensationActions: [\n    { action: 'delete_tasks', status: 'success', count: 15 },\n    { action: 'delete_milestones', status: 'success', count: 3 }\n  ],\n  timestamp: now()\n}
+activate User
+
+note right of User: **User Notification:**\nBoard Member sees:\n- Workflow failed\n- All changes rolled back\n- System state consistent\n- Can safely retry or abandon project
+
+User --> WS: acknowledged
+deactivate User
+deactivate WS
+
+Engine -> Engine: logStructured({event: 'workflow_rollback', workflowRunId, projectId, compensationActionsCount: 2, status: 'success'})
+
+Engine --> Engine: Workflow rollback completed successfully
+deactivate Engine
+
+note over Engine, User: **Saga Pattern Implementation:**\n\n**Forward Transactions (T1, T2, T3):**\nT1: CEO creates milestones\nT2: PM creates tasks\nT3: DEV implements code (FAILED)\n\n**Compensating Transactions (C3, C2, C1):**\nC3: DEV cleanup (none, as it failed)\nC2: PM deletes tasks\nC1: CEO deletes milestones\n\n**Guarantee: Atomicity**\nEither all steps succeed, or all\nare compensated, leaving system\nin consistent state.
+
+note over Engine, User: **Alternative: Partial Compensation Failure**\n\nIf compensation fails (e.g., database unavailable):\n\n1. Mark compensation as 'failed'\n2. Persist compensation plan to database\n3. Retry compensation with exponential backoff\n4. Alert admin if compensation retries exhausted\n5. Manual intervention required\n\n**Compensation Idempotency:**\nAll compensation actions are idempotent:\n- DELETE tasks WHERE id IN (...) - safe to retry\n- If already deleted, no-op
+
+note over Engine, User: **Design Patterns Used:**\n- **Saga Pattern**: Distributed transaction management with compensating transactions\n- **Command Pattern**: Each compensation action is a reversible command\n- **Memento Pattern**: WorkflowStep stores state for rollback\n- **Chain of Responsibility**: Compensation actions chained in reverse order\n- **Strategy Pattern**: Different compensation strategies per agent role
+
+note over Engine, User: **Key Invariants:**\n1. Compensations execute in strict reverse order\n2. Each compensation is atomic (database transaction)\n3. Compensation logs persisted before execution\n4. Agent notifications are best-effort (non-blocking)\n5. Workflow status transitions: running ΓåÆ failed (no intermediate states)\n6. Project status updated after workflow status\n7. All compensation results logged for audit trail
+
+note over Engine, User: **Timing Constraints:**\n- Compensation identification: < 1 second\n- Per-compensation execution: < 5 seconds\n- Total rollback time: < 30 seconds (for typical workflow)\n- WebSocket notifications: < 500ms delivery\n- Database transaction timeout: 10 seconds
+
+note over Engine, User: **Business Rules:**\n- BR-101: Workflow can only roll back if at least one step completed\n- BR-102: Compensation must execute for all completed steps\n- BR-103: Partial compensation leaves workflow in 'failed_partial_rollback' state\n- BR-104: Board Member notified immediately of rollback\n- BR-105: Agents notified asynchronously (best-effort)
+
+note over Engine, User: **Recovery Scenarios:**\n\n**Scenario 1: System crash during compensation**\n- On restart, WorkflowEngine.recoverWorkflows() detects\n  workflows with status='failed' and compensationCompleted=false\n- Resume compensation from last logged action\n- Idempotent operations ensure safe retry\n\n**Scenario 2: Database unavailable during compensation**\n- Retry compensation with exponential backoff (max 5 retries)\n- If all retries fail, mark workflow as 'failed_partial_rollback'\n- Alert admin for manual intervention\n\n**Scenario 3: Agent notification failure**\n- Log error but continue compensation (notifications are non-critical)\n- Agent will discover rollback on next task poll
+
+@enduml

+@startuml SM-001-agent-states
+title Agent State Machine - State Transitions
+'Based on: apps/api/src/domain/agent/AgentStateMachine.ts
+'Design Pattern: State Pattern
+
+[*] --> IDLE : Agent Created
+
+state IDLE {
+  [*] --> CheckingQueue
+  CheckingQueue --> WaitingForTask
+  --
+  <b>Entry:</b> Release resources, clear context
+  <b>Exit:</b> Log assignment timestamp
+  <b>Internal:</b> Heartbeat every 30s
+}
+
+state THINKING {
+  [*] --> AnalyzingProblem
+  AnalyzingProblem --> FormulatingPlan
+  FormulatingPlan --> ValidatingPlan
+  --
+  <b>Entry:</b> Load task context, initialize session
+  <b>Exit:</b> Persist execution plan
+  <b>Internal:</b> Query LLM for reasoning
+}
+
+state ACTING {
+  [*] --> ExecutingActions
+  ExecutingActions --> CallingTools
+  CallingTools --> ProcessingResults
+  --
+  <b>Entry:</b> Start action timer, log plan
+  <b>Exit:</b> Save intermediate results
+  <b>Internal:</b> Update progress metrics
+}
+
+state WAITING_APPROVAL {
+  [*] --> PendingReview
+  PendingReview --> AwaitingResponse
+  --
+  <b>Entry:</b> Send approval request, pause execution
+  <b>Exit:</b> Log approval decision
+  <b>Internal:</b> Check timeout every 5min
+}
+
+state ERROR {
+  [*] --> LoggingError
+  LoggingError --> DeterminingRecovery
+  --
+  <b>Entry:</b> Capture error details, rollback partial changes
+  <b>Exit:</b> Clear error state
+  <b>Internal:</b> Notify monitoring system
+}
+
+state COMPLETED {
+  [*] --> GeneratingReport
+  GeneratingReport --> CleaningUp
+  --
+  <b>Entry:</b> Finalize results, calculate cost
+  <b>Exit:</b> Archive session data
+  <b>Internal:</b> Store lessons learned
+}
+
+IDLE --> THINKING : <b>taskAssigned()</b>\n[guard: task != null]\naction: loadContext()
+
+THINKING --> ACTING : <b>planFormulated()</b>\n[guard: plan.isValid()]\naction: startExecution()
+
+THINKING --> ERROR : <b>llmError()</b>\n[guard: retries exhausted]\naction: captureError()
+
+ACTING --> COMPLETED : <b>actionsCompleted()</b>\n[guard: allActionsSuccess()]\naction: finalizeResults()
+
+ACTING --> WAITING_APPROVAL : <b>approvalRequired()</b>\n[guard: requiresHumanReview()]\naction: pauseAndRequest()
+
+ACTING --> ERROR : <b>toolError()</b>\n[guard: criticalFailure]\naction: rollback()
+
+WAITING_APPROVAL --> ACTING : <b>approved()</b>\n[guard: decision == APPROVED]\naction: resumeExecution()
+
+WAITING_APPROVAL --> COMPLETED : <b>autoComplete()</b>\n[guard: canAutoComplete()]\naction: skipApproval()
+
+WAITING_APPROVAL --> ERROR : <b>rejected()</b>\n[guard: decision == REJECTED]\naction: abortTask()
+
+ERROR --> IDLE : <b>recover()</b>\n[guard: canRecover()]\naction: reset()
+
+COMPLETED --> IDLE : <b>reuse()</b>\n[guard: agentPoolEnabled]\naction: reset()
+
+COMPLETED --> [*] : <b>terminate()</b>\n[guard: noMoreWork]
+
+ERROR --> [*] : <b>terminate()</b>\n[guard: fatalError]
+
+note right of IDLE
+  <b>Invariant:</b> No active task assigned
+  <b>SOLID:</b> Single Responsibility
+  - Only manages own lifecycle
+end note
+
+note right of THINKING
+  <b>Invariant:</b> Has task context loaded
+  <b>Pattern:</b> Strategy Pattern
+  - Different reasoning strategies
+  - Based on task complexity
+end note
+
+note right of ACTING
+  <b>Invariant:</b> Has valid execution plan
+  <b>Pattern:</b> Command Pattern
+  - Actions as command objects
+  - Supports undo/redo
+end note
+
+note right of WAITING_APPROVAL
+  <b>Invariant:</b> Approval request sent
+  <b>Timeout:</b> Auto-reject after 2 hours
+  <b>SOLID:</b> Open/Closed Principle
+  - Extensible approval types
+end note
+
+note left of ERROR
+  <b>Invariant:</b> Error details captured
+  <b>Recovery:</b> Max 3 retry attempts
+  <b>Pattern:</b> Circuit Breaker
+  - Prevents cascading failures
+end note
+
+note left of COMPLETED
+  <b>Invariant:</b> All work done
+  <b>Data:</b> Results persisted
+  <b>Cost:</b> Usage metrics recorded
+end note
+
+@enduml
diff --git a/docs/diagrams/state-machines/SM-002-workflow-states.puml b/docs/diagrams/state-machines/SM-002-workflow-states.puml
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..7136774

+++ b/docs/diagrams/state-machines/SM-002-workflow-states.puml

+@startuml SM-002-workflow-states
+title Workflow State Machine - State Transitions
+'Based on: apps/api/prisma/schema.prisma WorkflowRun.status
+'Valid states: running, paused, completed, failed, cancelled
+
+[*] --> RUNNING : <b>startWorkflow()</b>\n[guard: projectActive]\naction: initContext()
+
+state RUNNING {
+  [*] --> NODE_EXECUTION
+  
+  state NODE_EXECUTION {
+    [*] --> LoadingNode
+    LoadingNode --> ValidatingNode
+    ValidatingNode --> ExecutingNode
+    ExecutingNode --> NodeComplete
+    --
+    <b>Entry:</b> Load node definition
+    <b>Do:</b> Execute node logic
+    <b>Exit:</b> Save node result
+  }
+  
+  state PARALLEL_EXECUTION {
+    [*] --> ForkingBranches
+    ForkingBranches --> [fork] ParallelWork
+    --
+    state ParallelWork {
+      [*] --> Branch1
+      [*] --> Branch2
+      [*] --> Branch3
+      --
+      <b>Entry:</b> Allocate resources
+      <b>Do:</b> Execute in parallel
+      <b>Exit:</b> Join results
+    }
+    ParallelWork --> [join] JoiningResults
+    JoiningResults --> SynchronizationPoint
+    --
+    <b>Entry:</b> Fork execution paths
+    <b>Exit:</b> Merge all results
+  }
+  
+  state APPROVAL_WAITING {
+    [*] --> SendingRequest
+    SendingRequest --> AwaitingDecision
+    AwaitingDecision --> DecisionReceived
+    --
+    <b>Entry:</b> Pause workflow, send approval request
+    <b>Exit:</b> Resume workflow with decision
+  }
+  
+  NODE_EXECUTION --> PARALLEL_EXECUTION : [guard: isParallelNode()]\naction: fork()
+  PARALLEL_EXECUTION --> NODE_EXECUTION : [guard: allBranchesComplete()]\naction: join()
+  NODE_EXECUTION --> APPROVAL_WAITING : [guard: requiresApproval()]\naction: pause()
+  APPROVAL_WAITING --> NODE_EXECUTION : [guard: approved()]\naction: resume()
+  
+  --
+  <b>Composite State Entry:</b> Set status = "running"
+  <b>Composite State Exit:</b> Save execution context
+  <b>Invariant:</b> currentNodeId is set
+}
+
+state PAUSED {
+  [*] --> WaitingForApproval
+  WaitingForApproval --> CheckingTimeout
+  CheckingTimeout --> [choice]
+  --
+  <b>Entry:</b> Pause all agents, persist state
+  <b>Exit:</b> Restore execution context
+  <b>Internal:</b> Heartbeat every 1min
+}
+
+state COMPLETED {
+  [*] --> GeneratingReport
+  GeneratingReport --> ArchivingData
+  ArchivingData --> FinalCleanup
+  --
+  <b>Entry:</b> Calculate final metrics, close all tasks
+  <b>Exit:</b> Store completion timestamp
+  <b>Invariant:</b> completedAt is set
+}
+
+state FAILED {
+  [*] --> LoggingFailure
+  LoggingFailure --> RollingBack
+  RollingBack --> NotifyingStakeholders
+  --
+  <b>Entry:</b> Capture error, cancel pending tasks
+  <b>Exit:</b> Mark project as needs attention
+  <b>Data:</b> Error details in context.error
+}
+
+state CANCELLED {
+  [*] --> StoppingAgents
+  StoppingAgents --> CleanupResources
+  --
+  <b>Entry:</b> Stop all execution, cancel tasks
+  <b>Exit:</b> Release resources
+  <b>Trigger:</b> User cancellation
+}
+
+RUNNING --> PAUSED : <b>approvalRequired()</b>\n[guard: approvalNode]\naction: requestApproval()
+
+PAUSED --> RUNNING : <b>approved()</b>\n[guard: decision == APPROVED]\naction: resume()
+
+RUNNING --> COMPLETED : <b>allNodesCompleted()</b>\n[guard: noMoreNodes()]\naction: finalize()
+
+RUNNING --> FAILED : <b>criticalError()</b>\n[guard: !canRecover()]\naction: rollback()
+
+RUNNING --> CANCELLED : <b>userCancels()</b>\n[guard: userRequest]\naction: stopAll()
+
+PAUSED --> CANCELLED : <b>userCancels()</b>\n[guard: userRequest]\naction: stopAll()
+
+FAILED --> RUNNING : <b>retry()</b>\n[guard: retryAllowed]\naction: restoreState()
+
+COMPLETED --> [*] : <b>archive()</b>
+
+FAILED --> [*] : <b>cleanup()</b>
+
+CANCELLED --> [*] : <b>cleanup()</b>
+
+note right of RUNNING
+  <b>Invariant:</b> 
+  - currentNodeId != null
+  - At least one agent active
+  
+  <b>SOLID - Single Responsibility:</b>
+  - Only manages execution flow
+  
+  <b>Pattern - State Pattern:</b>
+  - Sub-states for different phases
+end note
+
+note left of PAUSED
+  <b>Invariant:</b>
+  - Approval request exists
+  - Execution context saved
+  
+  <b>Timeout:</b> 
+  - Auto-reject after 2 hours
+  - Transition to FAILED
+  
+  <b>Business Rule:</b>
+  - Only one pending approval per workflow
+end note
+
+note right of COMPLETED
+  <b>Invariant:</b>
+  - All tasks in DONE status
+  - completedAt > startedAt
+  - Final cost calculated
+  
+  <b>Business Impact:</b>
+  - Triggers billing finalization
+  - Updates project metrics
+end note
+
+note left of FAILED
+  <b>Recovery Options:</b>
+  1. Manual retry (max 3 times)
+  2. Rollback to last checkpoint
+  3. Cancel and restart
+  
+  <b>Pattern - Memento:</b>
+  - Saves checkpoints for rollback
+end note
+
+note bottom of RUNNING
+  <b>Design Pattern: Composite State</b>
+  
+  RUNNING contains three concurrent sub-states
+  that can transition independently:
+  - NODE_EXECUTION: Sequential workflow steps
+  - PARALLEL_EXECUTION: Concurrent agent work
+  - APPROVAL_WAITING: Human-in-the-loop gates
+  
+  This allows complex orchestration while
+  maintaining clear state boundaries.
+end note
+
+@enduml

+@startuml SM-003-task-states
+title Task Status State Machine - State Transitions
+'Based on: apps/api/prisma/schema.prisma Task.status
+'Valid states: todo, in_progress, review, done, cancelled
+'Requirement 12: Kanban status transitions
+
+[*] --> TODO : <b>createTask()</b>\n[guard: project.active]\naction: addToBacklog()
+
+state TODO {
+  [*] --> Backlog
+  Backlog --> ReadyForDev
+  ReadyForDev --> Assigned
+  --
+  <b>Entry:</b> Set priority, estimate cost
+  <b>Exit:</b> Assign to agent, log start time
+  <b>Internal:</b> Can update priority
+  <b>Invariant:</b> assignedAgent == null
+}
+
+state IN_PROGRESS {
+  [*] --> Analyzing
+  Analyzing --> Implementing
+  Implementing --> Testing
+  Testing --> Documenting
+  --
+  <b>Entry:</b> Agent claims task, start timer
+  <b>Exit:</b> Stop timer, calculate actualCost
+  <b>Internal:</b> Progress updates every 15min
+  <b>Invariant:</b> assignedAgent != null
+}
+
+state REVIEW {
+  [*] --> CodeReview
+  CodeReview --> QualityChecks
+  QualityChecks --> AwaitingApproval
+  --
+  <b>Entry:</b> Submit for QA, run automated checks
+  <b>Exit:</b> Approval decision recorded
+  <b>Internal:</b> QA agent adds comments
+  <b>Invariant:</b> Implementation complete
+}
+
+state DONE {
+  [*] --> VerifyingCompletion
+  VerifyingCompletion --> RecordingMetrics
+  RecordingMetrics --> Archived
+  --
+  <b>Entry:</b> Mark complete, update project cost
+  <b>Exit:</b> Release agent, archive data
+  <b>Internal:</b> Cannot be modified
+  <b>Invariant:</b> actualCost recorded
+}
+
+state CANCELLED {
+  [*] --> RecordingReason
+  RecordingReason --> CleaningUp
+  --
+  <b>Entry:</b> Stop work, cancel subtasks
+  <b>Exit:</b> Archive partial work
+  <b>Reason:</b> Stored in metadata
+}
+
+TODO --> IN_PROGRESS : <b>agentPicksUp()</b>\n[guard: agentAvailable]\naction: assignAgent()
+
+IN_PROGRESS --> REVIEW : <b>implementationComplete()</b>\n[guard: allCriteriaMet]\naction: submitForReview()
+
+IN_PROGRESS --> TODO : <b>blocked()</b>\n[guard: dependencyMissing]\naction: unassignAgent()
+
+REVIEW --> DONE : <b>qaApproves()</b>\n[guard: qualityGatePassed]\naction: markComplete()
+
+REVIEW --> IN_PROGRESS : <b>changesRequested()</b>\n[guard: issuesFound]\naction: sendFeedback()
+
+TODO --> CANCELLED : <b>projectCancelled()</b>\n[guard: project.cancelled]\naction: cleanup()
+
+IN_PROGRESS --> CANCELLED : <b>projectCancelled()</b>\n[guard: project.cancelled]\naction: cleanup()
+
+TODO --> DONE : <b>skipToComplete()</b>\n[guard: noImplementationNeeded]\naction: markComplete()
+note on link
+  Rare case: Task was
+  already completed
+  by another task
+end note
+
+DONE --> [*]
+
+CANCELLED --> [*]
+
+note right of TODO
+  <b>Entry Actions:</b>
+  - Validate task definition
+  - Set priority & estimate
+  - Add to Kanban board
+  
+  <b>Business Rule:</b>
+  - Cannot start without estimate
+  - Priority: low | medium | high
+  
+  <b>SOLID - Liskov Substitution:</b>
+  - Task subtypes maintain contract
+end note
+
+note right of IN_PROGRESS
+  <b>Entry Actions:</b>
+  - Agent: IDLE ΓåÆ THINKING
+  - Start cost tracking
+  - Lock task to agent
+  
+  <b>Progress Tracking:</b>
+  - Completion percentage
+  - Time elapsed vs estimate
+  - Blockers encountered
+  
+  <b>Pattern - Observer:</b>
+  - PM notified of progress updates
+end note
+
+note left of REVIEW
+  <b>Entry Actions:</b>
+  - Run static analysis
+  - Execute test suite
+  - Check code coverage
+  - Security scan
+  
+  <b>Quality Gates:</b>
+  1. All tests pass
+  2. Coverage ΓëÑ 80%
+  3. No critical security issues
+  4. Code standards met
+  
+  <b>Pattern - Strategy:</b>
+  - Different review strategies
+  - Based on task type
+end note
+
+note left of DONE
+  <b>Entry Actions:</b>
+  - Record completion timestamp
+  - Calculate final cost
+  - Update project metrics
+  - Store lessons learned
+  
+  <b>Invariants:</b>
+  - actualCost > 0
+  - completedAt > createdAt
+  - All acceptance criteria met
+  
+  <b>Immutability:</b>
+  - Cannot transition out
+  - Historical record preserved
+end note
+
+note bottom of CANCELLED
+  <b>Cancellation Triggers:</b>
+  1. Project cancelled by user
+  2. Task no longer needed
+  3. Replaced by other task
+  4. Scope change
+  
+  <b>Cleanup Actions:</b>
+  - Stop active agent work
+  - Release allocated resources
+  - Record partial progress
+  - Update sprint metrics
+end note
+
+note top
+  <b>Kanban Board Mapping:</b>
+  - TODO ΓåÆ Backlog column
+  - IN_PROGRESS ΓåÆ In Progress column  
+  - REVIEW ΓåÆ Review column
+  - DONE ΓåÆ Done column
+  - CANCELLED ΓåÆ Hidden/filtered
+  
+  <b>Business Metrics:</b>
+  - Lead Time: TODO ΓåÆ DONE duration
+  - Cycle Time: IN_PROGRESS ΓåÆ DONE duration
+  - Throughput: Tasks completed per sprint
+  - WIP Limit: Max 5 tasks in IN_PROGRESS per agent
+end note
+
+@enduml

index 0000000..21d3839

+++ b/docs/diagrams/state-machines/SM-004-approval-states.puml

+@startuml SM-004-approval-states
+title Approval State Machine - State Transitions
+'Based on: apps/api/prisma/schema.prisma Approval.status
+'Requirement 17.7: Status can only transition from pending to approved or rejected
+'Implementation: apps/api/src/modules/approvals/approval.service.ts
+
+[*] --> PENDING : <b>createApprovalRequest()</b>\n[guard: workflowPaused]\naction: sendNotification()
+
+state PENDING {
+  [*] --> RequestCreated
+  RequestCreated --> NotificationSent
+  NotificationSent --> AwaitingDecision
+  AwaitingDecision --> CheckingTimeout
+  CheckingTimeout --> AwaitingDecision : [timeout not reached]
+  --
+  <b>Entry:</b> 
+  - Create approval record
+  - Send WebSocket notification
+  - Set requestedAt timestamp
+  - Pause workflow execution
+  
+  <b>Internal:</b>
+  - Check timeout every 5 minutes
+  - Reminder notifications every 30 min
+  
+  <b>Exit:</b>
+  - Record respondedAt timestamp
+  - Resume workflow execution
+  
+  <b>Invariant:</b>
+  - respondedAt == null
+  - comment == null
+  - Workflow status = PAUSED
+  
+  <b>Timeout Rule:</b>
+  - Auto-reject after 2 hours
+  - Configurable per approval type
+}
+
+state APPROVED {
+  [*] --> RecordingDecision
+  RecordingDecision --> ResumingWorkflow
+  ResumingWorkflow --> TransitioningToNextNode
+  --
+  <b>Entry:</b>
+  - Set status = "approved"
+  - Record respondedAt
+  - Log approval comment
+  - Resume workflow with "onApprove" edge
+  
+  <b>Exit:</b>
+  - Workflow continues execution
+  - Notify all stakeholders
+  
+  <b>Business Impact:</b>
+  - Deployment proceeds
+  - Budget released
+  - Task marked complete
+  - Agent continues work
+  
+  <b>Audit:</b>
+  - Who approved
+  - When approved
+  - Reason (comment)
+}
+
+state REJECTED {
+  [*] --> RecordingRejection
+  RecordingRejection --> InitiatingRollback
+  InitiatingRollback --> NotifyingAgents
+  --
+  <b>Entry:</b>
+  - Set status = "rejected"
+  - Record respondedAt
+  - Log rejection reason
+  - Trigger rollback with "onReject" edge
+  
+  <b>Exit:</b>
+  - Workflow aborts or rolls back
+  - Resources released
+  
+  <b>Compensation Actions:</b>
+  - Undo partial changes
+  - Restore previous state
+  - Cancel pending tasks
+  - Notify failure
+  
+  <b>Audit:</b>
+  - Who rejected
+  - When rejected
+  - Reason (required)
+}
+
+PENDING --> APPROVED : <b>boardMemberApproves()</b>\n[guard: user.role == 'board_member']\naction: resumeWorkflow('onApprove')
+note on link
+  <b>Requirement 17.7:</b>
+  Only valid transition
+  from PENDING
+end note
+
+PENDING --> REJECTED : <b>boardMemberRejects()</b>\n[guard: user.role == 'board_member']\naction: rollbackWorkflow('onReject')
+note on link
+  <b>Requirement 17.7:</b>
+  Only valid transition
+  from PENDING
+end note
+
+PENDING --> REJECTED : <b>autoRejectOnTimeout()</b>\n[guard: elapsed > 2 hours]\naction: timeoutRollback()
+note on link
+  <b>Automatic Timeout:</b>
+  Prevents workflow hang
+  if no response received
+end note
+
+APPROVED --> [*] : <b>workflowResumes()</b>\naction: notifySuccess()
+
+REJECTED --> [*] : <b>workflowAborts()</b>\naction: notifyFailure()
+
+note right of PENDING
+  <b>Notification Channels:</b>
+  1. WebSocket (real-time)
+  2. Email (after 15 min)
+  3. SMS (after 1 hour)
+  
+  <b>Request Data Contains:</b>
+  - approvalType: deployment | budget | design
+  - context: relevant task/workflow info
+  - requester: which agent requested
+  - urgency: low | medium | high
+  
+  <b>SOLID - Interface Segregation:</b>
+  - Different approval types
+  - Specific approval interfaces
+  
+  <b>Pattern - Chain of Responsibility:</b>
+  - Escalation to senior board member
+  - If timeout approaching
+end note
+
+note right of APPROVED
+  <b>Post-Approval Actions:</b>
+  
+  <u>Deployment Approval:</u>
+  - Trigger CI/CD pipeline
+  - Deploy to production
+  - Update deployment history
+  
+  <u>Budget Approval:</u>
+  - Release funds
+  - Update cost tracking
+  - Enable resource allocation
+  
+  <u>Design Approval:</u>
+  - Proceed with implementation
+  - Update project timeline
+  - Notify development team
+  
+  <b>Metrics Tracked:</b>
+  - Approval latency (requestedAt ΓåÆ respondedAt)
+  - Approval rate by type
+  - Average response time per board member
+end note
+
+note left of REJECTED
+  <b>Rejection Handling:</b>
+  
+  <u>Mandatory Comment:</u>
+  - Must provide reason
+  - Helps agents learn
+  - Improves future requests
+  
+  <u>Rollback Strategies:</u>
+  1. Full rollback: undo all changes
+  2. Partial rollback: keep safe changes
+  3. Forward fix: apply corrections
+  
+  <u>Agent Learning:</u>
+  - Rejection stored in memory
+  - Similar requests adjusted
+  - Pattern recognition for future
+  
+  <b>Pattern - Memento:</b>
+  - Saves state before risky operations
+  - Enables safe rollback
+  
+  <b>Pattern - Command:</b>
+  - Approval as command object
+  - Supports undo/redo
+end note
+
+note bottom
+  <b>State Machine Invariants:</b>
+  
+  1. <b>Single Transition Rule (Req 17.7):</b>
+     - PENDING can only go to APPROVED or REJECTED
+     - No other transitions allowed
+     - Enforced in ApprovalService.handleApprovalResponse()
+  
+  2. <b>Timestamp Ordering:</b>
+     - requestedAt < respondedAt (always)
+     - Validated in application layer
+  
+  3. <b>Idempotency:</b>
+     - Duplicate approval requests prevented
+     - One pending approval per workflow step
+  
+  4. <b>Authorization:</b>
+     - Only board_member role can approve/reject
+     - Checked before state transition
+  
+  5. <b>Atomicity:</b>
+     - Approval response + workflow resume = atomic transaction
+     - Prevents inconsistent state
+  
+  <b>Design Patterns Applied:</b>
+  - <b>State Pattern:</b> Encapsulates approval states
+  - <b>Observer Pattern:</b> Notifies workflow on decision
+  - <b>Strategy Pattern:</b> Different approval strategies per type
+  - <b>Command Pattern:</b> Approval actions as commands
+end note
+
+@enduml

+' ====== EXTEND RELATIONSHIPS ======
+UC008_2 .> UC008 : <<extend>>\nRevoke is optional
+UC026_2 .> UC026 : <<extend>>\nSecurity events filter
+UC027_2 .> UC027 : <<extend>>\nBackup is optional
+UC027_3 .> UC027 : <<extend>>\nWorkflow restart is recovery action
+
+' ====== NOTES ======
+note right of UC003
+  **Agent Model Configuration:**
+  
+  Admin can configure LLM model for each
+  agent role (CEO, PM, DEV, QA, MARKETING,
+  BA, ARCHITECT, DEVOPS)
+  
+  **Configuration Fields:**
+  - Provider: opencode, anthropic, openai
+  - Model: e.g., "claude-3-5-sonnet-20241022"
+  - Fallback Provider: optional
+  
+  **Effect:**
+  Changes take effect immediately on next
+  LLM call (no agent restart required)
+  
+  **Stored in:**
+  Agent.modelRouteConfig (JSON field)
+end note
+
+note bottom of UC008
+  **RBAC Implementation:**
+  
+  **Roles:**
+  - admin: Full system access
+  - board_member: Project management,
+    workflow approval, cost monitoring
+  
+  **Use Cases:**
+  1. Admin assigns "admin" role to user
+     ΓåÆ User gains access to UC-003, UC-025, UC-008
+  2. Admin assigns "board_member" role
+     ΓåÆ User gains access to UC-002, UC-005, UC-008
+  
+  **Enforcement:**
+  @Roles() decorator on controllers
+  RolesGuard checks JWT token role claim
+end note
+
+note right of UC025
+  **Health Check Endpoints:**
+  
+  GET /health/db
+  ΓåÆ Returns { status: "connected" | "disconnected",
+              latency: 12ms }
+  
+  GET /health/redis
+  ΓåÆ Returns { status: "connected" | "disconnected",
+              queueLength: 5, workers: 3 }
+  
+  GET /health/llm
+  ΓåÆ Returns { status: "online" | "offline" | "degraded",
+              circuitBreakerState: "closed" | "open",
+              consecutiveFailures: 0 }
+  
+  GET /health/sandbox
+  ΓåÆ Returns { status: "available" | "unavailable",
+              dockerVersion: "20.10.8" }
+  
+  **Alert Thresholds:**
+  - Database latency > 500ms: WARNING
+  - Redis queue length > 100: WARNING
+  - LLM circuit breaker open: CRITICAL
+  - Docker unavailable: CRITICAL
+  
+  **Alert Channels:**
+  Email, Slack webhook (configurable)
+end note
+
+note bottom of UC007
+  **Performance Metrics Dashboard:**
+  
+  **Metrics per Agent Role:**
+  - Total LLM calls
+  - Total tokens used (prompt + completion)
+  - Average response time (ms)
+  - Success rate (%)
+  - Fallback trigger rate (%)
+  - Cost per agent (USD)
+  
+  **Aggregation Period:**
+  Last 24 hours, 7 days, 30 days
+  
+  **Visualization:**
+  - Line chart: Response time trend
+  - Bar chart: Token usage by agent
+  - Pie chart: Cost distribution
+  - Table: Top 10 slowest LLM calls
+  
+  **Data Source:**
+  ApiUsageLog table with Prisma aggregations
+end note
+
+note top of UC026
+  **Audit Log Categories:**
+  
+  1. **API Usage Logs** (UC-026-1)
+     - Every LLM call with token counts
+     - Model used, provider, response time
+     - Cost calculation
+     - Stored in ApiUsageLog table
+  
+  2. **Security Events** (UC-026-2)
+     - Failed login attempts
+     - Unauthorized API access (403)
+     - Prompt injection detected
+     - Sandbox violations
+     - Stored in SecurityEvent table (planned)
+  
+  3. **User Actions**
+     - Project created/deleted
+     - Approval approved/rejected
+     - Agent config changed
+     - Stored via audit interceptor
+  
+  **Retention Policy:**
+  - API usage: 90 days
+  - Security events: 365 days
+  - User actions: 365 days
+end note
+
+note right of UC027
+  **System Maintenance Tasks:**
+  
+  1. **Prune Old Logs** (UC-027-1)
+     - Delete ApiUsageLog records > 90 days
+     - Delete AgentMessage records > 30 days
+     - Runs daily at 2 AM via cron job
+  
+  2. **Backup Database** (UC-027-2)
+     - pg_dump full backup
+     - Compress and upload to S3
+     - Runs weekly on Sunday 3 AM
+     - Retention: 4 weekly + 12 monthly backups
+  
+  3. **Restart Failed Workflows** (UC-027-3)
+     - Query WorkflowRun.status = 'failed'
+     - Analyze failure reason
+     - If transient error (DB timeout, LLM outage):
+       Reset to last checkpoint, retry
+     - If permanent error: Mark as cancelled
+     - Manual trigger by admin
+  
+  4. **Vacuum Database**
+     - VACUUM ANALYZE on large tables
+     - Reclaim disk space, update statistics
+     - Runs monthly
+end note
+
+note bottom of UC025_3
+  **Circuit Breaker State Machine:**
+  
+  **States:**
+  - CLOSED: Normal operation, requests pass through
+  - OPEN: Provider failed 3x, all requests use fallback
+  - HALF_OPEN: Testing if provider recovered
+  
+  **Transitions:**
+  - CLOSED ΓåÆ OPEN: After 3 consecutive failures within 5 min
+  - OPEN ΓåÆ HALF_OPEN: After 60 seconds
+  - HALF_OPEN ΓåÆ CLOSED: If test request succeeds
+  - HALF_OPEN ΓåÆ OPEN: If test request fails
+  
+  **Stored in:**
+  LLMGatewayStatus table
+  
+  **Admin Action:**
+  Admin can manually reset circuit breaker
+  (e.g., after provider maintenance window)
+end note
+
+legend bottom
+  |= Color |= Package Category |
+  | #FFF0F5 to #FFA8BC | Admin capabilities (gradient) |
+  | #D3D3D3 | System background jobs |
+  
+  |= Relationship |= Meaning |
+  | <<include>> | Required sub-use case |
+  | <<extend>> | Optional extension |
+  | <<scheduled>> | Automated cron job |
+  
+  **Admin Privileges:**
+  Admin role has access to ALL use cases in the system
+  (board_member use cases + admin-specific use cases)
+  
+  **Security Note:**
+  Admin role is assigned during user registration
+  for first user (auto-admin) or manually by existing admin
+endlegend
+
+@enduml

+@startuml Agent Use Cases - Multi-Agent Collaboration
+!theme plain
+title AI Agent Use Cases - Detailed Multi-Agent Workflow (UML 2.5)
+
+' ====== AI AGENT ACTORS ======
+actor "CEO Agent" as CEO #90EE90
+actor "BA Agent" as BA #FFA500
+actor "Architect Agent" as Architect #FFA500
+actor "PM Agent" as PM #90EE90
+actor "DEV Agent" as DEV #90EE90
+actor "QA Agent" as QA #90EE90
+actor "Marketing Agent" as Marketing #90EE90
+actor "DevOps Agent" as DevOps #FFA500
+
+' ====== EXTERNAL ACTORS ======
+actor "LLM Gateway" as LLM #FFD700
+actor "Docker Sandbox" as Docker #D3D3D3
+
+rectangle "Multi-Agent Collaboration Platform" {
+  
+  package "Strategic Planning Phase" #E8F5E9 {
+    usecase "UC-015\nCoordinate Multi-Agent\nWorkflow" as UC015
+    usecase "UC-016-CEO\nPlan Milestones" as UC016CEO
+  }
+  
+  package "Requirements & Architecture Phase" #FFF3E0 {
+    usecase "UC-009\nAnalyze Requirements" as UC009
+    usecase "UC-010\nDesign System\nArchitecture" as UC010
+    usecase "UC-024\nEstimate Task\nComplexity" as UC024
+  }
+  
+  package "Development Planning Phase" #E3F2FD {
+    usecase "UC-011\nPlan Development\nSprint" as UC011
+  }
+  
+  package "Implementation Phase" #F3E5F5 {
+    usecase "UC-012\nExecute Development\nTask" as UC012
+    usecase "UC-028\nExecute Tool in\nSandbox" as UC028
+  }
+  
+  package "Quality Assurance Phase" #FCE4EC {
+    usecase "UC-013\nExecute Code Review" as UC013
+    usecase "UC-023\nPerform Code Quality\nAnalysis" as UC023
+  }
+  
+  package "Deployment Phase" #FFF9C4 {
+    usecase "UC-016\nDeploy Application" as UC016
+  }
+  
+  package "Marketing Phase" #E1F5FE {
+    usecase "UC-014\nCreate Marketing\nStrategy" as UC014
+  }
+  
+  package "Agent Infrastructure" #F5F5F5 {
+    usecase "UC-017\nManage Agent\nMemory" as UC017
+    usecase "UC-018\nRoute LLM Request" as UC018
+    usecase "UC-004\nAgent Think-Act\nCycle" as UC004
+  }
+}

+' ====== AGENT ASSOCIATIONS ======
+CEO --> UC015
+CEO --> UC016CEO
+
+BA --> UC009
+BA --> UC024
+
+Architect --> UC010
+
+PM --> UC011
+PM --> UC024
+

+UC009 ..> LLM : <<uses>>\nLLM reasoning
+UC010 ..> LLM : <<uses>>\nArchitecture generation
+UC011 ..> LLM : <<uses>>\nTask breakdown
+UC012 ..> LLM : <<uses>>\nCode generation
+UC012 ..> Docker : <<uses>>\nSandbox execution
+UC013 ..> LLM : <<uses>>\nCode review
+UC014 ..> LLM : <<uses>>\nMarketing copy

+UC016 ..> Docker : <<uses>>\nDeployment execution

+' ====== INCLUDE RELATIONSHIPS ======
+UC009 .> UC004 : <<include>>\nThink-Act cycle
+UC010 .> UC004 : <<include>>
+UC011 .> UC004 : <<include>>
+UC012 .> UC004 : <<include>>
+UC012 .> UC028 : <<include>>\nSandbox required
+UC013 .> UC004 : <<include>>
+UC014 .> UC004 : <<include>>
+UC015 .> UC004 : <<include>>
+UC016 .> UC004 : <<include>>
+UC016 .> UC028 : <<include>>\nSandbox required
+UC016CEO .> UC004 : <<include>>
+
+' All agents use memory and LLM routing
+UC004 .> UC017 : <<include>>\nLoad/save memory
+UC004 .> UC018 : <<include>>\nLLM calls
+
+' ====== EXTEND RELATIONSHIPS ======
+UC023 .> UC013 : <<extend>>\nDeep quality analysis
+
+' ====== DELEGATION CHAIN (DEPENDENCIES) ======
+UC016CEO ..> UC009 : <<delegates>>\n1. CEO ΓåÆ BA
+UC009 ..> UC010 : <<delegates>>\n2. BA ΓåÆ Architect
+UC010 ..> UC011 : <<delegates>>\n3. Architect ΓåÆ PM
+UC011 ..> UC012 : <<delegates>>\n4. PM ΓåÆ DEV
+UC012 ..> UC013 : <<delegates>>\n5. DEV ΓåÆ QA
+UC013 ..> UC016 : <<delegates>>\n6. QA ΓåÆ DevOps
+UC016 ..> UC014 : <<delegates>>\n7. DevOps ΓåÆ Marketing

+' ====== NOTES ======
+note right of UC015
+  **CEO Orchestration:**
+  - Analyzes project goal
+  - Determines workflow sequence
+  - Monitors overall progress
+  - Handles failures/escalations
+  - Provides strategic oversight
+  
+  **Coordination Pattern:**
+  CEO ΓåÆ BA ΓåÆ Architect ΓåÆ PM ΓåÆ DEV ΓåÆ QA ΓåÆ DevOps ΓåÆ Marketing
+end note
+
+note right of UC009
+  **BA Agent Output:**
+  - Functional Requirements (FR-001, FR-002, ...)
+  - Non-Functional Requirements (NFR-001, ...)
+  - Use Case Outlines
+  - Acceptance Criteria
+  - Requirements Document (Markdown)
+  
+  **Think Phase:**
+  Loads semantic memories from
+  "requirements_patterns" namespace
+  
+  **Act Phase:**
+  Calls LLM with structured output parsing
+  Generates IEEE 830 format document
+end note
+
+note bottom of UC010
+  **Architect Agent Output:**
+  - System Architecture Design
+  - Component Diagram (PlantUML)
+  - Technology Stack Recommendation
+  - Architecture Decision Record (ADR)
+  
+  **Think Phase:**
+  Loads memories from "architecture_patterns"
+  and "technology_decisions"
+  
+  **Act Phase:**
+  Validates PlantUML syntax
+  Generates ADR with rationale
+end note
+
+note right of UC011
+  **PM Agent Responsibilities:**
+  - Break down milestones into tasks
+  - Prioritize backlog (MoSCoW method)
+  - Estimate task effort (hours or story points)
+  - Assign tasks to sprints
+  
+  **Task Estimation:**
+  Uses ML model (UC-024) when ΓëÑ50
+  historical tasks exist, else heuristic
+end note
+
+note bottom of UC012
+  **DEV Agent Workflow:**
+  1. Load task description + architecture
+  2. Load coding conventions from memory
+  3. Call LLM for code + unit tests
+  4. Write files to temp directory
+  5. Execute tests in Docker sandbox
+  6. Parse test results
+  7. If tests pass: Task ΓåÆ "review"
+  8. If tests fail: Retry (up to 2 times)
+  
+  **Sandbox Execution:**
+  Docker container: node:18-alpine
+  Resource limits: 256MB RAM, 50% CPU, 30s timeout
+end note
+
+note top of UC013
+  **QA Agent Review Process:**
+  1. Load code files from DEV Agent
+  2. Load quality standards from memory
+  3. Call LLM for code review
+  4. Run static analysis (ESLint, SonarQube)
+  5. Merge findings
+  6. Calculate quality score (0-100)
+  7. If score ΓëÑ80 and no high-severity issues:
+     Approve (Task ΓåÆ "done")
+  8. Else: Reject (Task ΓåÆ "todo", feedback)
+  
+  **OWASP Check:**
+  Reviews against OWASP Top 10 vulnerabilities
+end note
+
+note right of UC016
+  **DevOps Agent Process:**
+  1. Receive approved code from QA
+  2. Generate deployment artifacts:
+     - Dockerfile
+     - docker-compose.yml
+     - CI/CD pipeline config
+  3. Execute deployment in sandbox (Docker-in-Docker)
+  4. Perform health checks (HTTP /health endpoint)
+  5. If success: Report deployment URL
+  6. If failure: Rollback and notify
+  
+  **Zero-Downtime:**
+  Supports blue-green or rolling updates
+end note
+
+note left of UC014
+  **Marketing Agent Output:**
+  - Product Announcement (Markdown)
+  - Release Notes
+  - Social Media Posts (Twitter, LinkedIn)
+  
+  **Brand Voice:**
+  Loads examples from "brand_voice" memory
+  to maintain consistent tone
+end note
+
+note bottom of UC004
+  **Template Method Pattern:**
+  BaseAgent.execute() defines skeleton:
+  
+  async execute(context) {
+    thought = await this.think(context)
+    action = await this.act(context)
+    return { thought, action }
+  }
+  
+  All agents implement:
+  - abstract think(context)
+  - abstract act(context)
+end note
+
+note top of UC017
+  **Memory Management:**
+  - Generate vector embeddings (LLM embed API)
+  - Store in AgentMemory with pgvector
+  - Semantic search via cosine similarity
+  - Prune old low-importance memories (importance < 5)
+  
+  **Namespaces:**
+  - requirements_patterns
+  - architecture_patterns
+  - coding_conventions
+  - brand_voice
+  - lessons_learned
+end note
+
+note bottom of UC018
+  **LLM Routing:**
+  1. Read agent model config from DB
+  2. Create provider (9Router/Anthropic/OpenAI)
+  3. Send request with circuit breaker
+  4. If primary fails 3x: Use fallback
+  5. Log usage to ApiUsageLog
+  6. Return ChatResult with token counts
+  
+  **Circuit Breaker:**
+  Open after 3 failures ΓåÆ Half-open after 60s
+end note
+
+note right of UC028
+  **Sandbox Security:**
+  - Isolated Docker container per execution
+  - No network access (unless whitelisted)
+  - Read-only file system (except /tmp)
+  - Resource limits enforced
+  - Execution timeout: 30 seconds
+  - Output sanitized before returning to agent
+  
+  **Tool Authorization:**
+  ToolRegistry validates agent role
+  before allowing tool execution
+end note
+
+legend bottom
+  |= Color |= Agent Type |
+  | #90EE90 (Green) | Existing Agent (CEO, PM, DEV, QA, Marketing) |
+  | #FFA500 (Orange) | New Agent (BA, Architect, DevOps) |
+  | #FFD700 (Gold) | External LLM Service |
+  | #D3D3D3 (Gray) | External Docker Sandbox |
+  
+  |= Relationship |= Meaning |
+  | <<include>> | Required sub-use case |
+  | <<extend>> | Optional extension |
+  | <<uses>> | External dependency |
+  | <<delegates>> | Agent-to-agent task handoff |
+  
+  **Workflow Sequence:**
+  CEO ΓåÆ BA ΓåÆ Architect ΓåÆ PM ΓåÆ DEV ΓåÆ QA ΓåÆ DevOps ΓåÆ Marketing
+endlegend
+
+@enduml

+@startuml Board Member Use Case Diagram
+!theme plain
+title Board Member Use Cases - Detailed View (UML 2.5)
+
+actor "Board Member" as BoardMember #lightblue
+actor "Workflow Engine\n(System)" as System #lightgray
+
+rectangle "Board Member Capabilities" {
+  
+  package "Authentication & Access" #E6F2FF {
+    usecase "UC-001\nUser Authentication" as UC001
+  }
+  
+  package "Project Management" #D4E9F7 {
+    usecase "UC-002\nCreate Project" as UC002
+    usecase "UC-007\nMonitor Workflow\nProgress" as UC007
+    usecase "UC-022\nGenerate Project\nReport" as UC022
+  }
+  
+  package "Task Management" #C2DFF2 {
+    usecase "UC-020\nManage Task Kanban" as UC020
+    usecase "UC-024\nEstimate Task\nComplexity" as UC024
+  }
+  
+  package "Cost & Budget Control" #B0D5ED {
+    usecase "UC-008\nView Cost Dashboard" as UC008
+    usecase "UC-011\nSet Budget Alert\nThreshold" as UC011
+  }
+  
+  package "Workflow Control" #9ECBE8 {
+    usecase "UC-004\nPause/Resume\nWorkflow" as UC004
+    usecase "UC-005\nApprove Workflow\nStep" as UC005
+    usecase "UC-006\nReject Workflow\nStep" as UC006
+    usecase "UC-019\nRequest Workflow\nRollback" as UC019
+  }
+  
+  package "Collaboration & Monitoring" #8CC1E3 {
+    usecase "UC-021\nView Agent Meeting\nRoom (Virtual Office)" as UC021
+  }
+  
+  package "Supporting Use Cases" #CCCCCC {
+    usecase "UC-004-SYS\nExecute Project\nWorkflow" as UC004SYS
+    usecase "UC-018\nRoute LLM Request" as UC018
+    usecase "UC-017\nManage Agent Memory" as UC017
+  }
+}
+
+' ====== PRIMARY ASSOCIATIONS ======
+BoardMember --> UC001
+BoardMember --> UC002
+BoardMember --> UC007
+BoardMember --> UC008
+BoardMember --> UC020
+BoardMember --> UC021
+BoardMember --> UC022
+
+BoardMember --> UC004
+BoardMember --> UC005
+BoardMember --> UC006
+BoardMember --> UC019
+BoardMember --> UC011
+
+' ====== SYSTEM ACTOR ASSOCIATIONS ======
+System --> UC004SYS
+System --> UC017
+System --> UC018
+
+' ====== INCLUDE RELATIONSHIPS ======
+UC002 .> UC004SYS : <<include>>\nAuto-starts workflow
+UC002 .> UC001 : <<include>>\nRequires authentication
+UC007 .> UC001 : <<include>>\nRequires authentication
+UC008 .> UC001 : <<include>>\nRequires authentication
+UC020 .> UC001 : <<include>>\nRequires authentication
+
+' UC-005 includes workflow resume
+UC005 .> UC004SYS : <<include>>\nResumes workflow after approval
+
+' ====== EXTEND RELATIONSHIPS ======
+UC006 .> UC005 : <<extend>>\nRejection is alternative to approval
+
+' Alternative flow: rollback extends workflow pause
+UC019 .> UC004 : <<extend>>\nRollback available when paused
+
+' Budget alert extends cost monitoring
+UC011 .> UC008 : <<extend>>\nAlert threshold is optional feature
+
+' ====== DEPENDENCY RELATIONSHIPS ======
+UC004SYS ..> UC018 : <<uses>>\nAgents call LLM
+UC004SYS ..> UC017 : <<uses>>\nAgents load/save memory
+
+' ====== NOTES ======
+note right of UC002
+  **Main Flow:**
+  1. Enter project details
+     (name, goal, description, budget)
+  2. System validates input
+  3. Project created with status='active'
+  4. CEO Agent auto-starts milestone planning
+  
+  **Pre-condition:**
+  User authenticated with board_member role
+  
+  **Post-condition:**
+  WorkflowRun created, CEO Agent executing
+end note
+
+note right of UC005
+  **Main Flow:**
+  1. System notifies via WebSocket
+  2. Board Member reviews approval data
+  3. Board Member approves (or rejects)
+  4. Workflow resumes via "onApprove" edge
+  
+  **Pre-condition:**
+  Workflow paused at human_approval node
+  Approval.status = 'pending'
+  
+  **Post-condition:**
+  Approval.status = 'approved'
+  Workflow continues to next node
+  
+  **State Machine Rule (Req 17.7):**
+  pending ΓåÆ approved (only valid transition)
+end note
+
+note bottom of UC008
+  **Displayed Metrics:**
+  - Total cost vs. budget (%)
+  - Cost by agent role (pie chart)
+  - Cost by LLM model (bar chart)
+  - Cost trend over time (line chart)
+  - Top 10 most expensive API calls
+  
+  **Data Source:**
+  ApiUsageLog table aggregations
+end note
+
+note top of UC020
+  **Kanban Columns:**
+  TODO ΓåÆ IN_PROGRESS ΓåÆ REVIEW ΓåÆ DONE
+  
+  **Valid Transitions (Req 17):**
+  - todo ΓåÆ in_progress
+  - in_progress ΓåÆ review
+  - review ΓåÆ done (QA approved)
+  - review ΓåÆ todo (QA rejected)
+  
+  **Invalid Transitions:**
+  - done ΓåÆ todo (rejected by system)
+  - todo ΓåÆ review (rejected by system)
+end note
+
+note left of UC021
+  **Virtual Office Features:**
+  - Real-time agent messages
+  - Agent avatars with status
+  - Thinking bubbles during LLM calls
+  - Action notifications (tool execution)
+  - Chat history (last 100 messages)
+  
+  **Purpose:**
+  Transparency into agent collaboration
+  and decision-making process
+end note
+
+note bottom of UC004
+  **Workflow Control Actions:**
+  - Pause: Save state, stop at node boundary
+  - Resume: Restore state, continue execution
+  
+  **Use Cases:**
+  - Pause during budget review
+  - Pause before critical deployment
+  - Resume after stakeholder approval
+end note
+
+note top of UC019
+  **Rollback Process:**
+  1. Board Member selects target checkpoint
+  2. System validates checkpoint is valid
+  3. System creates WorkflowSnapshot (memento)
+  4. System restores context to checkpoint state
+  5. System marks subsequent steps as 'skipped'
+  6. System invokes rollback handlers
+  7. Workflow resumes from checkpoint
+  
+  **Memento Pattern:**
+  WorkflowSnapshot captures/restores state
+end note
+
+legend bottom
+  |= Color |= Package Category |
+  | #E6F2FF to #8CC1E3 | Board Member capabilities (gradient) |
+  | #CCCCCC | System-level supporting use cases |
+  
+  |= Relationship |= Meaning |
+  | <<include>> | Required sub-use case (always executed) |
+  | <<extend>> | Optional extension (conditional) |
+  | <<uses>> | External dependency |
+  | --- (dashed) | Association between actor and use case |
+endlegend
+
+@enduml

+# Requirements Traceability Matrix ΓÇö AI-CORP Multi-Agent Platform
+
+| **Field** | **Value** |
+|-----------|-----------|
+| **Document Version** | 1.0.0 |
+| **Date** | December 10, 2024 |
+| **Authors** | AI-CORP Development Team |
+| **Status** | Draft - Under Review |
+| **Project** | Design and Development of a Multi-Agent Platform for Object-Oriented Enterprise Workflow Automation |
+
+---
+
+## 1. Overview
+
+This Requirements Traceability Matrix (RTM) establishes bidirectional traceability between:
+- **Functional Requirements** ΓåÆ **Use Cases** ΓåÆ **Design Artifacts** ΓåÆ **Implementation Files** ΓåÆ **Test Cases**
+- **Non-Functional Requirements** ΓåÆ **Architecture Decisions** ΓåÆ **Design Patterns** ΓåÆ **Implementation** ΓåÆ **Test Cases**
+
+The RTM ensures every requirement is implemented, tested, and verifiable, satisfying academic standards for thesis documentation and enabling impact analysis for future changes.
+
+---
+
+## 2. Subsystem Organization
+
+Requirements are organized into 7 subsystems:
+
+1. **Authentication & Authorization (AUTH)** - User login, JWT tokens, RBAC
+2. **Project Management (PROJ)** - Projects, milestones, tasks, Kanban
+3. **Agent Framework (AGENT)** - Base agent architecture, 8 specialized agents, memory system
+4. **Workflow Orchestration (WF)** - DAG execution engine, state management, recovery
+5. **Approval System (APPR)** - Human-in-the-loop, approval state machine
+6. **Cost Tracking (COST)** - API usage logging, budget monitoring, cost analytics
+7. **Memory & Learning (MEM)** - Vector embeddings, semantic search, memory management
+
+---
+
+## 3. Functional Requirements Traceability
+
+### 3.1 Authentication & Authorization Subsystem
+
+| Req ID | Requirement Name | Description | Use Case IDs | Design Artifacts | Implementation Files | Test IDs | Status |
+|--------|------------------|-------------|--------------|------------------|---------------------|----------|--------|
+| FR-AUTH-001 | User Authentication | System shall authenticate users via email/password and issue JWT tokens | UC-001 | Class: AuthService, JwtStrategy | apps/api/src/modules/auth/auth.service.ts, apps/api/src/modules/auth/jwt.strategy.ts | TEST-AUTH-001, TEST-AUTH-002 | Implemented |
+| FR-AUTH-002 | Password Security | System shall hash passwords using bcrypt with salt rounds ΓëÑ12 | UC-001 | Class: AuthService (register method) | apps/api/src/modules/auth/auth.service.ts | TEST-AUTH-003 | Implemented |
+| FR-AUTH-003 | Role-Based Access Control | System shall enforce RBAC with roles: admin, board_member | UC-003, UC-008 | Guard: RolesGuard, Decorator: @Roles() | apps/api/src/modules/auth/roles.guard.ts | TEST-AUTH-004, TEST-AUTH-005 | Implemented |
+| FR-AUTH-004 | JWT Token Expiry | System shall expire JWT tokens after 24 hours | UC-001 | JwtModule configuration | apps/api/src/modules/auth/auth.module.ts | TEST-AUTH-006 | Implemented |
+

+### 3.4 Workflow Orchestration Subsystem
+
+| Req ID | Requirement Name | Description | Use Case IDs | Design Artifacts | Implementation Files | Test IDs | Status |
+|--------|------------------|-------------|--------------|------------------|---------------------|----------|--------|
+| FR-WF-001 | DAG Workflow Execution | System shall execute workflows as DAGs with nodes (agent_task, human_approval, condition, parallel) | UC-004 | Class: WorkflowEngine | apps/api/src/modules/workflow/workflow.engine.ts | TEST-WF-001, TEST-WF-002 | Implemented |

+| FR-WF-008 | Workflow Rollback | System shall rollback workflow to previous checkpoint with rollback handlers | UC-019 | Class: WorkflowEngine (rollback method), Memento pattern | apps/api/src/modules/workflow/workflow.engine.ts | TEST-WF-009 | Planned |
+| FR-WF-009 | Workflow Pause/Resume | Board Member shall pause and resume workflows manually | UC-004 | Class: WorkflowEngine (pauseWorkflow, resumeWorkflow methods) | apps/api/src/modules/workflow/workflow.engine.ts | TEST-WF-010 | Implemented |
+| FR-WF-010 | Workflow Status Tracking | System shall track workflow status: running, paused, completed, failed, cancelled | UC-004, UC-007 | Enum: WorkflowStatus in schema.prisma | apps/api/prisma/schema.prisma | TEST-WF-011 | Implemented |
